- 2026-09-13: "Its controller adds" is the controller of the permanent the event is about (`GameEvent.subject_controller`), not the enchanted permanent read off `attached_to`: Vernal Bloom has no attachment and Utopia Sprawl's subject is the enchanted land anyway. `_types_match` never read a land subtype, so "you tap a Swamp for mana" fired for any land; `_required_tapped_subtype` reads the capitalised subtype the way `_ENTERING_SUBTYPE` does.
- 2026-09-13: Attack requirements (B7-04) are read off computed ability lines by whole-line match only: Dragon's Rage Channeler's printed delirium line always contains "attacks each combat if able", and only the subjectless line the layer engine grants while delirium holds is the requirement. The unscripted default declaration is passed into `_ask_declaration` as `default` (stored as `Decision.default_cards`) rather than computed in `default_answer`, which is stateless and cannot name a defender.
- 2026-09-13: Scry's bottom order (B7-12) reuses `Game.ask_library_bottom_order`, asked after the split and before `_end_looks` so a paused seat still sees the look. CR 701.22a says "on the bottom of your library in any order", so the question is real, not a documentation note. Schema 15 -> 16 with no new field (battles 6 and 4 landed first as 14 and 15, so this took the next number per the 7 -> 8 merge note): a triggered mana ability resolving off the stack, the extra scry question on a replayed tape and the newly read look templates all change how existing state resolves (the bump rule's second bullet). Crypt Ghast was swapped for Nirkana Revenant as the subtype proof because Crypt Ghast's Extort added an ignored keyword to `sweep_pool.py --keywords`.
- 2026-09-13: Scry's bottom order (B7-12) reuses `Game.ask_library_bottom_order`, asked after the split and before `_end_looks` so a paused seat still sees the look. CR 701.22a says "on the bottom of your library in any order", so the question is real, not a documentation note. Schema 14 -> 15 with no new field (battle 6 landed first as 13 -> 14, so this took the next number per the 7 -> 8 merge note): a triggered mana ability resolving off the stack, the extra scry question on a replayed tape and the newly read look templates all change how existing state resolves (the bump rule's second bullet). Crypt Ghast was swapped for Nirkana Revenant as the subtype proof because Crypt Ghast's Extort added an ignored keyword to `sweep_pool.py --keywords`.
- 2026-09-13: Triggered mana with a count or a may (battle 7 round 2). A "for each" count reads only "<Subtype or card type> on the battlefield / you control" (`parse_mana_count`); Sasaya's Essence's "other land you control with the same name as it" stays unread on purpose, because its only printing is a flip card's flipped face and flipping (CR 710) is unmodelled, so it could never be tested. "May add" has one triggered-mana printing, Snowfall. The payment search treats a may as optional, and `CostPayment.auto_tap` answers it from its own plan (`CostPayment._planning` -> `Game.mana_hints_planned`) instead of asking mid-payment, where a paused seat would raise `DecisionPending` inside a cast. A hand-pinned tap still asks. Restricted triggered mana is left out of the search because `mana_candidate_scan` is hoisted independent of the spend. Literal colours now consume their own colour hints too; before, Leyline's {G} left its hint behind for Fertile Ground's any-colour choice to take.
- 2026-09-13: Snowfall's "Spend this mana only to pay cumulative upkeep costs" is read into its own `SpendPurpose.PAY_CUMULATIVE_UPKEEP` rather than added to `KNOWN_UNREAD_RESTRICTIONS`, whose comment forbids parking a card whose sentence is merely inconvenient. No `ManaSpend` carries that purpose until CR 702.24a is modelled, so the mana still pays nothing. Adding Snowfall moved `sweep_pool.py --keywords` from 16 to 17 ignored, because Cumulative upkeep is unexecuted; it is the only printed "may add" triggered mana ability, so no other card could avoid that.
- 2026-09-13: An ability target's category is read off its phrase (`TargetRequirement.ability_kinds`): "target triggered ability" refuses an activated `AbilityOnStack` and "target activated ability" refuses a trigger. Empty means the phrase named no category and accepts either. Consign to Memory used to accept Karn's +1 and Rimewind Cryomancer a trigger.
- 2026-09-13: Act of Treason's "Gain control ... Untap that creature. It gains haste until end of turn." reached `parsed_layer_effects` whole, which combines only the sentences it reads and skips the rest without a notice, so the untap and haste vanished while the sweep called the card handled. `_gain_control_untap_grant` now reads both word orders for spells and abilities. The silent skip in `parsed_layer_effects` itself is unchanged (noted, not done here).
- 2026-09-13: `Game._legal_target_candidates` walked cards and players only, so once #19 announced activation targets before costs, Rimewind Cryomancer's mandatory "target activated ability" with Karn's +1 on the stack was refused as having no legal target whenever a caller did not name targets up front. It now offers each `AbilityOnStack`; `rejects` still decides which.
- 2026-09-13: An ability outlives its source (CR 113.7a). The gain-control ability parser and `_source_reads_its_exiled_cards` returned silently when `find_by_id(source_id)` missed, which it always does for a token that ceased to exist (CR 704.5d). The SBA now copies each vanished token onto `AbilityOnStack.source_last_known` (persisted, schema 15 -> 16, which also covers the Act of Treason resolution change) and both read `source_or_last_known`; with neither, a `LayerNotice`. `AbilityOnStack.source` itself is unchanged: Tidebinder's "for as long as" wants the live source. `CardState.printed_power`/`printed_toughness` are public now (were `_printed_*`), for the api serializer.
- 2026-09-13: B8-02. `Game.cast_records` is keyed by `CardState.zone_change_stamp`, not card id, with `CastRecord.card_id` kept only so `forget_cast_record` can drop a card's records once its stamp has moved on. Keying by stamp rather than checking a stamp stored in an id-keyed record was chosen so no reader can reach an earlier object's cast by the id at all. Two moves carry the key (`_carry_cast_record`): `_put_on_stack`'s re-stated `move_to(STACK)` and `enter_the_battlefield` for a cast permanent (CR 400.7d). The first run broke Wistfulness's own {G}{G}/{U}{U} triggers: the evoke sacrifice resolves first, and the CR 603.4 recheck then read the graveyard object. `AbilityOnStack.source_stamp` (event and state triggers) makes the recheck read the object the ability came from (CR 113.7a). Schema 15 -> 16.
- 2026-09-13: B8-01. Cascade casts the hit inside its own resolution through `Game.cast_during_resolution` (CR 608.2g), which drives the real `Casting` machine under an `immediate` permission and asks face/modes/targets through `Game.ask`; the permission is removed in a `finally`. `cast_free_from_exile` was not reused: it bypasses `Casting`, so it announces no modes or faces and emits no becomes-target events. Noted, not done: rebound, hideaway, Knowledge Pool and Possibility Storm still use it. `PlayPermission.bottom_if_unused` is kept, always False, because magic-cards-model-api `serialize.py`/`schemas.py` read it.
- 2026-09-13: B8-01's face question is `CHOOSE_A_QUALITY` with `quality="face"` rather than a new `DecisionKind`, so the api needs no new decision kind; options are face names, the adventurer's own creature face named for the "no face" option.
- 2026-09-13: B8-03. A single card put into a library from a face-up public zone is recorded for every viewer after the placement (`_record_cards_seen_going_into_libraries`); two or more at once are not (CR 401.4). Subtlety's `_put_spell_in_library` used to insert by hand and now goes through `put_cards_into_library`, which also raises the put-into-library event it never raised. Staging note: a test moving a card with `Player.move` outside a `Game` method mints a nondeterministic stamp and fails the replay invariant; use a `Game` method.
- 2026-09-13: An edict's "sacrifices a <description> of their choice" is read by the target grammar itself (`targeting.permanent_description`, `_read_phrase(strict=True)`), not by `_parse_mass_type_qualifier`: that reader works on `Characteristics`, which carry no token-ness, so "nontoken creature" and "creature token" could not be expressed there. Strict mode exists because the ordinary target scan silently tolerates an unread trailing tail ("with the greatest power ..."), which is acceptable for a restriction checked elsewhere but would sacrifice the wrong permanent here. A player with no choice to make (no candidate, or no more candidates than required) is not asked (B8-08).
- 2026-09-13: The counterspell predicate (`_SPELL_PREDICATE`) now embeds `targeting.COMPARISON_CLAUSE_PATTERN` instead of its own narrower copy, so Drown in the Loch's graveyard-count bound stopped being a mode notice the moment targeting could already enforce it. Spellstutter Sprite's "X or less, where X is the number of Faeries you control" is a new `TargetRequirement.comparison_x_definition`, evaluated lazily through `effects.count_expression` (imported inside `_defined_x`: effects imports targeting at load time). The Drown recheck test passed before the fix, since the CR 608.2b recheck was already targeting's; only the counter half was missing. No schema bump: `TargetRequirement` is never part of a `Game` dump.
- 2026-09-13: B8 follow-up. `Game.cast_free_from_exile` and `has_legal_free_cast` are gone; every "cast it without paying its mana cost" during resolution goes through `Game.may_cast_during_resolution` (one `MAY`, legal only per `can_cast_during_resolution`) or `cast_during_resolution` directly when the effect asks its own question (cascade, Knowledge Pool, Possibility Storm). Rebound, Paradigm, Fight Rigging, Mosswort Bridge, Isochron Scepter, Transcendent Dragon and suspend moved onto it. Mosswort Bridge and Transcendent Dragon used to grant a permission for later and suspend an `immediate` one; all three now cast during the resolution (CR 608.2g), which changed the existing tests that asserted a permission.
- 2026-09-13: B8 follow-up. `finish_suspend` now returns the cast spell, not a `PlayPermission`. magic-cards-model-api `engine_api.py` (comment near line 1025) and `tests/test_special_actions.py`/`tests/test_play_permissions.py` still describe or drive the old suspend permission and need updating when the api takes this pin. Suspend's haste (CR 702.62a) was only ever applied by tests; the cast is now recorded as "Suspend" and `_create_resolution_delayed_triggers` grants it as the creature enters.
- 2026-09-13: B8 follow-up. The optional additional-cost "yes" is legal only when payable from untapped sources and available objects, so an unscripted caster pays kicker/replicate/offspring when it can (the ward and unless-pays default). Multikicker and replicate stop at `REPEATED_ADDITIONAL_COST_BOUND` (20) so a cost of nothing cannot loop. A scripted "yes" on an illegal `MAY` raises in `Game.ask`, so tests of an impossible cast assert `affirmative_is_legal` instead of scripting.
- 2026-09-13: B8 follow-up. A land "played" by a resolving effect (hideaway) is `_play_land_during_resolution`: CR 305.2a counts it, CR 305.2b and 305.3 refuse it, and CR 305.1's main-phase/empty-stack timing does not apply. Its triggers wait for the resolution to end, unlike `play_land`, which puts them on the stack at once.
- 2026-09-13: B8 follow-up. `_DAMAGE_TARGET_RE`'s quote guard also refused possessive names, so every modal "<Name's> deals N damage" mode was a `ModeNotice`; `_UNQUOTED_PREFIX` admits an apostrophe between letters only.
- 2026-09-13: Phase A merge of battles 7, 8 and PR #21 found Rimewind Cryomancer (added by #21) silent in `sweep_pool.py --effects`: `_parse_targeted_clause` returned `counter_targets` for the counter-an-ability shapes only `for_spell`, on the stale ground that no ability printed them. The gate is gone (CR 113.9); Azorius Guildmage is the off-pool proof. The three branches share one schema bump 15 -> 16 with one consolidated note and one refusal test.
- 2026-09-14: [Board snapshot caching contract](memory/board-snapshot-caching-contract.md) - computed_board is cached only inside Game.board_snapshot() (legal_actions opens one); invalidation is per game (a field store on a model reachable from it, a command on it in any thread, bracketed Counters/ManaPool/open_payment_pools edits), the epoch moves after the value lands, closed scopes never serve, unannounced in-place edits raise BoardSnapshotStale; oracle sentence parsing is lru-memoised
- 2026-09-14: T11 - a mutating command's own read-only scans read one board each. `snapshot.command` (T13) never caches, so `triggers.abilities_triggered_by`, the `state_triggers_for` sweep and `_state_based_actions_once`'s losers check rebuilt the board per permanent (a land drop 97 boards at 22 permanents, a step-moving pass 80, resolving a creature 238). Each now runs inside `snapshot.scan_pin(game)`: not a snapshot scope and no fingerprint (under a `board_snapshot` the legend-rule question and notice appends raised `BoardSnapshotStale`, 23 failures), but dropped by `_before`/`_signal` on any store bearing on that game - commands running or not - and lifted by `command()` for a nested command; it serves only its own thread and never after closing. Board computation no longer draws on `_allocate_id`: `_compute_board` installs a per-computation `uuid5(game_uid:board:n)` allocator, since the effects it builds are discarded and letting them move `objects_created` made every later id depend on how often the board was computed. Strict xfail left: Blood Artist still triggers after Dress Down removed its abilities (look-back, CR 603.10a). Not a caching gap: the xfail is parametrised cached/uncached (`uncached_boards()`) and fails both ways, and the same scenario run on 538ad5f, which has no board caching at all, also triggers Blood Artist and fails the same strict xfail.
- 2026-09-14: T14 - the hideaway scenarios (every test in `test_fight_rigging.py` and `test_mosswort_bridge.py`, the Fight Rigging / Mosswort Bridge / Watcher for Tomorrow cases in `test_cascade_and_hideaway_reveals_are_known.py` and `test_free_casts_go_through_one_funnel.py`, and `test_layer_computed_type_selection.py`'s two Mosswort Bridge cases; 20 tests) leave a different game on different runs on efe067b, before any T11 change: which cards hideaway exiles and bottoms, and the library order it keeps, change between identical runs. Checked by digesting the game (UUIDs renumbered by first appearance) after every outermost `snapshot.command` in separate processes: three runs of those files plus `test_board_snapshot.py` and `test_cascade.py` on efe067b disagree on exactly 19 hideaway tests while the 33 controls match, and the Watcher for Tomorrow library-look case gave 4 distinct sequences in 8 runs. They are outside T11's cached-vs-uncached equivalence claim. On f5982d4 the whole suite run twice cached and once inside `uncached_boards()`: the two cached runs differ only on those hideaway tests, and 4189 tests (66058 commands) are byte-identical cached vs uncached. The other 15 are tests of the caching mechanism itself (`test_board_snapshot.py`, `test_scan_pin.py`, the board-count test in `test_board_snapshot_commands.py`), which fail or trace a different code path with caching forced off.
- 2026-09-14: T17 - a scan pin opened inside an open board snapshot on the same game, with no command running on it, is seeded from the snapshot's board (filling the snapshot if it holds none) instead of computing its own (`snapshot.cached_board`, the snapshot path split out as `_snapshot_board`). `cost.triggered_mana_bonus` opens a pin per mana source through `triggers.abilities_triggered_by`, so `legal_actions` on a board with a triggered mana ability computed the board 11 times; now 1, not growing with mana sources (`test_board_snapshot.py` over Leyline of Abundance, Badgermole Cub, Wild Growth, Utopia Sprawl). Inside a command the snapshot is suspended and pins behave as before; every drop rule still clears a seeded pin. `ComputedBoard.of` raises `layers.NotOnBoard` (a `KeyError` subclass) so the api can tell a board that predates a card from any other missing key. The snapshot docstring now says what the fingerprint check cannot see: an unannounced edit followed by an announced change before the board is dropped.
- 2026-09-14: E1/E2 land-sequencing helpers (`machines/hypothetical.py`: `predict_enters`, `payability`) answer by running `Game.put_onto_battlefield` and `legal_actions` on a decoded copy (`Game.model_validate(game.model_dump(mode="json"))`, decisions reset so nothing pauses), never a second reading of enters-tapped text or a second payment search. `untapped_if_paid` is found by entering twice, optional payments taken when legal and then declined. A self enters-tapped line the engine skips (an unread condition, or an "If you don't" not paired with a life payment) is predicted tapped with a notice (`replacement.unread_self_enters_tapped_lines`), although the real play still enters it untapped. Basking Broodscale is a colorless Eldrazi, so Eldrazi Temple's restriction is proved on Glaring Fleshraker against Ensnaring Bridge; Mystic Sanctuary was swapped for Canopy Vista as the unread-condition proof because its enters-untapped trigger added a silent clause to the sweep. No schema bump: nothing new is persisted.
- 2026-09-14: E1/E2 review fixes. Battle lands' "two or more basic lands" is a `_read_enters_condition` reader and reveal lands pair their "you may reveal ... from your hand" with "If you don't" through `_optional_enters_payment`, so Canopy Vista and Port Town enter correctly in real play and no pool card is left in `unread_self_enters_tapped_lines` (Spectator Seating is the off-pool fail-closed proof). Hypothetical copies resolve choice-free entering triggers and name, never answer, a trigger that asks anything; a trigger is identified across rebuilds by its deterministic id. legal_actions judges an X cast at X=0, which also fixed Toxic Deluge's 'pay X life' being read with X unsubstituted once mandatory additional costs joined cast rows.
- 2026-09-14: `hypothetical.payability_matrix` evaluates each distinct land once on the board its prediction built and runs `legal_actions` once per board; library copies of one printing share an evaluation (keyed by `Card.id`, skipped when a copy is also a card asked about), which is what brings a 7-card hand against 19 lands from about 4.8 s of single calls to about 0.34 s. The declining copy is only entered when the paying run was actually offered an optional payment.
- 2026-09-14: `payability`/`payability_matrix` count "{T}, Pay N life: Add ..." mana abilities the player may activate themselves (CR 605.3a), which auto-tap withholds (CR 118.3c). An unaffordable option is judged again by `legal_actions(..., include_life_paid_mana=True)` (life rows sorted after free ones), then the planned activations are played on a copy and the state-based check (CR 704.5a) decides lethality, rather than comparing life arithmetically, so Phyrexian life and can't-lose effects are the engine's answer. Real auto-tap and default `legal_actions` are unchanged. `Payability.life_paid` is new. Sacrifice and other extra costs fail closed with a notice (Lotus Petal). No schema bump: nothing persisted.
- 2026-09-14: Integration of e1-land-helpers onto the board-snapshot line. e11054a's test put Search for Azcanta in the pool, silent in `sweep_pool.py`: a self transform was read only as a whole ability, and nothing read a graveyard count inside an effect. `_parse_transform_clause` now also runs from `_parse_mixed_clause` (so "you may transform this <type>" reads, proven on Cloistered Youth) and `_parse_if_graveyard_count_clause` reads "if you have / there are N or more cards in your graveyard, ...". No off-pool card proves the condition half: Illuminate History and Cemetery Tampering print it, but their other sentences (discard any number then draw that many; play the hideaway card) are unread, so each would stay silent. Merge clashes: the `legal_actions` snapshot wrapper dropped `include_life_paid_mana`, and hypothetical.py's answer models were plain BaseModels.
- 2026-09-14: Portent of Calamity (live game, f26408a: resolved to the graveyard doing nothing). `_merge_reveal_top_one_card_per_type_clauses` reads the reveal / one pick per card type / rest run for both verbs; Atraxa's whole-text ability-only reader is folded into it and keeps the engine's CHOOSE_CARDS default (the last card offered), which its scenarios pin. The free-cast tail counts cards that actually reached exile, not types revealed. Found on the way: `spell_options_castable_during_resolution` offered an exiled land as a free cast; it now refuses land options (CR 305.9). X needs no reader: `effect_for`'s CR 107.3a substitution turns "top X" into a number. No off-pool printing shares the per-type or count-gate template; Hurkyl, Master Wizard is the nearest variant and needs an unread intervening if plus a cast-history type set, not built.
- 2026-09-14: Dress Down's end step trigger resolved as CHANGED NOTHING: `_SACRIFICE_IT_RE` read only "sacrifice it", and `Game._resolve_ability` skipped a None trigger effect with no notice. Fixed four ways on `dressdown-self-sac`: "sacrifice this <noun>" plus a source_stamp check (CR 400.7, a bounced-and-recast source is not sacrificed); "unless it escaped" off `CardState.cast_with_keyword` (Uro, Kroxa); `ExtraTriggerEvent.UNTAPPED_DURING_UNTAP_STEP` with `amount` and a local "that many" counters reader (`_triggering_event_amount`) the number sweep's G0b is to absorb (Calendar, Marauding Mako); and `Game._note_unread_trigger` fails every unread trigger closed at resolution. `sweep_pool.py --effects` silent 119 of 846 -> 116 of 848 (-Dress Down, Uro, Calendar, Marauding Mako; +Kroxa, the new fixture's own unread discard trigger). The session scratchpad is shared with sibling forks: a sweep baseline written there was overwritten mid-session, so write sweeps to unique names.
- 2026-09-14: Merge of main (portent-calamity, dressdown-self-sac) into int-engine-dsl at e491ed5. Checked whether `Game._note_unread_trigger`'s new notice could leak into `hypothetical.py`'s answers and wrongly fail-close a land's `predict_enters`/`payability` over an unread trigger belonging to some other permanent (Territorial Bruntar's landfall): it cannot. `_settle` builds `EntersPrediction`/`Payability.notices` only from triggers `_resolve_choice_free` left unresolved for a real choice, never from the copy's own `unmodeled_trigger_effect_notices`, and the copy itself is never returned - only ids-and-strings data models are. An unread trigger set off inside a hypothetical copy still resolves silently there, exactly as before this notice existed. Confirmed with `test_hypothetical_unread_trigger_and_untap_step.py`, which also checks `ExtraTriggerEvent.UNTAPPED_DURING_UNTAP_STEP` (raised only from `Game._untap_step_impl`, which `hypothetical.py` never calls) cannot fire on a hypothetical copy, using Amulet of Vigor's own (different) untap trigger as the near-miss case. No code change: both were already correct.
- 2026-09-14: Number sweep G0 - `magic_cards_model/numbers.py` is the one count reader (`COUNT`, `AMOUNT`, `MULTIPLIER`, `ORDINAL`, `QUANTITY`, `count_pattern`, `where_x_pattern`, `parse_count`/`parse_amount`/`parse_multiplier`/`parse_ordinal`/`parse_quantity`, markers `VariableX` and `Quantity`); the fourteen per-module tables are gone. Tables that never held "a"/"an" call `parse_count(..., article=False)`, because their `\w+` captures can land on an article ("Choose a color" must not become a one-mode choice). `COUNT` carries no X so a parser cannot match an X it cannot evaluate. replacement `_amount_of` returns None for a word that is not a count, and `_try_enters_with` reports the sentence UNPARSED: "that many"/"twice that many" (Shimatsu the Bloodcloaked, Devouring Hellion) and "your choice of" counter lines used to be read as CR 107.3m's X with a made-up counter kind. No real Modern printing reaches the six former `\w+` whole-card parsers (Darkness Crystal, Turntimber Symbiosis, Atraxa, Nashi, Dalkovan Encampment, Guide of Souls) with an X (Scryfall dump probe), so their capture change is covered by their existing scenarios. Dead ends as past-ten proof cards: Persistent Petitioners ("Tap four untapped Advisors you control" - `Game.activation_cost` fills only EXILE and REVEAL object costs, so the activation cannot be paid), Fractured Sanity ("Each opponent mills N cards" unread for any N) and Blasphemous Edict (its "thirteen or more creatures" alternative-cost condition is unread, so it would add a silent card); Army of the Damned and Archive Trap are used instead.
- 2026-09-14: Number sweep G9 - `Game.condition_holds` is the one board-condition reader for "Activate only if ..." and a modal "If ..., choose N instead"; an unread activation condition now refuses (it used to allow, so Barbarian Ring's threshold and Rimewind Cryomancer's snow count never restricted anything). Spell-count caps read who/count/quality and a conjoined turn window (Curse of Exhaustion, Deafening Silence, Fires of Invention). Not built: descend tracking (CR 700.11, Molten Collapse) needs a per-turn graveyard-entry count on Player plus a schema bump outside G9's regions, so "you descended this turn" fails closed with a ConditionalClauseNotice; "choose any number instead" (Inscriptions) is likewise unread.
- 2026-09-14: Number sweep G13. `_sacrifice_restriction` returns `_SacrificeRestriction(is_self, candidates, quantity)` and one `_choose_permanents_to_sacrifice` serves conditional, reflexive and Gut's attacking-token cost. Dead end: `targeting.permanent_description` reads a lone plural subtype ("Islands", "Foods") as creature + subtype "islands", so an Island is rejected; `_bare_subtype_word` reads that shape first. effects.py defines `_singular_subtype` three times and the last ("drop one s") shadows the vocabulary one at module scope - not changed here. Morbid is `Game.creatures_died_this_turn`, counted at the dies emit; finality counters are a replacement in `applicable_effects`. G13-4 (Subtlety's "choose up to one target ... its owner puts it") is left pinned to one on purpose: the singular anaphora is the grammar, and no printing in any format prints another count. G13-3 likewise has only Gut (count one).
- 2026-09-14: Number sweep G12 - self/group counters read X-where and "that many" via a local `_triggering_event_amount`, group counters (Honden cycle, Go-Shintai of Boundless Vigor) read through `amount_expression`, and targeted counter placement reads a trailing "for each `<quality>` you control" (Immaculate Magistrate). Merge integration: the three same-named `_singular_subtype` functions this module carried (count_expression, transform-all, amass) are now uniquely named - `_singular_subtype_for_count_expression` (G7), `_singular_subtype_for_transform_all` (G12), `_singular_subtype_for_amass` (independently renamed by both G7's merge and G12 itself, identically, so the merge picked up cleanly) - closing the shadow G13 flagged but left alone. Overlap for G0b: `_triggering_event_amount` (self counters' X-where/that-many) and the untap-count "that many" reader on dressdown-self-sac (engine main, not yet in num-integration) solve the same problem from two sides; G12's version is kept here, and the two should be reconciled into one reader when dressdown-self-sac lands.
- 2026-09-14: Number sweep G8 - cost phrases read their count through `numbers.py`. An object cost's count is `cost._COST_COUNT` (a number, X, or "one or more") read by `_cost_count`; a variable component carries the existing `CostComponent.variable_count` with `required` left at the fewest that pays (0 for X, 1 for "one or more") and `amount` for life and counters, and `cost.announce_count` fixes it from the announced X or the nominated objects in `Game.activation_cost` - no new persisted field, no schema bump. The per-turn activation limit reads `MULTIPLIER` against the existing `Game.activations` count. Blight reuses `Casting.blight_paid` and `additional_costs`: `pay_blight` handles optional, mandatory "blight X" and "blight N or pay {M}" (unblighted charges the mana via `mandatory_additional_costs(blighted=)`), and `_announce_blight_x` reads Soul Immolation's "greatest toughness" cap and refuses any other X cap. An activated "Blight N" asks which creature in `begin_activating`. Dead ends as proof cards: only silent-in-the-sweep printings exist for Pay X life (Krumar Initiate - endure unread anywhere), exile X from graveyard (Necropolis Fiend - its -X/-X is not layer-readable), discard X (Gix), exile / sacrifice "one or more" (Corpseweft, Radiant Lotus - "this way" counts unread) and blight X (Soul Immolation - its damage sentence); Champion of Stray Souls was swapped for Copper-Leaf Angel and Manaforge Cinder for Sewer Rats because the sweep reads those. "Tap X untapped artifacts" reads X but stays unpayable: `Game.activation_cost` has no tap-object fill (the G0 Persistent Petitioners gap).
- 2026-09-14: Number sweep G1 - "for each `<qualifier>`" on gain life, lose life, draw and mill (Kaito's hardcoded regex folded in), X-where on scry and surveil, discard "up to N/any number of", "may discard N, if you do" (an unread "draw that many" tail records a notice), and delayed "draw N cards". `DrawACardForController` gained `count` - a persisted `DelayedEffectSpec` field, schema bumped 20 -> 21 (see `Game.CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`'s own docstring) even though `count`'s default of 1 matches every pre-G1 use, per the bump rule's first bullet having no such exception. Atlas, Keruga, Mastery of the Unseen, Oath of Jace and Thrilling Discovery came off the silent list.
- 2026-09-19: Merge of num-integration (b07dbd1) into int-engine-dsl (39f1796). Reconciled the G0b overlap G12's own memory line above flagged: dressdown-self-sac's local `_triggering_event_amount` (untap-step "that many" via `ExtraTriggerEvent.UNTAPPED_DURING_UNTAP_STEP.amount`) and G12's own `_triggering_event_amount` (self/group counters' X-where/"that many" via `amount_expression`/`count_expression`) are now one function: it tries the triggering-event amount first (untap-step count, Millennium Calendar/Marauding Mako) and falls through to the X-where/count_expression reader otherwise (Heroes' Bane, Voja, Jaws of the Conclave), so a card printing either shape gets a reader and neither mechanism was dropped. Schema settled at 21 (num-integration's own docstring already carried the combined note including int-engine-dsl's 16->17 turns_taken_by bump, since num-integration branched after e1-land-helpers landed); no hotfix commit had added a persisted field needing its own bump. `tests/scenarios/test_untap_step_count_trigger.py` (hotfix) and `tests/scenarios/test_counters_that_many_trigger.py` (G12) both kept and both pass against the merged reader.
- 2026-09-19: Number sweep G0b - one "that many" reader, `effects.that_many`: `Game.record_count` (private, cleared by `resolve_top`) then the resolving trigger's `event.amount`; `_triggering_event_amount` is gone. Reflexive abilities get the count written into their text as a digit (`_carry_count`) because their targets are announced later from the text alone. "this way" reads `AbilityOnStack.cost_exiled_card_ids`/`cost_sacrificed_card_ids`, now filled from the paid `CostPayment` (`Game._opened_payments`), not from the caller. Scapeshift and Necrodominance whole-text parsers retired. Batched "one or more <desc> attack" fires once with its own count. Schema 21 -> 22.
- 2026-09-19: G0b follow-ups - emblems are CardStates with `Layout.EMBLEM` in `shared_zones[Zone.COMMAND]` (not a new model), so triggers/parse_abilities work unchanged; a DIES condition with no self-reference and no "another" now matches any creature (it used to be read as self-only, which made Blade of the Bloodchief and Ygra unmatchable). Per-player "that many" is `Game.counting_for`, a scope on the same private channel.
- 2026-09-19: g0b-enterswith. "As [this] enters, [you may] sacrifice any number of <description>. [If you do, ]it enters with [<multiplier>] that many <kind> counters on it." (Shimatsu the Bloodcloaked, Devouring Hellion): `_read_counters` learned "[<multiplier>] that many <kind> counters" (`ReplacementEffect.that_many_multiplier`), and `_self_enters_modifiers` pairs the AS_ENTERS sacrifice clause with the ENTERS_WITH follower the same way it already pairs a shockland's pay-life-or-tapped choice, reusing `effects._sacrifice_restriction` for the candidate description. Dead end found along the way: `_names_of` only split a legendary short name on a comma, so Shimatsu's own "As Shimatsu enters" (no comma in "Shimatsu the Bloodcloaked") never counted as self-referential (CR 201.5c) - fixed by also adding the bare first word. `scripts/sweep_pool.py`'s `_static_ability_category` had no reader for CR 614.12 "enters" replacement statics at all, so every card of this shape - Walking Ballista's own X case included - was `static_unaudited` regardless of engine coverage; it now asks the real `_self_enters_modifiers` against a throwaway permanent (`_self_enters_line_category`), which also promoted Steam Vents and the other shocklands. No schema bump: `ReplacementEffect` is never persisted on `Game`.
- 2026-09-19: G0b mana retention - `ManaRetentionScope`/`ManaPool.retained` is the general mechanism CR 500.5a/500.5b need: mana tagged `until` a scope survives `ManaPool.empty` unless that scope is in the caller's `expiring` set, spends exactly like ordinary floating mana, and is decremented on a successful `pay()` the same way restricted mana already was. Firebending (`END_OF_COMBAT`, expired in `Game._advance_past_checks`/`advance_to` when the step that just ended was `Step.END_OF_COMBAT`) and Photon, Mighty Marvel (`END_OF_TURN`, expired inside `Game.cleanup_step`'s CR 514.2 action) both went from matched-but-inert regexes to real behaviour with no new parser. `machines/cost.py`'s payment machinery (`_copy_pool`, `_pay_impl`'s rollback snapshot, `_write_back_remainder`, `_restore_payer_pool`, and the auto-tap flow solver's `_Bucket` shape/sort key in `_bucket_map`/`_chosen_alternative`/`_best_flow_plan`) needed the same `retained` field threading `restricted` already had - missing it silently dropped an unspent retention tag on write-back even though the live pool still held the mana. Schema stays 22 (G0b's existing bump covers it).
- 2026-09-19: "deals X damage to each opponent and each <type> they control, where X is ..." (Cavalier of Flame) reads X through `_resolve_multiplied_amount` like the unfiltered each-opponent/each-creature shapes G2 widened; the damage-X agent built its own copy on main, only its tests and fixtures were kept.
- 2026-09-19: "Put a counter on X. If X is a <Subtype>, put N counters on it instead" is one whole-text reader (`_parse_counters_more_if_subtype_clause`): equipped/enchanted creature is the source's attached_to, a target resolves normally; proven on Elder Cathar.
- 2026-09-19: int-engine-dsl merge of num-g0b: fu-costs' `Player.emblems: list[Emblem]` and G0b's command-zone emblems unified into one subsystem - emblems are `Layout.EMBLEM` CardStates in `shared_zones[Zone.COMMAND]`, `Player.emblems` is a read-only view of them, and their statics join the computed board (so Kaito's anthem applies). Schema 23 settles both lines' 21 -> 22.
- 2026-09-19: FRA S4 (planeswalker/loyalty subsystem). Two general engine bugs found via this stream's own proof cards, fixed at cause: `Game.legal_target`'s CR 608.2b re-check substituted an announced X back into ability text only when the printed cost literally had `{X}` (a mana symbol) - a loyalty ability's own "-X"/"+X" cost has none, so every variable-loyalty-cost ability's own target was re-checked against a literal, unsubstituted "X" and silently dropped (Sorin, Vengeful Bloodlord's -X reanimated nothing); fixed to read `cost_announces_x` like the resolution-side dispatch already does. Two independent sentence-splitters (`abilities._layer_readable_sentences_uncached`, `effects._parse_whole_text_token_clause`) treated any period as a sentence boundary with no quote-awareness, breaking any multi-sentence quoted grant or token CDA ability (Way of the Deathbringer's own granted "-2", and pre-existing Wrenn and Seven's own -3 token) - both fixed with a quote-depth toggle. `numbers.py` had no compound number word above the tens ("twenty-five") at all. Documented, deliberate gap: `Game.proliferate` is permanents-only - CR 701.34a's player-counter half needs a player-choice decision shape `DecisionKind` doesn't carry yet, and nothing in this stream's own scope needs it.
- 2026-09-20: T2, `payability_matrix(..., assuming_untapped=True)`. The untapping goes through `Game.untap_step_rule` (T1) and then `Player.untap_all`, never a local filter and never a direct `state.tapped = False`: the rule deliberately leaves CR 122.1d's stun counter and CR 701.43a's exert to `CardState.untap`, so untapping the batch by hand would report a stunned or exerted permanent as untapping and the hypothetical would disagree with the step it is predicting. Two things the requirement asked to be provable turned out to need a design decision each. "No permanent another player controls is touched" has no observable consequence in the matrix at all - a seat cannot tap an opponent's land, so untapping one changes no answer - and the obvious proof card does not work: `legal_actions`' affordability verdict does not fold in a board-wide static cost modifier, so an untapped Trinisphere (floor of three mana) leaves Counterspell reported payable off two Islands. Noted, not chased: that is a real gap in `legal_actions`, not this task's. What makes the claim provable instead is a second notice, generated from the permanents that actually changed from tapped to untapped, so the answer itself names what moved and a reader can see that the opponent's permanent and the "doesn't untap" one are not in the list. The same notice is what proves the CR 122.1d route: a stunned permanent is in the batch, is not in the notice, and its counter on the caller's own game is not spent.
- 2026-09-20: Walking Ballista's own X made it uncastable ("asks for target, but when I pick one I get error, that it can't take more than 0 targets"). `Game.announcement_profile`'s `{X}`-substituted branch was the one cast-time reader still calling `profile_for_text` (whole-text, built for one ability's own words) instead of `spell_profile_for_text`/`_spell_text` (drops ability lines, CR 113.1c), so once a caller announced X it alone counted the activated "It deals 1 damage to any target." as the creature spell's own requirement - `targeting_profile`, `Casting.targeting`, and `legal_actions` (no `x_value` at all) already agreed on 0 targets, which is why this only reproduced after typing an X. Swapped in `spell_profile_for_text`, already written and already naming Walking Ballista in its own docstring; the swap also fixed a quieter gap, protection going unchecked on this branch's profiles, since only `spell_profile_for_text` stamps `TargetingProfile.source_card`. Swept 1420 printings at X=0/1/3: exactly three moved, all from `any target` to none - Walking Ballista, Chamber Sentry (same activated shape, different printing), Ajani's Anguish (the identical "any target" on a *triggered* ability on an Enchantment instead). `scenarios/test_x_spell_announcement_excludes_ability_targets.py`.
- 2026-09-20: kicker was never offered to a seat, for any card - and the mechanic was not the missing thing. Reported live as "Sowing Mycospawn, cast with kicker, I simply wasn't presented with the option to pay the kicker". `Casting.pay_kicker`/`pay_replicate`/`pay_offspring` and `CastRecord.kicked` were all built and passing, and `Game._announce_optional_costs` already offered the whole family for a cast the engine makes during resolution (CR 608.2g) - but **both** of the two guarantees this library owes its consumers missed it: `Action` had no field for an optional additional cost, so `legal_actions` told a seat nothing (one CAST row, `cost` `{3}{G}`, nothing else - identical for Burst Lightning and Orim's Chant, so this was never about Sowing Mycospawn), and `Game.cast` - the atomic entry `magic_cards_model_api.engine_api.cast_card` mirrors - had `offspring`/`blight`/`gift`/`splice`/`convoke` parameters but no `kicker` or `replicate`, so a seat that somehow knew still could not say yes. Every existing kicker test drove `Casting` by hand, which no seat does; that is what let a fully-built mechanic be unreachable for months with a green suite. Fixed at the mechanic, one enumeration for both surfaces so they cannot drift again: `machines/casting.optional_additional_costs(card)` reads every CR 601.2b optional additional cost a printing carries - the keyword lines (kicker CR 702.33a, multikicker CR 702.33c, replicate CR 702.56a, offspring CR 702.175a, gift CR 702.174a, `repeatable` read off the keyword's own rule rather than guessed) and the "As an additional cost ..., you may ..." sentences (CR 701.68a blight, and anything else with `understood=False`) - and is read by `legal_actions` (into `Action.additional_cost_options`) *and* by `Game._announce_optional_costs`. `Game.cast` gained `kicker=`/`replicate=` as counts, not bools, because CR 702.33c/702.56a are "any number of times" while CR 702.33a is not. Two deliberate non-changes: the option stays out of `Action.cost` (CR 118.8d - an unannounced additional cost belongs there no more than an unannounced `{X}` does), and `understood=False` is a loud row rather than an omission, mirroring `Action.restriction_understood`. **Still open, and not an engine defect**: `magic_cards_model_api` has to read the new field and pass the announcement back - the engine can now say it and take it, but nothing upstream does yet.
- 2026-09-20: a free cast under a `PlayPermission` was free only on the two paths that *announced* it. Reported live as Ugin, Eye of the Storms' `−11`: "the spells should be castable from exile for free, but the engine kept tapping and paying some mana for them. Not all, random-ish." The permission machinery was right and the grant was right (`pays_mana_cost=False`, round-trips, survives a decode); the asymmetry was in `Casting`. `_refuse_unless_allowed_from_its_zone` deliberately accepts a live permission as CR 601.3's claim with nothing announced - a resolving effect's grant *is* the permission, unlike a keyword - but `total_cost` reads `alternative_cost`, which only `use_permission` ever set, so any caller driving a `Casting` by hand cast the card legally and paid its printed mana cost, while `Game.cast` and `engine_api.cast_card` (both read `play_permission_for` before CR 601.2a's move, both call `use_permission()`) cast it free. Same board, same permission, two prices - that is the "random-ish". Fixed at the mechanic with `Casting._claim_zone_permission_if_unannounced`, run at CR 601.2f before the cost is locked: CR 118.9b's second sentence ("an effect that allows you to cast a spell may require a certain alternative cost to be paid") is what makes claiming it right rather than presumptuous, and is also where it stops - the `Zone.HAND` guard keeps Omniscience's CR 604.2 static grant an optional alternative cost, because casting from hand was legal without it. The step machine is not rewound (`use_permission` goes through `_enter_modes`, which refuses after CR 601.2c's targets); the two fields are set directly. CR 118.9d untouched: a tax (Sphere of Resistance) and a printed additional cost (Cathartic Reunion's discard) are still owed on a free cast. Generality on Ral, Leyline Prodigy's `−8`, a different parser and wording. Separate defect found and not fixed: `Game.alternative_costs_for` (the pure port `legal_actions` reads) omits `Casting.alternative_costs`' conditional-printed-offer block, so the two listings' **indices** disagree for Archive Trap, Mindbreak Trap and Disrupting Shoal - an index-based announcement taken from the wrong listing names the wrong cost.
- 2026-09-22: Ensnaring Bridge was never read (static_unaudited, and `attack_refusal` only looked at the attacker's own text). `combat.group_attack_restriction_refusal` now reads "Creatures [with power <cmp>] can't attack [you]" off every permanent's computed abilities at ask time, bound via `effects.count_expression` for the source's controller (CR 109.5). "Can't attack you" is read as the player only, not their planeswalkers. `Game.declare_attackers` used to tap earlier attackers before a later one's refusal raised; it now pre-judges every attacker (CR 508.1).
- 2026-09-22: CR 603.1b compound trigger conditions ("When you cast this spell and whenever this creature attacks") are parsed as one `TriggeredAbility` per condition sharing `text`/effect (`abilities._split_compound_condition`), not a new multi-condition field: every consumer (`condition_matches`, `zones_ability_functions_in`, `triggers_for`) already keys on one `event`, and CR 113.6k wants per-condition zones anyway. Before, the first matched event word swallowed the compound (Leveler cast half, Bowmasters draw half, Voice opponent-casts half all dead). Same wave reversed the anaphoric "Its controller ..." skip on an indestructible target: printed rulings (Beast Within, Assassin's Trophy) say the second sentence still runs for a legal target that was not destroyed.
- 2026-09-22: Mana source control. `CostPayment.tap_for_mana` never asked whose a hand-picked source was, so a seat naming an opponent's untapped land paid with it; auto-tap already filtered by payer. `cost.why_payer_cannot_use` is now the one check (CR 602.2/605.3, and CR 723.5a when the source's controller is deciding for the payer), used by `tap_for_mana`, both auto-tap candidate scans and `Game.why_cannot_pay_with` (which had no production caller). The payer is the spell's controller, so a controlled player's cast pays from their own board. Convoke/improvise/delve already refused another player's objects; their refusals now cite the keyword's rule, delve says whose graveyard, and a controller's creature offered for a controlled player's convoke cites CR 723.5a.
- 2026-09-23: CR 603.3b ORDER_TRIGGERS now carries `Decision.abilities` (`PendingAbility`: id, source_id, source_name, text, controller) because the batch is in no zone when asked and the api could not name it or point at its source; schema 35 -> 36.
- 2026-09-23: T06 keyword-line expansions (Battle cry, Outlast, Power-up). Battle cry (CR 702.91a) is `_TRIGGER_EXPANSIONS`/`KEYWORD_TRIGGER_EFFECTS`'s new "battle cry" entry, an untargeted attack trigger whose pumped set is fixed by reading `game.attacking_creatures()` once, at resolution (CR 611.2c) - one `ContinuousEffect` per other attacker, excluding the source, rather than a dynamic selector, since a dynamic "is attacking" filter would keep re-evaluating after the fixed-set moment CR 611.2c requires. Outlast (CR 702.107a) goes through the same synthetic-`KeywordActivation` shape equip/cycling already use, and its `{T}` cost exposed a real gap: `KeywordActivation.why_not` had no CR 602.5a/302.6 summoning-sickness check at all, because equip's and cycling's own costs never carry `{T}` so nothing before this exercised it - fixed generally (any battlefield-zone `KeywordActivation` that taps its own card, via `Game.can_activate_tap_ability`), not specially for outlast. Power-up (CR 702.193a-b) needed no new resolution machinery - its printed line already parses as an ordinary `ActivatedAbility` and its effect clause already resolves generically - only `ActivatedAbility.power_up` (read off the same em-dash prefix `exhaust` already is) feeding CR 702.193a's "activate only once" and CR 702.193b's cost reduction (`mana.minus(ManaCost.for_card(permanent.card))`, reusing `ManaCost.minus`'s existing CR 118.7a-d). Reusing exhaust's own `exhausted` bookkeeping (as instructed) surfaced a latent, unrelated defect in it: `Game._activation_key` keyed only on `permanent.id`, which this engine's own "same id, new object" design (`CardState`'s module docstring) keeps stable across an ordinary zone change - so exhaust's own claim that "a card that leaves and returns is not exhausted by its earlier self" was false for a real (non-token) permanent, never caught because no exhaust test bounced one. Fixed at the shared root: `_activation_key` now folds in `permanent.zone_change_stamp`, the field CR 400.7's new-object semantics actually live on, correct for both exhaust and power-up and verified against `test_exhaust.py` (still green) plus a bounce-and-recast proof in `test_power_up.py`. New fixtures: Signal Pest, Accorder Paladin (battle cry, off-pool); Ainok Bond-Kin (outlast, off-pool, proves its own "creature with a +1/+1 counter has first strike" static end to end); Abzan Falconer, Brave Brawler, Pet Avengers, Aerial Doombot (all reported).
- 2026-09-23: T09 discover. `DiscoverResult`/`discover` in `machines/keywords.py`, next to `cascade`, reusing its exile-until/free-cast/random-bottom machinery with two deliberate divergences from it: "N or less" (CR 701.57a) is inclusive, read as `mana_value_below=n + 1` against the existing exclusive-bound helpers rather than adding a second bound parameter to them; and a declined (or uncastable) hit goes to hand (CR 701.57a's own second sentence), not the bottom with the misses - cascade's `bottomed = [... not (cast_hit and state is result.hit)]` line becomes `bottomed = [state for state in result.exiled if state is not result.hit]` here, with the hand move done by hand when the hit was not cast. `machines/effects._parse_discover_clause` reads "discover N" as a bare keyword-action clause (the same shape as amass/investigate), registered in `machines/keywords.executes` for the sweep. No schema bump: nothing new is persisted, the free cast already goes through `cast_during_resolution`'s existing `x_value=0` (CR 107.3b) and CastRecord fields. Proof cards: Trumpeting Carnosaur (bare ETB), Geological Appraiser ("if you cast it" intervening if, read separately and for free by the existing `intervening_if_holds`), Zoetic Glyph (leaves-the-battlefield). Off-pool X=0 proof: Walking Ballista discovered and cast free enters 0/0 and dies to CR 704.5f on the spot - a stronger, real-game demonstration of CR 107.3b than a quiet counter check would have been. `tests/dispatch_fingerprint_baseline.json` regenerated (`mise run dispatch:fingerprint`): the new clause parser and the two new pool printings (Trumpeting Carnosaur, Geological Appraiser) both shift dispatch-chain winners, an intended reordering.
- 2026-09-23: T01 sweep truth. OWNER RULE, standing: every card legal in Modern, Pioneer or Standard (`scripts/sweep_pool.in_target_formats` - banned counts as legal, since a banned card was printed into the format and can be unbanned; only `not_legal` in all three excludes) must be fully supported - a fail-closed `NOTICE` is never an acceptable end state for one of those, only an unclosed gap, so `report_effects` now lists legal notice cards in their own "legal cards failing closed" section (`legal_cards_failing_closed`) rather than folding them into the ordinarily-fine notice count; an out-of-format card may rest on a notice indefinitely. `main` filters `--keywords`/`--effects`/`--targeting` through the format rule by default (`--all-formats` disables it, `--card` always reports the named card and flags it when the filter would have dropped it), which lowered flow-1's own `--effects` silent count (118 of 1406 -> 106 of 1069 cards) by removing off-format printings such as Corpseweaver Prodigy's digital-only Conjure from the count entirely, on owner instruction, rather than building Conjure (no CR text exists for it at all). `ABILITY_WORDS` is now CR 207.2c's complete verbatim list (60 entries, `mise run rules:find 207.2c`), matched through `_normalized_ability_word` (curly apostrophe -> straight, a trailing printed count stripped so `Descend 4`/`Descend 8` both reach one `descend` entry) rather than the eight words a sweep had happened to see; doing this surfaced that the pre-existing list's uncited "ascend" entry was wrong - CR 702.131 ascend is a real, already-built keyword ability (`machines/keywords.py`), not an ability word, and had been silently hidden from every sweep report since it was added. Flavor words (CR 207.2d) generalised past the bullet-mode label shape to any `<Label> — <text>` line (em dash with real space on both sides) whose label is one of the card's own Scryfall keywords and is neither a CR 701/702 keyword name (`CR_KEYWORD_NAMES`, transcribed from `rules:find 701`/`702`) nor a CR 207.2c ability word - Power-up and Cumulative upkeep both wear the same "Label — text"/"Label—text" clothing as a real flavor word and must not be swallowed by it. Partner and Choose a Background joined `NEEDS_NO_CODE` (CR 702.124a: Commander-only deck construction, function before the game begins); Conjure got nothing, on the reasoning above. Six already-built keywords (Shroud, Split second, Endure, Recruit, Tiered, Spree) and Ascend got named `executed_keywords` branches, each conditioned on the machinery actually reading that card's own clause (Tiered/Spree: the modal profile is understood and a mode carries a cost; Endure/Recruit: the clause parser matches the card's own text, X substituted first) rather than on the bare keyword name. Waterbend and Hexproof from are deliberately not registered here (T02/T03).
- 2026-09-23: T03 hexproof. `Game.hexproof_refusal(target, source_controller, source)` is now the one hexproof vocabulary (Fork F8a): `TargetingProfile._check_hexproof` beside `_check_shroud`, the live candidate lists (`_legal_target_candidates`, `Game.legal_targets`, `legal_actions._target_candidates` - which never filtered shroud either, so a live trigger ask offered shrouded targets) and `Game.legal_target`'s CR 608.2b recheck; `Casting._hexproof_refusal` only delegates. The "[quality] source" is read computed on the battlefield and from new `CardState.last_known_colors/types/subtypes` once gone (no colour LKI existed). Player hexproof/shroud: static "You have ..." lines read fresh off the computed board; effect grants on `Player.targeting_grants` (`PlayerTargetingGrant`, one per CR 702.11f quality) via `Game.grant_player_hexproof`/`grant_player_shroud`, expired in `cleanup_step`/`begin_turn_for`; "You gain hexproof [from ...] until ..." is a `parse_clause_effect` player clause. Knight of Grace's pump needed `SelfIfControls.color`/`controlled_by`. Schema 36 -> 37.
- 2026-09-23: T02 keyword proofs + Waterbend. DDR#991's own `Game.activation_cost` OTHER-branch fold-in (`total_cost_for_phrase` for a mana-shaped action word) was already built and merely mis-flagged `xfail` in `judge/test_judge_costs.py`'s module docstring - fixed the docstring, no code gap there. CR 701.67b (the tap method reaches only the waterbend cost's *own* generic, even when the total cost has other generic in it - no printed card needs this yet, but the rule is explicit) needed a real field: `TotalCost.waterbend_generic`/`CostPayment.waterbend_generic`, set by `total_cost_for_phrase`/`Game.activation_cost` and checked in `objects_as_costs.waterbend` ahead of the ordinary `mana_owed` bound. CR 701.67c's "whenever you waterbend" is `ExtraTriggerEvent.WATERBENDS` (the same no-`TriggerEvent`-member pattern `COUNTERS_PUT`/`MANA_ADDED` already use), emitted once per waterbend cost actually *paid* by `Game.activate_ability` (not per tap) - proved on Avatar Aang, whose own printed condition is a four-verb OR-list ("waterbend, earthbend, firebend, or airbend"), which needed its own condition-boundary regex in `abilities._parse_triggered` (`_BENDING_LIST_CONDITION_BOUNDARY`, mirroring the existing cast-spell type-list boundary) since a bare top-level comma split cuts the list apart from its own trailing "or". `EARTHBENDS`/`FIREBENDS`/`AIRBENDS` are named with no emitter (nothing bends that way yet) purely so the condition parser reads the whole printed list rather than only the one word this task's own keyword has a rule number for. Aang's "if you've done all four this turn, transform" reuses `Game.turn_facts`/`record_turn_fact` (the existing general per-turn ledger, CR 606.2's loyalty trigger already uses it) - no schema bump, since the field already exists; the other three bends' facts are staged directly through that same API in tests, since no built mechanic emits them yet. Separately, found and fixed a real ordering bug while writing the Spree proof: `ModalProfile.resolve` returned chosen modes in *announcement* order, not printed order, so `choose_modes([3, 1])` ran mode 3 before mode 1 against CR 608.2a/702.42b - fixed with a stable sort by `Mode.number`, general to every modal card, not special-cased. Sound the Trumpets' "Counter target spell. If that spell's mana value was 2 or less, recruit." is a second Recruit shape (CR 400.7 anaphora gating the keyword action) needing its own clause-*pair* reader (`_parse_counter_then_recruit_by_mana_value_pair`), alongside the existing bare-word one; `scripts/sweep_pool.py`'s recruit registration check was extended to try both, since the sweep's own check is a second, independent reader of the same clause and would otherwise disagree with what actually executes.
- 2026-09-23: T04 infect/wither/toxic. CR 120.3's results branch inside `Game.deal_damage` (not a replacement, not a post-damage hook): infect gives poison via `add_player_counters` with no life-loss event, infect/wither give -1/-1 counters via `add_counters`, both after prevention. CR 702.90d's LKI needed a persisted snapshot, `CardState.last_known_keywords` (taken in `_note_left_battlefield`, cleared by `move_to` like the P/T one) - schema 37 -> 38, which also covers pool printings (Blightsteel, Myr Convert) whose combat now resolves differently; take the next free number at merge. Damage-dealing ability sites now resolve their source with `source_or_last_known` so a ceased-to-exist token still carries its keywords. Toxic's N is read per instance by `abilities.keyword_abilities_in_lines`; granting "toxic 1" needed `layers._NUMBERED_KEYWORD_ITEM_RE` (whitelist `_KNOWN_NUMBERED_KEYWORDS`). Venerated Rotpriest's trigger subject "a creature you control" belongs to the targeted object, so `triggers._OBJECT_TARGET_SUBJECT` consumes it before the targeting-controller check. Noted, not done: noncombat lifelink and deathtouch are still applied only in `combat._deal_combat_damage`.
- 2026-09-23: T05 changeling. `CardState.subtypes` now includes changeling's creature types in every zone; `printed_subtypes` is the type line and is what layer 1 starts from, so on the battlefield the types arrive as a layer 4 CDA (`layers._changeling_effects`, built at layer 4 time so copy effects decide) that a later type-setting effect overrides. A battlefield creature-type reader must ask the computed board, never `CardState.subtypes`. `ManaSpend.casting` takes the spell's `CardState`, not its `Card`.
- 2026-09-23: T07 cumulative upkeep (CR 702.24). The multiplied cost is one `TotalCost` (`cost.cumulative_upkeep_cost`): "{A} or {B}" becomes one `{A/B}` per age counter, which is exactly "each choice per counter, all or nothing"; non-mana parts are limited to discard/sacrifice/life because those are all `Game.may_pay_unless_cost` can pay mid-resolution - any other printed cost fails closed with a notice rather than forcing a sacrifice. `may_pay_unless_cost`/`may_pay_mana_cost` take `spend=` so Snowfall's restricted mana pays it. No legal printing has two instances, so 702.24b's "triggers separately" is unproven by a scenario.
- 2026-09-23: T08 generic explore clause. `machines/effects._parse_explores_clause` reads "<subject> explores[, then it explores again]." (CR 701.44a-d) off triggered, activated, and spell text alike - subject one of "this creature"/"this permanent" (a literal self-name normalizes to this via `_self_normalized_effect_text` before the parser sees it)/"it" (self, gated to abilities: a resolving spell is never itself a permanent, CR 300.1), "target creature you control", or "each creature you control" (no Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal card carries this exact plural wording today - `o:"each creature you control explores"` and `o:"creatures you control explore"` both return zero Scryfall matches - so it is built for generality per the "target the mechanic" rule but has no scenario proof). Registered in the shared `_parse_mixed_clause`/`_parse_mixed_clauses` per-clause dispatcher, twice: once against a clause's whole unsplit text (the "again" doubling has a bare ", then " that `_CLAUSE_SEPARATOR` cuts into two fragments neither half of which parses alone - Jadelight Ranger, Over the Edge) and once per ordinary clause (a plain "it explores." sharing a card with something else - Enter the Unknown). CR 701.44c fix: `AbilityOnStack.source_or_last_known` in place of `.source`, both here and in Amalia Benavides Aguirre's own dedicated whole-sentence parser (`_parse_explores_then_conditional_mass_destroy`), which had the identical bug - her trigger stopped exploring *entirely* the moment she left the battlefield, rather than exploring with no counter (`_explore`'s own zone check already reads correctly off whichever object - live, wherever it currently sits, since this engine keeps one id across an ordinary zone change, or a detached last-known-information snapshot - is passed in). CR 701.44d (several explorers from one instruction go one at a time, APNAP) is `_explore_each_in_apnap_order`; every subject this parser reads names at most one permanent, so the multi-explorer branch is implemented and exercised only by construction, not by a real card (same "no printing exists" reasoning as "each creature you control" above). Proven live on Merfolk Branchwalker (land/nonland-keep/nonland-graveyard/empty-library/bounced-in-response), Jadelight Ranger (land-then-nonland, two-nonlands), Legion Vanguard (activated, "this creature"), Miner's Guidewing (death trigger, targeted), Enter the Unknown (spell, plus an unrelated second clause), and Over the Edge (modal spell mode, targeted + "again"). `tests/test_sweep_pool.py`'s T01 pool-wide acceptance set had Explore hardcoded as still-ignored (a forward-looking placeholder); updated to drop it now that it registers, twelve keywords remain (not the docstring's stale "fourteen", which was already off by one before this).
- 2026-09-23: T3's own review caught two real gaps, both fixed rather than argued away (every legal card must be fully supported - no silent-notice end state, see `~/.claude/.../memory/target-formats-modern-pioneer-standard.md`). (1) `test_copy_carries_choices.py`'s case (f) asserted against a reader the test defined itself, not the engine - and the engine genuinely had no "copy target instant or sorcery spell" dispatcher at all, so a real cast of the Modern-legal Increasing Vengeance silently did nothing (`unmodeled_trigger_effect_notice`). Built `machines/effects._parse_copy_target_spell_clause` (a `_COPY_TARGET_SPELL_RE` whole-text parse, read by `_parse_mixed_clauses` ahead of the ordinary clause split for the same bare-period reason `_parse_copy_that_spell_clause` already is) covering Reverberate's plain shape and Increasing Vengeance's "if this spell was cast from a graveyard, copy that spell twice instead" rider, keyed off *this spell's own* `CastRecord.is_copy`/`origin_zone` - not the copied target's - so a further copy of Increasing Vengeance itself correctly copies once, never twice (CR 707.10: a copy "isn't cast"). Case (f) now casts Increasing Vengeance for real (with flashback) and resolves it through the real dispatcher; a second case builds a copy of it and resolves that. The card's oracle text also needed `_strip_non_effect_preamble` run first - its trailing "Flashback {cost} (reminder)." line otherwise trails past the clause regex's own `$` anchor and the whole match silently fails. (2) `Game.copy_spell_targeting` (CR 707.10e, Frontline Heroism's own path) overwrote a copy's flat `targets` but never `CastRecord.mode_targets`, which `resolve_modal_spell` actually reads - a modal spell copied and forcibly retargeted this way would resolve against the *original's* target. Fixed by mirroring `offer_new_targets_for_copy`'s own `profile_for_modes_with_owners`/`assign_to_owners` sync, proven on Abrade (a modal card, no dispatcher change needed to reach it directly). Both are covered by new scenario cases in `test_copy_carries_choices.py`; `tests/dispatch_fingerprint_baseline.json` regenerated for the new dispatcher entry (`mise run dispatch:fingerprint`).
- 2026-09-23: T3-r1's own follow-up review caught a third real gap in the same file, same class of defect: case (e) asserted only `CastRecord.mana_spent` on the two records directly and never actually resolved either spell, so a real cast of the Modern-legal Dawnglow Infusion (`{X}{G/W}`, "You gain X life if {G} was spent to cast this spell and X life if {W} was spent to cast this spell") still resolved silently with no dispatcher at all - CR 707.2's own named example of what a copy does not carry, proven only on paper. Built `machines/effects._parse_gain_life_if_color_spent_clause` (a whole-text `_GAIN_LIFE_IF_COLOR_SPENT_RE` match, read by `_spell_effect_or_notice` next to `_parse_converge_exile_by_colors_spent` - the same "read `CastRecord.mana_spent`'s color keys at resolution" shape - and matched on the literal "X" the same reason `_parse_x_life_mass_debuff` is, since `effect_for`'s X-substitution cannot express "count how many of two named-color conditions held"), generalised over which two colors are named rather than hardcoded to {G}/{W}. Case (e) now resolves both spells for real: the original (paid {G}{G}, X=1) gains 1 life; the copy (same X=1, but `mana_spent` is never copied - CR 707.2) gains 0. `tests/dispatch_fingerprint_baseline.json` regenerated again for the new dispatcher entry.
- 2026-09-23: T10 monstrosity (CR 701.37a-c). `CardState.monstrous`/`monstrous_x` modelled directly on the `prepared` pattern - not an ability, not a copiable value, so a token copy or a "becomes a copy of" effect is simply not monstrous with zero exclusion logic anywhere; `Game._note_left_battlefield` is the only clearer (no "becomes unmonstrous" effect exists in the rules the way `prepared` has `become_unprepared`). CR 701.37c's X reaches the "becomes monstrous" trigger through `GameEvent.amount` on the `TriggerEvent.BECOMES_MONSTROUS` event itself, read back by `Game._effect_for`'s own substitution branch (parallel to CR 107.3m's ETB one) - a triggered ability has no `CastRecord` of its own to read X from the way a cast spell does. Polukranos, World Eater's own divided-damage-then-retaliate trigger ("Each of those creatures deals damage equal to its power to ~") is a new whole-text parser generalised on the target-list phrase, not pinned to "your opponents control". Schema bumped 41 -> 42.
- 2026-09-23: T11 goad (CR 701.15). `CardState.goaded_by` is a list of goader usernames; the entry is its own expiry (dropped in `begin_turn_for` for that player only), so no separate turn stamp. CR 508.1d is now counted per creature and per defender (`combat.attack_requirements_obeyed`, `most_obedient_attack`), not "holder was declared": each goader adds "attacks each combat" plus "attacks a player other than [goader]", so 1v1 must-attack falls out of the count rather than a shortcut, and `unobeyed_attack_requirement` takes `{creature_id: TargetRef}`. Schema 42 -> 43.
- 2026-09-23: T11 review fix. The mandatory "for each opponent, ... target X that player controls" is `TargetRequirement.one_per_opponent` on `_for_each_opponent_profile` (minimum 1; `TargetingProfile.check` with a game demands one target from each opponent controlling a legal one), and `Game._ask_targets` now runs `profile.check` after `ask` whenever a requirement has `distinct_controllers`, because `ask` only validates membership and count. Candidates are reordered one-per-controller first so the unscripted default answer is already legal. The profile regex now stops at a sentence end (`[^.]`), since it no longer needs "up to one" to anchor it.
- 2026-09-23: T13 sneak (CR 702.190a-b). A keyword option can now carry a timing *window* (`CastTiming.OWN_DECLARE_BLOCKERS`): `Casting.model_post_init` sets CR 117.1a's refusal aside only while such an option is usable and `cast()` re-raises it unless that keyword was announced, so a sorcery is castable in the window by sneak only. `cost.why_not_unblocked` is the one CR 509.1h check (blockers must have been declared; `Attack.blocked` sticks), shared with ninjutsu - which used to accept an attacker in the declare attackers step. The returned creature's defender is read as it is chosen and persisted as `CastRecord.sneak_defender`; schema 43 -> 44 (take the next free number at merge). `Game.cast` now passes `choosing` to `use_keyword` too. Proofs: Foot Ninjas (off-pool), Michelangelo's Technique.
- 2026-09-23: T14 miracle (CR 702.94). No `KeywordCastOption` on purpose: the miracle cost exists only while the linked trigger resolves, so it is cast through `Game.cast_during_resolution(alternative_cost=...)` + `Casting.use_triggered_alternative_cost`, never offered at priority. CR 702.94b's revealed state is computed (`Game.revealed_for_miracle`: same stamp in hand + its miracle trigger pending/stacked/resolving) rather than a stored flag, so it needs no clearing hooks. The reveal `MAY` defaults to decline (`default_may=False`, hidden information). Schema 44 -> 45 (new reveal question on the tape, new `ExtraTriggerEvent`).
- 2026-09-23: Flow 2 T15, ability-word clauses. Block cap (CR 509.1b, "can't be blocked by more than one creature", menace's mirror) lives in `combat.declaration_refusal` beside menace; its conditional grant (Akawalli's descend 8) is checked directly via `has_ability(BLOCKED_BY_AT_MOST_ONE_GRANT_TEXT)`, never through the sentence-search path `_evasion_restriction_sentences` uses, because a bare granted fragment with no subject and no trailing period is not a `layer_readable_sentences` sentence at all - the identical reason `ATTACK_WITHOUT_DEFENDER_TEXT` is checked as a literal rather than pattern-matched. Real, separate gap found and fixed along the way: `machines/abilities.py`'s `_ABILITY_WORD` (the CR 207.2c label stripper ahead of `_parse_triggered`) capped its second word at letters only, so "Descend 4"/"Descend 8" never stripped and every *triggered* descend clause in the pool silently became a `StaticAbility` instead - harmless for the static/pump shape (`machines/layers.py` reads raw oracle text directly and never consults this classification) but fatal for a triggered one (`abilities_triggered_by` only ever finds a real `TriggeredAbility`). Fixed to accept a bare number, matching `layers.py`'s own `_ABILITY_WORD_RE`. Descend's own intervening-*if* shape ("if there are N or more permanent cards in your graveyard") is new in `triggers.intervening_if_holds` (`_descend_permanent_card_count_holds`, `count_permanent_cards` imported locally inside the function to dodge a real circular import: `layers.py` imports `combat.py`, which imports `triggers.py`). Building "any number of target creatures each gain `<quoted ability>` until end of turn" (Strive) surfaced that this engine's only "grant a quoted ability" machinery (`quoted_ability_grants`/`sync_granted_ability_texts`) is sourced entirely off a *permanent's own printed static text*, reconciled fresh every board sweep - a one-shot spell resolution granting to its own *targets* has no such permanent to re-derive from (the existing docstring already names Urza's Saga's one-shot chapter II grant as the precedent this excludes). Went with direct-append-plus-`DelayedTrigger` instead (`_parse_plural_target_gain_quoted_ability_whole_text`, reusing `RevokeGrantedAbility` unchanged, watching `TriggerEvent.BEGINNING_OF_STEP`/`Step.CLEANUP` rather than a named game event - CR 514.3a keeps a cleanup step open for priority when something genuinely triggers there, so this is not a special case, just an under-used one) rather than widening the shared static-reconciliation function, which is read by every permanent on every sweep and felt like the wrong place to bolt on a one-shot-resolution code path. Same task also closed a genuinely unrelated, unconditional "enters with N counters" gap: `_ENTERS_WITH` only read a *leading* "If X, ... enters with Y" condition; Festerhide Boar's own Morbid line puts it trailing ("... enters with two +1/+1 counters on it if a creature died this turn"), which is a CR 614.1c replacement effect, not a triggered ability, so it shares no code with the intervening-if fix above despite reading as the "same" condition in English - `replacement._read_enters_condition` now has a "a creature died this turn" case of its own. `--card` keeps reporting Charging Rhino's/Festerhide Boar's own clauses as `static_unaudited` even after both are proven correct by scenario - confirmed against Mindskinner's identical, long-standing `static_unaudited` verdict for the same CR 509.1b shape, so this is `scripts/sweep_pool.py`'s own documented "an unknown, never a claim of coverage" category, not a bug to chase. `tests/dispatch_fingerprint_baseline.json` regenerated for the new dispatcher entries.
- 2026-09-23: T16 Spree's own five unread modes. Two adjacent defects, both surfaced only once the mode text actually reached its reader: (1) `mode_rules_text` (`machines/modes.py`) fed `machines/targeting.py` a mode's own *unstripped* CR 700.2h cost prefix ("{3} — Target player draws two cards."), so the mana cost's own digit read as if it were a printed target count - "target player" needing three targets, not one - making a mode whose printed cost differs from its own target count (Metamorphic Blast's second mode among them) uncastable with any legal target list at all; fixed by routing through `mode_effect_text` first, the same cost-stripping `machines/effects.py` already does for a resolution-time reader. (2) `_mass_creature_group_targets`'s own plain-prefix `startswith("creatures you control")` check silently matched a "with power N or greater" filtered subject too and handed back every creature the caster controls, dropping the filter entirely rather than reading it - a card printing the single-keyword shape (Gustrider Exuberant's own "gain flying") would have applied to the wrong group with nobody noticing, since the effect still "resolved" successfully; a multi-keyword filtered grant (Smuggler's Surprise's own "gain hexproof and indestructible") failed outright instead, because `_CLAUSE_SEPARATOR`'s bare " and " split it into two clause fragments before `_parse_mass_pump_group_clause` ever saw it whole - fixed with a whole-text early try in `_parse_mixed_clauses`, the same pattern `search_reveal_shuffle_top` already used for the identical bare-"and" shape. The five general readers (search-put-on-top with an optional reveal, becomes-creature-type-and-base-PT with a leading or trailing duration, mill-then-take-with-an-and/or-quality, the power-filtered mass-pump group grant with the set locked in once at CR 611.2c resolution, and the token-copy-of-target reader's widened noun phrase) are each proven off-pool: Ainok Guide, Startling Development, Seed of Hope, Gustrider Exuberant, Cackling Counterpart. `--card` now reports all four pool Spree cards HANDLED.
- 2026-09-23: T17 Compleated (CR 702.150a). `CastRecord` gained `phyrexian_life_symbols_paid` (schema bump 45 -> 46), read off `CostPayment.phyrexian_life_symbols_paid` (counting `ManaPayment.options` entries with `life` set - one per Phyrexian symbol CR 118.13a's own choice actually paid with life, not a flag). Deliberately did **not** fold the reduction into `ReplaceableEvent.counters`, the field every other CR 614.1c "enters with N counters" replacement already shares: those are all additive on top of an entering-with-nothing baseline, while compleated is subtractive from CR 306.5b's own intrinsic printed-loyalty replacement, which `counters` never carries in the first place (that intrinsic placement happens directly inside `CardState.move_to`, unmodelled as a `ReplaceableEvent` at all - see `_enter_with_loyalty`'s own docstring). Gave it a dedicated `ReplaceableEvent.loyalty_reduction` field instead, applied by `Game.enter_the_battlefield` right after `event.counters` is placed, via `remove_counters` clamped to what CR 306.5b actually put there (CR 122: never negative) - this also means a doubling effect that watches counters *placed* would not (wrongly) double compleated's removal, though nothing in the pool tests that interaction. No real printing (Nahiri, Ajani Sleeper Agent, Tamiyo Compleated Sage, or off-pool Nissa Ascended Animist with two symbols) reduces loyalty all the way to a state-based 0 through its own symbols alone (the lowest, Ajani, bottoms out at 2 with one symbol paid); that corner is proven instead by finishing a reduced-entry walker off with ordinary loyalty-removing damage. The copy-not-affected corner needed a different controller for the copy (`copy_spell(..., controller="bob")`) rather than the caster's own - copying a cast, still-legendary-named planeswalker under the *same* controller as the real cast triggers CR 704.5j's legend rule the instant the second one enters, an unrelated rule that would kill one of them before the loyalty comparison ever happened. `Compleated` was misclassified in `scripts/sweep_pool.py`'s `ABILITY_WORDS` (a stale bug from before this task, not fixed here - out of this task's scope, left for the sweep-truth cluster); it is `sunburst`-shaped instead, so registered by hand in `ignored_keywords` the same way Sunburst is, which makes the misclassification harmless regardless (`ignored_keywords` `or`s the two checks). `tests/dispatch_fingerprint_baseline.json` regenerated for the three new fixture printings' own ability text.
- 2026-09-23: T19 "Partner with [name]" (CR 702.124j), the in-game half. `machines/keywords._partner_with_ability` writes the keyword line out as a CR 603.6a enters-trigger reading `[name]` off `KeywordAbility.parameter` (never a card-name table); `machines/effects._parse_target_player_may_search_named_card_clause` runs it through the ordinary search/reveal/shuffle machinery (`Game.search_and_put`), gated by an outer `Game.may` (declining skips the shuffle too) and CR 701.23b's own declinable per-card find. Deck-construction half (CR 702.124a) stays inert, same as plain "partner"/"choose a Background" already are. Found and fixed a real, general gap while wiring the scenario tests: `Game.why_cannot_target_with_trigger` (the `choose_trigger_targets` scripting shortcut) read only `parse_abilities(source.card)`'s literally-printed triggers, never `machines/keywords.keyword_triggered_abilities`'s CR 702.1 keyword-shorthand ones - nothing in that family (Ward, Evoke, Battle cry, ...) needed a target before this one did, so a scripted target for any of them would have silently fallen through to whichever *other* printed ability the card happened to carry and been refused in its name; the live, unscripted path (`_ask_trigger_targets`, reading `AbilityOnStack.ability` directly) was never affected.
