Verified exhaustively against Scryfall (52 "Partner with" printings, every `legalities.modern`/`.pioneer`/`.standard` read) that Evie Frye/Jacob Frye is the *only* Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal "Partner with" pair ever printed - no second real pair exists to add for an off-pool proof; genericity is instead proven by running both directions of that one pair.
- 2026-09-23: G6 (f1-t9-r2) merge: `GameEvent.zone_change_stamp` (CR 400.7e dead-self lookup) is persisted through `AbilityOnStack.event`, so schema 46 -> 47 was added at merge time; the branch itself had not bumped.
- 2026-09-23: T12 Prowl (CR 702.76a). `Game.combat_damage_to_players_this_turn` (`CombatDamageToPlayer`, schema 49 -> 50 - renumber to the next free number at merge) captures controller + computed creature types *as the damage is dealt* in `combat._deal_combat_damage`; the older `combat_damage_dealt_this_turn_by` (ids only) cannot answer "at the time it dealt that damage" for a source that died or changed since. The prowl-paid condition is a generic "if this spell's <keyword> cost was paid" reader (`effects._parse_if_keyword_cost_paid_clause`, CastRecord keyword + keyword_cost_paid), not a prowl-named one.
- 2026-09-23: Flow 1 T12, Veil of Summer's player hexproof (CR 702.11c/702.11d/702.11f), on top of flow 2's already-landed generic player-hexproof machinery. `_parse_you_and_group_gain_clause`'s player half is now handed to `parse_clause_effect` (the same `_PLAYER_GAINS_HEXPROOF_RE` reader flow 2 already proved reads it) instead of only recording a `PlayerSubjectSplitNotice`; when it parses, `Game.grant_player_hexproof` is called with the resolving spell/ability's controller, reusing `hexproof_qualities_in` (the permanent-side CR 702.11f quality reader) rather than a second one - a future unreadable player-half predicate still falls back to the notice unchanged. No enforcement code changed: `Game.hexproof_refusal`/`TargetingProfile.check`/`legal_target`'s CR 608.2b recheck were already generic over `quality`. No schema bump (`PlayerTargetingGrant.quality`/`PlayerSubjectSplitNotice` both pre-existing fields) and no dispatch-fingerprint change (same winning parser, different internals). `scripts/sweep_pool.py`'s `_leaves_you_and_group_player_half_unread` now actually tries `parse_clause_effect` on the player-half predicate rather than downgrading every "You and `<group>`" card unconditionally, so Veil reads `handled`. D2 exemption argued in `docs/engine-coverage.md`'s own T12 row: no other Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal printing in the local pool grants a player hexproof from a quality, so the mechanism is proven on Veil itself rather than a second printing.
- 2026-09-23: E1 power-up "order dependence" was not order dependence and not a cross-Game cache leak: `Game.exhausted` was the one model field still a bare `set[str]`, so its JSON order followed per-process `PYTHONHASHSEED` string hashing and the scenario round-trip invariant failed about 1 process in 14 once it held two keys (only `test_a_new_aerial_doombot_object_may_activate_again` did; "passes alone" was luck). Fixed by annotating it `SortedSet[str]` (`serialization.py`); also a real-game bug - a saved game could dump its exhaust/power-up keys in a different order after decode. Proof: `test_power_up_bookkeeping_dumps_in_one_canonical_order` (six entries, 1-in-720 false green).
- 2026-09-23: T4b "instead" pump alternatives. The bare `create_continuous_effect` sentinel re-reads the spell's whole oracle text, so a kicked/conditioned pump alternative (Colossal Growth, Might of Murasa, You're Not Alone) always applied the base amount. `_instead_branch_effect` in `machines/effects.py` now scopes a layer-sentence branch to its own text via `_create_continuous_effect_for_mode_text` (the modal-spell mechanism); `_anaphoric_target_substitution` also swaps a leading "it". No layers.py change was needed, no persisted field, no schema bump.
- 2026-09-23: Flow 3 integration (F3T7). CR 510.4's extra combat damage step is a second occurrence of `Step.COMBAT_DAMAGE` (`TurnMachine.add_second_combat_damage_step` re-queues the same step), not a distinct `Step` enum value - deliberately: the automatic path's ordinary step-entry machinery already gives CR 510.3's priority round its own turn between the two occurrences (with CR 704 checked in between), so a second `Step` would duplicate machinery for no rules-correctness gain; only the manual `Game.combat_damage()` helper skips that round, by design, for a scenario driving combat directly.
- 2026-09-23: A copy's own `CastRecord` (T3, `is_copy=True`, `origin_zone` forced to `Zone.STACK`, owner set to the copy's controller per CR 707.10) needed every `origin_zone`/`mana_spent`/`cast_record(` read in `game.py`/`effects.py` audited for a copy, not just the new field added: every "cast from a zone" reader is a positive check that already reads correctly (`Zone.STACK` matches no zone clause), and `mana_spent`/`spend_effects` readers already read empty since a copy never populates either (CR 707.2).
- 2026-09-23: `Game.copy_spell_targeting` is the one CR 707.10e reader every "copy a spell and specify/restrict its new target" card routes through (Frontline Heroism's pre-existing "you control" shape and T8's Ivy, Gleeful Spellthief "other than itself" shape both call it) - builds the copy only when the effect-specified target is legal for every instance of "target", rather than an unconditional `copy_spell` relying on the ordinary CR 608.2b recheck to bin an illegal one after the fact.
- 2026-09-23: CR 724.1's owed cleanup (T2). `Game.end_the_turn` jumps to `Step.CLEANUP` (CR 724.1d) but does not run its CR 514.1/514.2 turn-based actions - CR 724.1c is explicit that no player gets priority and no trigger reaches the stack during the process itself - so `Game.cleanup_owed` records the debt and `_before_priority` (every resolution's own final step, CR 608.2m) pays it off the next time anyone would receive priority, re-asking `step_grants_priority` with the debt cleared; CR 514.3a's own "another cleanup step" is the same machinery, generalized past just the owed case.
- 2026-09-23: `CardState.tap_if_untapped` (T5) is the effect-side tap verb - CR 701.26a is silent, not a refusal, on an already-tapped target, so every resolution-time tap in `effects.py` calls it instead of the strict `tap` (kept strict only for a cost, CR 118.3, and attack declaration, CR 508.1f). `untap_ability_targets`/`CardState.untap` were already CR 701.26b's silent twin; CR 122.1d's stun-counter replacement only spends a counter where there is a real "would become untapped" event, so an already-untapped target never loses one.
- 2026-09-23: CR 704.5r's "can't have more than N counters of a kind" cap is dropped by format rule, not left unbuilt by oversight: the only card that has ever printed it is Rasputin Dreamweaver (Ice Age), legal in none of standard, pioneer or modern and never reprinted - `docs/engine-coverage.md` records it as a deliberate non-implementation, the same policy that already governs every other mechanic with no legal printing to support.
- 2026-09-23: Frontline Heroism is not legal in standard, pioneer or modern - it stays in the fixture pool as the parser-shape fixture `judge/test_judge_copying.py` was already built around, but it is never the in-format legality proof. Ivy, Gleeful Spellthief (Modern/Pioneer-legal, added T8) is the real proof that CR 707.10e's specified-target copy is enforced on a card this engine must fully support.
- 2026-09-23: T1/G1a (design fork F1-A, approved): "look at the top N, put them back in any order" reuses `Game.look_at_top_and_split` with `moved_amount=0` rather than a second look-and-order primitive, so the whole looked-at set is always the "rest" half and CR 401.4's free arrangement is its own `top_order`. `look_at_top_and_split` grew an optional `asked_of` parameter (defaulting to the acting player) only because Architects of Will looks at and orders a library that belongs to a *different* player than the one who does the looking.
- 2026-09-23: T1/G1a: "you may have that player shuffle" (a second player choosing to shuffle a first player's library) was not built - no printing legal in Modern, Pioneer or Standard uses that shape (Natural Selection, which does, is not legal in any of the three and was dropped as T1's third proof for it). Only "you may shuffle" (the acting player shuffling their own just-looked-at library) exists in the reader.
- 2026-09-23: T4/G2a retired `_INSTEAD_CONDITIONS` - its two entries (Morbid's "a creature died this turn", Twinned Vision's "this spell wasn't cast from your hand") moved into `Game.condition_holds` itself, so an "instead if" reader and a plain "if" reader answer the same named condition the same way instead of one dict maintaining its own private copy that could drift from the general one.
- 2026-09-23: T9/G6's `GameEvent.zone_change_stamp` on a dies trigger is not only a schema-bump field - it is the fix for a real double-move bug: the old "exile it"/"return it" siblings moved *whatever card the graveyard held under that object's id* at resolution, so a card that died, left the graveyard, and came back as a genuinely new CR 400.7 object (still the same `id` in this engine's own "same id, new object" design) was wrongly moved by a trigger that should have found nothing. Stamping the incarnation at dies-emission time and checking it before every move closes this for the whole "put it .../exile it/return it" dies family at once, not per-card.
- 2026-09-23: T10/G7 replaced `_parse_optional_player_clause`'s hardcoded "you " retry - the fallback that silently attributed an orphaned bare-verb clause fragment to the resolving ability's own controller - with `_parse_shared_subject_clauses`' carried subject for any sentence that opens with target opponent/target player/each opponent/each player/you. The retry itself is untouched for a genuinely subject-less fragment; only the shared-subject-list case (Archon of Cruelty, Arbiter of Woe) now reaches the carried subject before the retry ever sees it, which is why Arbiter of Woe was silently wrong (the opponent's own discard/life-loss was charged to the controller) rather than merely silent.
- 2026-09-23: Four proof cards named in flow 1's own brief were dropped rather than added: Tegan Jovanka (T7, "target attacking historic creature") is not legal in Modern, Pioneer or Standard; Alabaster Dragon (T9, dead-object-to-library) likewise not legal in any of the three; Natural Selection (T1, "have that player shuffle") likewise not legal, and is also the one printing that would have exercised the not-built multi-player shuffle shape above; Grave Birthing (T6, exile-from-own-graveyard) is legal but carries its own separate, still-unread Scion-token clause, so it would have stayed `silent` overall even with T6's reader landed and proved nothing. Each task's own in-format substitute is recorded in its `docs/engine-coverage.md` row.
- 2026-09-23: D1 (owner decision, Bound // Determined's front face): option (a) - Bound's "sacrifice a creature, return up to X cards by the number of colors it was, exile this card" is implemented for real (T5b), not left as a notice (b) or dropped from scope (c). No off-pool proof exists for "the number of colors that `<X>` was" in any Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal printing besides Jared Carthalion (not legal there either), so the color-count reader is proven generally on `_is_referent_power_expression`'s own sibling shape instead - the same kind of proof exemption D2 already carries for Veil of Summer's player hexproof-from, and documented the same way in Bound's own `docs/engine-coverage.md` row.
- 2026-09-23: F2T18 closing audit. `--keywords` reports 0 gaps (`none - every keyword in the pool is read by some machine`). `--card` for every reported card in the flow was silent-free except one real defect found and fixed: Aang, Swift Savior's own back face, Aang and La, Ocean's Fury, has "Whenever Aang and La attack, put a +1/+1 counter on each tapped creature you control" - the trigger condition and Waterbend transform were both already read, but `_COUNTER_EACH_GROUP_RE`/`_parse_put_counters_on_group_clause` (`machines/effects.py`) had no reading for a "tapped"/"untapped" qualifier on the group noun, so the clause was silent. Added as an optional leading token, read off `CardState.tapped` (CR 110.5's status) directly rather than the computed board, since CR 110.5a says status is not a characteristic. Proven off-pool on Effie, Fast Learner (a real printing, not legal in Modern, Pioneer or Standard, added to the pool for this proof). Dispatch fingerprint regenerated. Two other silent clauses were found while confirming T19 landed - Evie Frye's own loot-then-reflexive-trigger ("When you discard a creature card this way, ...") and Jacob Frye's own combat-damage-exile-and-copy trigger - both on the Partner-with pair T19 added; the Partner with keyword itself is fully read (`machines/keywords.py`, confirmed `handled`) and both silent clauses are on the pair's *other*, unrelated printed abilities (a reflexive "this way" trigger and a CR 707.10e-adjacent copy-and-cast combo), neither a keyword nor an ability word this flow touches - left as a genuine, out-of-flow gap for a future task rather than built here. Schema bump decision (integration wave, applying `Game.CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`'s own bump rule second bullet - a name-/kind-keyed dispatch resolving differently on already-existing state): f1-t8's `e0b2e14` widened `_ATTACKS_OR_BLOCKS_RE`/self-reference-type-word matching in `machines/triggers.py` and changed how an already-pool card (Smuggler's Copter, "this Vehicle attacks or blocks") reads its own condition - bumped 52 -> 53, with a docstring entry on `Game.CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`. f3-t8's `8411e22` (CR 707.10e's Ivy, Gleeful Spellthief reader) is purely additive - Frontline Heroism's own existing "targets only a single creature you control" branch is untouched, byte-for-byte the same check, and the new "other than `<self>`" branch matches nothing any pre-existing pool card's condition text could already trigger - no bump. `docs/engine-coverage.md`'s "Not modelled" table has no stale full-gap row for any of flow 2's 24 in-scope keywords (Shroud, Split second, Endure, Recruit, Tiered, Spree, Waterbend, Explore, Hexproof/Hexproof from, Infect, Wither, Toxic, Cumulative upkeep, Changeling, Battle cry, Outlast, Monstrosity, Goad, Discover, Prowl, Miracle, Compleated, Power-up, Sneak); the mass-counter-placement row (line ~190) got a note for the tapped-state addition. T01-T17 and T19 each already carried their own dated MEMORY line before this task; this is T18's own. `mise run test`/`mise run lint` both green on the full suite (see verification).
- 2026-09-23: FRYE. Evie/Jacob Frye read generically: the reflexive discard reader now takes "a[n] <quality> card this way" (one ability per qualifying card, CR 603.12a); batched "one or more <description> deal combat damage to a player" is `ExtraTriggerEvent.COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYERS`, emitted per damaged player per combat damage step with `amount` = that step's tail of `combat_damage_to_players_this_turn` (matched at emit time, so the tail is exact - no new persisted field, no schema bump); "you may cast the copy" with no "without paying" is `cast_during_resolution(pays_mana_cost=True)`. Targeting "legendary or Rat card" used to AND the supertype and subtype; it is now an `alternatives` disjunction.
- 2026-09-23: F6 W1 `magic_cards_model.support.card_support(card) -> CardSupport` is the support oracle: the sweep's whole classifier (`classify_clauses`/`classify_card`/`executed_keywords`/`ignored_keywords`/`in_target_formats` and every helper) moved verbatim from `scripts/sweep_pool.py` into `src/magic_cards_model/support.py`; the script now only reports through `card_support` and re-exports the old names (`__all__`) for `survey_cards.py`/`known_gaps.py`/scenario tests. Proof of no drift: every mode (`default`, `--effects`, `--keywords`, `--targeting`, each with `--all-formats`) and `--card` for all 1600-odd pool names diffed byte-identical before/after. Non-obvious: the trigger-condition and "enters" probes build `CardState`s, which call `ids.new_id()` - inside a live game's `_id_allocator_scope()` that would have consumed the game's deterministic id sequence, so `card_support` installs a uuid4 allocator for the call (pinned by `test_classification_never_consumes_a_games_id_allocator`). Probe cards come from the package's own `data/printings` (built exactly like `tests/cards.pool`, duplicates and order included - changing that order changes verdicts). Not exported from `magic_cards_model/__init__` (convention there is `__version__` only).
- 2026-09-23: S01 enter-as-a-copy. No second legal printing carries a mana value bound on an enter-as-copy clause (Scryfall `o:"as a copy of" (o:"or less" or o:"less than or equal")` in Modern/Pioneer/Standard returns Mockingbird alone), so `_COPY_MANA_VALUE_BOUND_RE` is proven on Mockingbird only; its numeric spellings ("N or less", "less than or equal to N") were dropped in review as speculative - no printing carries them, so they fail closed. Echoing Deeps' "land card in a graveyard" is likewise unique; Vesuva proves the "enter tapped as a copy" half. Spark Double (candidate proof, "exception with counters") was not used: no listed card needs a counters/"isn't legendary" exception, and copying a planeswalker needs copiable loyalty, which `BecomeACopy` does not carry - adding it to the pool would have added a new fail-closed card to the sweep.
- 2026-09-23: S01 decline branch: Echoing Deeps (ruling 2023-11-10) and Vesuva (ruling 2021-03-19) both enter untapped when the copy is declined or there is nothing to copy - "tapped" is part of the copy replacement (CR 614.1c), so `_enters_as_copy` sets `event.tapped` only after a candidate is chosen. Once a copy applies, `ReplaceableEvent.entering_as_copy` switches `_self_enters_modifiers` to the copied object's own entering lines (CR 614.12) under `copy:` keys; the printed card's other self-enters lines are no longer offered.
- 2026-09-23: S01 sweep: `support._enters_as_copy_line_category` (was `sweep_pool.`, moved with the F6 W1 classifier) runs ahead of `missed_layer_shape` and asks `replacement.enters_as_copy_outcome` - a copy clause's "in addition to its other types"/"it has flying" tail is CR 707.9 copiable values, not a missed layer 4/6 effect. Side effect: Clever Impersonator had been credited handled while its "any nonland permanent" phrase did not read; it is now genuinely handled.
- 2026-09-23: S02 (nth-resolution branches). Proof cards: Vito, Fanatic of Aclazotz (multi-branch template) and Soulbright Seeker (targeted mana-adding branch, fizzle not counted); the "each <type> you don't control" damage widening has only two legal printings besides Omnath - Barrage of Boulders was tried and dropped (its Ferocious "creatures can't block this turn" has no reader in the engine), Artistic Process substituted, which needed the additive `_COLOUR_PAIR_AND_RE` mask in the shared `split_effect_clauses` for its "blue and red" token mode (sweep categories unchanged for every other card). Branch effects go through `_ability_effect_or_notice` but a notice stand-in is refused, so an unreadable branch stays silent rather than being hidden inside a handled whole. Printings verified via the Scryfall API (the spellslinger MCP was not available to this agent).
- 2026-09-23: S03 proof printings: Traumatize (half-library, rounding down), Reliquary Tower (static no maximum hand size), Ulamog's Crusher (fixed annihilator) and Cemetery Recruitment (the "If it's a <quality> card, <rider>" template) - all fetched from Scryfall with `add_card.py`, legality checked off the printing (the spellslinger MCP was not connected in that session). Cut Your Losses and It That Betrays were dropped: each carries a second silent clause (casualty; the sacrifice-steal trigger) that would have added a new silent card to the pool.
- 2026-09-23: S03 "no maximum hand size for the rest of the game" is a new persisted `Player.no_maximum_hand_size_for_rest_of_game` bool (schema 53 -> 54), not `maximum_hand_size: int | None`: the api repo's schema and serializer type `maximum_hand_size` as `int`. All three sources (emblem, permanent static, rest-of-game) are an any-of; CR 613.11's timestamp ordering against a "your maximum hand size is N" effect (Necrodominance) is not modelled - noted, not done.
- 2026-09-23: S03 Ulamog, the Defiler's "has annihilator X, where X is ..." is a layer 6 self-grant (`layers._SELF_HAS_COUNTED_TRIGGER_KEYWORD_RE`) of the keyword line, read back by the widened `keywords.granted_keyword_ability`, not a printed-line scan in `keyword_triggered_abilities`: keyed to the static's own line, a Turn to Frog'd Ulamog correctly has no annihilator. Only keywords `keywords.writes_triggered_ability` expands are granted this way. The new `_OBJECT_COUNTER_COUNT_RE` amount term made `deal_damage_equal_to_expression` pass the spell as `obj` (it was None) so an "it" term never silently reads 0.
- 2026-09-23: S04 (Grist's CR 113.6c "isn't on the battlefield" grant). The grant lives on `CardState.types`/`.subtypes`/P/T off the battlefield, NOT in `printed_characteristics`: that function doubles as CR 707.2's copiable values, which exclude a static ability's effect, so `Game.characteristics_of` off the battlefield now goes through `layers.off_battlefield_characteristics_of` instead. No second legal printing has the template (Scryfall `o:"isn't on the battlefield"` - only Grist and Falkenrath Gorger's madness grant), so per the D1 precedent the widened readers are proven on Essence Scatter, Negate, Duress, Thalia GoT, Soul-Scar Mage, Grisly Salvage, Raise Dead, Boneyard Wurm, The Darkness Crystal instead.

Several `effects.py` card filters moved from `state.card.types` to `state.types`; for every other card that is the same front-face answer, plus CR 406.3a (face-down exile has no types) and a split/adventure half on the stack.

- 2026-09-23: S14 granted suspend lives on `granted_ability_texts` as the bare "Suspend" line rather than a new `CardState` field, so no schema bump; `CardState.move_to` drops only that line on leaving exile (CR 400.7), since every other granted line is deliberately never cleared (evoke must survive stack-to-battlefield). `SuspendRedirectNotice` and `Game.suspend_redirect_notices` are retired but kept: removing a persisted field is itself a schema change.
- 2026-09-23: S14 widened two shared helpers additively: `_SPELL_QUALIFIER` accepts the serial-comma list ("A, B, or C") - also reaches "copy target ... spell" - and `_parse_referent_clause` gained a "loses N life" branch. `sweep --effects` before/after moved only the S14 cards and proof cards out of silent/notice.
- 2026-09-23: S15 built resolution-created "this turn" triggers as one generic `CreatedTriggeredAbility` (the created ability's printed-trigger text, re-read by `condition_matches`/`trigger_effect_for`) rather than one filter field per card; `DelayedTriggers.fire` gained an optional `game` for that. The existing per-shape readers (Dalkovan, Lyra, Mog, Last Ronin, High Tide) stay ahead of it in the chain; a mana-adding tap trigger is refused (CR 605.1b). The source's `Card` is stored on the record because a resolving spell is in no zone mid-resolution (fused Beck // Call's Birds enter then).
- 2026-09-23: S15 proof-card substitutions: Loan Shark does not carry "enters this turn" -> Rite of Harmony; no legal instant besides Plagiarize "skips that draw" -> Notion Thief (static twin, same reader); the "until end of turn, whenever" form -> Mistway Spy; Gather Specimens' only other printing -> Crafty Cutpurse (token-creation event); First Day's "counter on it and it gains haste" reader -> Wartime Protestors.
- 2026-09-23: S15 replaced `Game.draw_redirections`/`DrawRedirection` (a regex fitted to Plagiarize's sentence, and a second draw that looped on self-target) with `Game.created_replacements` (schema 57 -> 58). A redirected draw is now the modified event (CR 614.6): `Game.draw_cards` draws for `event.player`, which is what makes the two-Plagiarize and two-Notion-Thief rulings come out. `draw_step_draw` fixed to "first card drawn this draw step" (was: first card of any draw call during it). Prairie Dog's "you would put" reads `ReplaceableEvent.putter`: `Game.resolving`'s controller (CR 608.2c), else the recipient's controller (CR 122.6a for "enters with", and costs/turn-based actions). Not persisted - it lives only on the transient event.
- 2026-09-23: S15 changed shared code additively: `ReplaceableEvent.controller` (token creation's would-be controller; `_extra_token_applies` reads `creating_controller`), `ApplicableEffect.controls_entry` (CR 616.1b sort), `_compile_counter_doubler` now wraps `read_counter_amount`, `CountersAndKeywordsEventSubject` body moved to `put_counters_and_grant_keywords_to_event_subject`. `test_card_support`'s split-card example and the Gather Specimens notice test were updated: no split card in the pool has a dead half any more.

- 2026-09-23: S16 proof printings (the spellslinger MCP was not connected; legality and oracle text checked against the Scryfall API, the source `add_card.py` writes): Vision of Love for "you may discard a card or sacrifice `<x>`" (Polygraph Orb and Zoyowa Lava-Tongue print the choice too but carry other silent clauses); Shadow of the Goblin for the mandatory "Discard a card. If you do, ..."; Cruel Grimnarch and Refurbished Familiar for "who can't" (Liliana's Triumph is a different template); Teysa, Opulent Oligarch for "investigate for each" - "for each card discarded this way" was already read by `_for_each_multiplier`, so the widening was investigate's own tail; Wand of Vertebrae for the graveyard shuffle; Kaito Shizuki (the only other "discard ... unless `<board fact>`" printing - his end-step phasing clause stays silent for the phasing task) and Gathering Place for "this land entered this turn" (Mirrex was dropped: its Mite-token ability is silent).
- 2026-09-23: S16 decisions. Discard-or-sacrifice is one MAY plus, only when both halves are payable, a `CHOOSE_A_QUALITY` naming the half (no new `DecisionKind`, so the api's answer mapping is untouched); an unscripted player accepts and pays the first printed half, the engine-wide MAY default. "Who can't" is a member who discarded nothing (CR 118.12's "can't" = never started to pay), not "fewer than N". No schema bump: "you attacked this turn" is a new `turn_facts["attacked"]` entry (existing field), recorded in `declare_attackers`.
- 2026-09-23: S16 shared helpers changed additively: `cost._PHRASE_EXILE` reads "exile this `<permanent type>`" from the battlefield; `Game.condition_holds`/`condition_is_readable` gained self-entered-this-turn, you-attacked-this-turn and "`<a>` or if `<b>`" (Gathering Place's cycle had its colored ability always refused as unread); `_MAY_DISCARD_N_CARDS_RE`'s "you may" is optional; `_INVESTIGATE_RE` takes a "for each" tail; `parse_clause_effect` tries `_parse_discard_unless_condition_clause` first; the group-discard pair reader got a third consequence (`_who_cant_discard_consequence`).
- 2026-09-23: S17 proof printings (Scryfall search; card_search MCP not reachable here): multi-token list on Bestial Menace + Trostani's Summoner, target-player-creates on Ashling's Command + Verdant Command, "It has" grant on Spawning Breath + Blisterpod. The brief's Awakening Zone candidate was not used (its "you may" upkeep trigger adds nothing the dies/spell proofs do not). The "It has "<ability>"" sentence is folded into "token with "<ability>"" (CR 608.2c) rather than given its own reader, so every existing token reader gets it.
- 2026-09-23: S17 widened shared `machines/targeting._spell_text` additively: a colon only inside a quoted grant no longer marks the line as an activated ability. Found via Spawning Breath, whose "any target" was dropped because its token's "Sacrifice this token: Add {C}." sits on the same line; only Kozilek's Command (modal, unaffected) and Spawning Breath in the pool carry that shape.
- 2026-09-23: Fail-closed test vehicles (unread spell, unbuildable activation cost) are now chosen at test time from out-of-format pool printings (ante/subgame spells, Moniker Mage's "Say your middle name"), never a named in-format card: the owner rule makes every in-format example the next thing the campaign fixes (Ponder, Strix Serenade, Relic of Progenitus each broke a test that way).
- 2026-09-23: S07 proof printings (Scryfall search; card_search/resolve_cards MCP not reachable here, legality read off each fetched printing): Spellskite and Ricochet Trap as the brief proposed. No other legal printing carries "pay N life or pay {M}" or a power-N cost reduction; Terrific Team-Up (mana-value twin) was dropped because its spell body is unread and would have entered the pool silent, so the widened `_CONDITION_POWER` is proven on Baxter Building's toughness activation condition instead and reads power/toughness only.
- 2026-09-23: S07 changed shared readers additively: `targeting._read_phrase` reads "target spell or ability" as spell-or-ability (it was "target spell" alone, so Bolt Bend/Swat/Spellskite could never target an ability); `_CONDITION_POWER` takes any `_permanent_quality` noun and toughness; `optional_additional_costs` also lists a life-or-mana sentence (keyword `PAY_LIFE_KEYWORD` = "pay life", rule CR 601.2b) - a handle rather than None because a keyword-less option has nothing a seat can name back; the api's `_announce_optional_costs` maps `Pay life` onto `Casting.paying_life_for_additional_cost`; `_resolution_cast_option_is_legal` treats an unreadable "or pay {M}" sentence as payable by its mana half.
- 2026-09-23: S07 `Game.change_targets` is separate from `offer_new_targets_for_copy` (not edited): it places each existing target on its requirement slot and judges a replacement against that slot only, since `TargetingProfile.check`'s greedy fill passes a two-slot swap that is really illegal (Prey Upon's "you control"/"you don't control"). No BECOMES_TARGET event is raised on a change - noted, not done.
- 2026-09-23: S18 proof-card substitutions (Scryfall search; card_search/resolve_cards MCP not reachable here): Dawnglare Invoker + Subjugator Angel for tap-all (Sleep's second sentence is a different reader), Honest Work + Charmed Sleep + Blitz Leech for tap-attached/remove-all (Vampire Hexmage already read, targets rather than an anaphor), Ludevic's Test Subject for the counter threshold, Gleam of Battle for the attack "on it". Petrifying Meddler/Slumbering Trudge not used: stun counters were already proven, and no second printing of Suppression Ray's energy-chosen-stun sentence exists.
- 2026-09-23: S18 shared helpers changed additively: `_referent_reader` gained an attached-object shape tried only when none of its three shapes match; `_goad_group_member` reused read-only for tap-all's controller words. `support.classify_clauses` now passes `legendary=` to `activated_effect_for` as `Game` does - without it the sweep scored nonlegendary Treasure Map a notice ("Treasure" read as a short self-name). Trigger call sites were left alone (noted, not doing now: they also omit `legendary=`).
- 2026-09-23: S18 schema 58 -> 59 for `CardState.last_known_attached_to`: leaving the battlefield unattaches (CR 400.7), so without the stamp an Aura removed in response to its own enters trigger (River's Grasp) left the trigger nothing to act on. `test_sweep_pool`'s worst-mode example is now chosen from the pool (any modal card with one mode read and one not) since Cryptic Command became fully read.
- 2026-09-23: S19 proof printings (Scryfall search; card_search MCP not reachable here): Justice, Vance Astrovik for the returned-to-hand reader and Wizard Class for "becomes level N". The brief's Innkeeper's Talent carries no "becomes level" trigger, so it could not prove the class reader. Stormfront Riders was tried and dropped: its own ETB return is silent, so its "this creature or another" subject is left unread rather than shipped untested.
- 2026-09-23: S19 shared helpers changed additively: `Game.return_to_hand` defers its departure events inside the new `Game.returning_together` block (wrapped around the target, mass, chosen-land return effects and the RETURN_TO_HAND cost), stamps look-back types and pre-move controller, and its `LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD_TO_HAND` event's `player` is now the owner (whose hand); `cost._why_not_return_to_hand` skips the ninjutsu unblocked check for a component with a requirement; `legal_actions` offers controlled permanents for that component. No schema bump: only an `ExtraTriggerEvent` member was added.
- 2026-09-23: Tameshi's "Return a land you control to its owner's hand" cost was unbuildable (ValueError) despite the sweep reporting the activated clause handled - the sweep does not build activation costs. Built in S19 so the task's own-cost trigger is playable.
- 2026-09-23: S20 proof printings (Scryfall search; card_search MCP not reachable here): Mystical Teachings proves "a card with flash". No printing legal in Modern/Pioneer/Standard other than Moonsilver Key says "with a mana ability" (Scryfall o:"with a mana ability", 2026-09-23), so `has_mana_ability` is proven across eleven artifacts (sacrifice-cost, tap-a-creature-cost, conditional-output, artifact land, triggered CR 605.1b Caged Sun, library-moving Chromatic Sphere, granted-quoted Paradise Mantle) instead of a second search card.
- 2026-09-23: S20 "with <keyword>" is gated on CR 702's own headings (`abilities.KEYWORD_ABILITY_NAMES`, from the 2026-09-25 CR) inside the regex itself, not a permissive "with .+" plus a readability check: a permissive group would pull clauses owned by other readers ("with different names", "with power ...") into `_SEARCH_AND_PUT_RE` and turn them into notices. Shared `_SEARCH_AND_PUT_RE`/`_SEARCH_DESCRIPTION_RE`/`_quality_description` changed additively (one optional `having` group/param). Noted, not done: CR 605.1a's library criterion is applied only by `has_mana_ability`; whether activation treats Chromatic Sphere's ability as a mana ability is untouched.
- 2026-09-23: S21 proof printings (Scryfall search and /cards/named for oracle text and legality; resolve_cards/card_search MCP not reachable in this session): named modes on School Daze + You Find the Villains' Lair (Choose Your Weapon rejected: its other mode "Double target creature's power and toughness" is unread); mass lose-all on Sudden Spoiling (the only legal non-static one; "lose all abilities until end of turn" has no second printing); choose-then-anaphor on Crush Underfoot (the only other legal "Choose a <x> you control. It ..." printing); plural graveyard exile on The Death of Gwen Stacy (the only other printing). Gwen Stacy's chapter II is silent and was not built (no second printing of that punisher exists).
- 2026-09-23: S21 moved flavor-word stripping for modes into `modes.mode_effect_text` so targeting (`mode_rules_text`) and resolution see the same instruction; before, only `effects.mode_effect_body` (triggered modes) and the select-from-top regex stepped over the label, which is why Look Around read and Bring Down did not.
- 2026-09-23: S21 shared helpers changed additively: `split_effect_clauses` masks "lose(s) all abilities and have/has base power and toughness N/N" (it cut Sudden Spoiling into three clauses); `_parse_referent_object_clause` gained the "deals damage equal to its power to <target>" rider; two new pair/clause readers are appended to the existing dispatch chains, not reordered.
- 2026-09-23: Legal-notice sweep-test vehicle is chosen at test time (`_a_legal_notice_card`), and `test_card_support`'s notice example is Bank Job (out of format), after Final Showdown stopped failing closed.

No second legal printing exists for Prized Amalgam's self graveyard return, Lightning Coils' "remove all of them ... that many" or Nahiri's "attacks a player each combat".
- 2026-09-23: S22 `_CREATE_TOKEN_COPIES_EXCEPT_RE` never matched singular "that's a copy" (it required a space before "'s"); fixed, which is what lit Kiki-Jiki/Reflection. `parse_mixed_effect` had read "Exile target ... card from your graveyard. Create a token that's a copy of it." as a copy of the *source* (Nahiri copying herself); `_parse_exile_card_then_token_copy_clause` now runs ahead of it.
- 2026-09-23: S22 shared changes kept additive: `targeting._read_adjectives` reads a serial-comma adjective list ("red, white, or black"); `Game.enter_the_battlefield` records the entry zone in a private, non-persisted `_entry_origins` that `_announce_entry` puts on the enters event's existing `origin_zone`; `combat._ATTACKS_EACH_COMBAT_RE` takes "a player". The put-then-delayed composer runs *after* `parse_mixed_effect`, so every text that already parsed keeps its reader.
- 2026-09-23: S22 block requirement (Sisters of Stone Death's "{G}: Target creature blocks ~ this turn if able", proof Tangle Angler) is a granted "This creature blocks <source name> each combat if able." line, matched to attackers by name like the attack-side grant - no persisted field, no schema bump. Every other legal "Put a creature card exiled with ~" printing (Yggdrasil, Profane Procession, Sothera) also carries an unread clause, so swapping the proof card was not an option. CR 509.1c max is found by moving one holder at a time; a menace attacker needing a second, requirement-free blocker is not caught.
- 2026-09-23: S23 proof printings (Scryfall search; card_search/resolve_cards MCP not reachable here): chosen-colour anthem on Heraldic Banner + Hall of Triumph, protection from the chosen colour on Order of the Stars (Pentarch Ward carries it only as an Aura grant), two-subtype lord on Warg Rider, legendary/nontoken qualifiers on Arvad the Cursed + Always Watching, per-object "for each other ... creature you control" count on Heroes' Podium. - 2026-09-23: S23 Voice of All's protection is a layer 6 grant built from `CardState.choice`, not a special case in `Game._effective_protection_qualities`, so ability removal (CR 613.1f) takes it away with the static. The printed "protection from the chosen color" phrase still yields the quality "the chosen color" from `protection_qualities`, which matches no source and is harmless.
- 2026-09-23: S24 proof printings (card_search MCP not reachable; Scryfall search API, legality read off the returned printing): Koth, Fire of Resistance and Omnath, Locus of the Roil for "deals damage to `<target>` equal to the number of `<subtype>`s" (Calamitous Cave-In and Rockalanche are already fixtures but carry different templates - mass X damage, earthbend - so not used); Hold the Gates for "+N/+M for each `<subtype>` you control" (the only other legal "for each Desert" card, Desert's Due, is a one-shot -1/-1 rider, not the static template); Earthen Ally for "for each color among `<group>` you control" (Chromatic Orrery is an artifact draw, not a pump); Villainous Wrath for the target-player "they control" amount (no other legal printing reads "the greatest power among creatures they control" as an amount); Tromp the Domains for the until-EOT team pump "for each"; Dungrove Elder, Aquitect's Will, Melek Reforged Researcher, Mindsplice Apparatus, Armorcraft Judge for Eluge's three abilities. Nissa's "X is Nissa's loyalty" has no second printing anywhere in Modern/Pioneer/Standard (searched "(X is|equal to) (its|<name>'s|this planeswalker's) loyalty").
- 2026-09-23: S24 shared helpers changed additively: `effects.parse_mode_effect` no longer substitutes the announced X into a mode that defines its own X (CR 107.3c) - it turned Inscription of Abundance's "gains X life, where X is ..." into "gains 0 life, where 0 is ..."; `triggers._expand_enters_or_attacks` takes the card name so "Whenever Eluge enters or attacks" splits like "this creature" (the attack half was silently dropped); `_QUALIFIER_YOU_CONTROL_RE` gained "with a `<kind>` counter on it"; `_COUNT_BASIC_LAND_TYPES_RE` reads the singular "for each basic land type"; `layers._singular_subtype_word` reads "-ies" plurals ("Allies").
- 2026-09-23: S24 "for as long as it has a `<kind>` counter on it" is `layers.HasCounter`, making `layers.Condition` a discriminated union (new `kind` member only, no new persisted field, so no schema bump). CR 611.2b says the duration never restarts, but a counter can come back, so `ContinuousEffects.active(game)` drops a for-as-long-as effect whose condition `can_restart` (only `HasCounter`) the moment it is false; `RemainsOnBattlefield` effects still wait for `end_the_turn`'s sweep (a test pins that).
- 2026-09-23: S24 dead-end avoided: the new counted life reader (`_parse_counted_life_change_clause`) first caught Momentous Fall's "you gain life equal to its toughness" and would have read the spell's own toughness; it now refuses anaphoric "its"/"that X's" terms so that clause stays with the referent readers (dispatch fingerprint unchanged for it). Noted, not done: Boneyard Wurm's `LAYER_EFFECTS` row could move onto the new `_GRAVEYARD_CARD_COUNT_CDA_RE`.
- 2026-09-23: S35 Kaito, Bane of Nightmares' conditional self type change read by widening `layers._conditional_self_type_change` (no new persisted field, no schema bump). Proof printing Idol of False Gods (Modern); Gideon Blackblade and Grand Master of Flowers share the template but were not added to the pool - each has other silent loyalty lines outside this task. Kaito on his own turn is a creature and not a planeswalker (CR 205.1a, his ruling), so ninjutsu now puts him in attacking (CR 702.49c) - the old test_ninjutsu assertion was corrected; CR 606.6's check counts loyalty counters, not `CardState.loyalty` (None while he is not a planeswalker).
- 2026-09-23: S25 proof printings: Anger of the Gods does not carry a qualifier ("each creature" plus an exile rider), so the mass-damage qualifier proofs are Sagittars' Volley (keyword + controller) and Fiery Cannonade (non-Subtype). Flame Sweep is the only legal "each creature except for" printing (Scryfall 2026-09-23); Ajani Unrelenting (legal nowhere) is the second, and its old card-specific "except for tokens you control" reader was removed in favour of the general `_parse_damage_group_phrase`. Dusk // Dawn and Solar Tide were rejected as the power-threshold proof (Dawn's aftermath and Solar Tide's entwine are unread) in favour of The Battle of Bywater.
- 2026-09-23: S25 shared helpers changed additively: `_parse_damage_each_filtered_clause`'s last fallback now reads `_parse_damage_group_phrase` (controller-aware; the old `_parse_damage_group_filter` grammar is its base case); `_parse_mass_destroy_predicate` gained a power/toughness comparison; `_parse_token_clause` reads a trailing "for each `<count>`" (The Battle of Bywater's Food) through `count_expression`. Screaming Nemesis's lock reuses the existing persisted `Player.cannot_gain_life` - no new field, no schema bump. `_parse_damage_that_much_clause` refuses an event-relative target ("that player controls", "they"): it first claimed Questing Beast's "target planeswalker that player controls", which the target reader would widen to every planeswalker.

- 2026-09-23: Behavioural pool sweep (`tests/test_behavioural_sweep.py`, `mise run test:behaviour`, in `test:full` not `test`): every handled trigger/activation of every in-format pool card is executed, not parsed - 818 pass, 3 xfail(strict) cost gaps (Illuminated Folio, Tameshi, Filigree Sylex), ~280s. Catches the Devourer regression (fails at 51b1edd, passes at 838d1b0) via the adversarial opposing board plus "removed for no legal target while `rejects` accepts a candidate". It found 11 real gaps on main, fixed test-first (Adventure pile, Darkness Crystal link, attacks-you, batched attack count, four restriction/intervening-if shapes, Relic's self-exile cost, off-zone exile cost, outside-the-game wish). Dead ends: a busy fallback alone rescued Devourer via alice's own coloured permanents (hence the no-legal-target check); `start_at` untaps staged tapped material, so it is tapped after.
- 2026-09-24 GREEN-MAIN (owner rule: main holds only stable, fully tested, green code; only that deploys): merge gate is `lint` + `test:full` green here and the api's `lint`/`test`/`test:pg` green against it. test:full was red on main with 13 sweep cases no driver could raise; they are strict-xfail in `SWEEP_DRIVER_GAPS` keyed by event kind + precondition (BEH2's drivers flip them). Named legal "gap example" cards (Gavony Dawnguard, Alhammarret's Archive, Bloodghast, Obsidian Charmaw) replaced by `tests.cards.gap_example`, out-of-format first.

The "base power and toughness X/X" proof is Mirror Entity (group subject, "gain all creature types"), since no other legal card prints Minsc's single-target "becomes a <type> in addition" rider; the quoted-trigger proof is Dreadmaw's Ire.

- 2026-09-23: S30 The Ring is a real emblem (CR 701.54c) whose text is the rule's own ability sentences for the current count, so layers/triggers/effects read it like a card. The emblem's `Card` is rebuilt (`game._emblem_card`) rather than edited: `abilities.parse_abilities` memoises on `Card.id`, and an edited copy kept the old id and the old abilities (dead-end hit). Count and designation live on `Player` (schema 62); the layer engine gets the designation through `LayerContext.ring_bearers` because `Game.ring_bearer_of` asks control, which is the board being computed.
- 2026-09-23: S30 shared helpers changed additively: `Game.create_emblem` gained `name=`; `ContinuousEffects._note_control_change` also calls `Game.drop_ring_bearers_controlled_by_others` (CR 701.54a's control-change end); `combat.evasion_restriction_refusal` reads "can't be blocked by creatures with greater power"; new `layers.AddSupertype` and `delayed.SacrificedByItsController` union members. Noted, not done: Nazgul's deck-size line stays static_unaudited.
- 2026-09-23: S31 phasing stores a phased-out permanent in `Game.phased_out` (out of `Game.battlefield`, `zone` left at BATTLEFIELD per CR 702.26d) rather than a flag every battlefield reader would have to skip: all ~140 `game.battlefield` readers and `find_by_id` get CR 702.26b's "does not exist" for free. Only holders of a direct object reference need a check; targeting (`TargetingProfile.check`) and `combat.attack_refusal`/`block_refusal` got additive phased-out refusals (targeting.py and combat.py are shared with other round-4 tasks). Schema 62 -> 63.

If you do, ..." printing, so it proves the optional-transform reader, and its payoff forced three subsystems into this task: "that player controls" bound to the trigger event's player (`ControllerRestriction.EVENT_PLAYER`), an attach-self-to-target reader, and Ghastly Haunting's static "You control enchanted creature" (layer 2 from the battlefield sweep, so `ContinuousEffects.has_control_effect` now also asks `static_control_change_in_play`). - 2026-09-23: S33 CR 701.27f is a count, not a face: `AbilityOnStack.source_times_transformed` (schema 63 -> 64) because a permanent turned over twice in response is back on the same face yet has transformed. "that player controls" stays unbound (as permissive as before) when a "target player/opponent" precedes it in the text, since "that player" is then the target, not the event's player.
- 2026-09-24: S33 review: CR 103.8a skip now skips the whole first draw step in `Game.advance` (CR 500.11), not just the draw - several tests had walked alice's turn-1 draw step and were rewritten; `skips_draw` also requires `resume_index is None` so an extra beginning phase's draw step (CR 614.10a) is not skipped. Also renamed S33's duplicate `effects._as_sentence` (it shadowed the original for every caller) to `_as_capitalized_sentence`.
- 2026-09-23: S33 found Avatar Aang's own draw trigger being read as earthbend's "target land you control" (the bare verb in its condition matched `targeting._EARTHBEND`), so with no land it was removed from the stack; `_EARTHBEND` now needs the instruction's N/X. Earthbend (CR 701.66b) and firebend (CR 702.189b) now raise their events too, so all four bends are real for Aang.

- 2026-09-24: BEH2 goldens: `tests/golden/<card>/<triggered|activated>-<n>.json` per executed sweep case (board as `Scenario().player(...)` args + recipe + action + normalised delta), compared in the sweep, `mise run golden:update` / `golden:replay`. Format decision: engine-native JSON over the harness DSL - `snapshot.py` is a board cache not a format, and the api play-script DSL is a seat's conditional plan with no board setup (and engine tests must not depend on the api repo). Dead end: seeding the board id allocator by case+variant alone made a cost's `source_id` from one board name a card on another (Kalitas lost its sacrifice fodder) - seed includes the board contents. Also fixed: "play a land or cast a spell from anywhere other than your hand" (Shadow of the Goblin, Vega); the last 13 sweep cases now execute via generic drivers.

- 2026-09-24: C00-T2 found Soulless Jailer's "Permanent cards in graveyards can't enter the battlefield" was silently inert: `replacement._CARD_TYPE_WORDS` has no "permanent" entry, so `_cant_enter_replacements` read `wanted is None` and skipped building the replacement outright - a genuine pre-existing engine bug, not something this task's own change introduced. Fixed by giving `_cant_enter_applies`/`_cant_enter_replacements` the same "permanent = no type filter, non`<type>` = excluded type" convention `_copy_candidates` already used for Clever Impersonator; proven with Kunoros, Hound of Athreos and Weathered Runestone.
- 2026-09-24: C00-T2 built the per-turn draw cap (Narset, Parter of Veils / Spirit of the Labyrinth) as a check inside `Game.draw_cards`'s own CR 121.2 per-individual-draw loop, mirroring `casting_restrictions.blanket_cant_cast_restriction`'s spell-count cap but counted off `Game.cards_drawn_this_turn`; and the search prohibition (Ashiok, Dream Render) as a new `caused_by` parameter on `Game.search_library`/`search_and_put`, checked only for a *self*-search (the causing spell/ability's own controller searching their own library) since that is the only shape the printed grammar ("their controller"/"their library") reaches.
- 2026-09-24: C00-T2 scope boundary, not a card-name hack: `caused_by` is wired into the dominant `_search_and_place_for`/`_build_search_clause` "search your library for ..., put it ..., then shuffle" family only (fetchlands, ordinary tutors - e.g. Diabolic Tutor, the proof card) - not into the anaphoric "its controller may search" shape (Ghost Quarter) or the top-of-library "reveal it, then shuffle and put that card on top" family (Mystical Tutor). Neither is needed by any card this task lists; both keep `caused_by=None` (unchanged prior behaviour) rather than guessing a controller.
- 2026-09-24: C00-T2 could not verify a TDD red run for the replacement.py fix by reverting via `git stash`: this worktree shares one `refs/stash` stack with every other active worktree of the same repo, and a `git stash push`/inspection here surfaced unrelated, actively-changing entries from other concurrent agents (layers.py/targeting.py content neither pushed nor expected by this task). Verified the bug by direct code reading instead (`wanted is None or zones is None: continue` with no "permanent" key in `_CARD_TYPE_WORDS`) and confirmed the draw-cap and search-prohibition fixes red/green by toggling the new code paths off with `Edit` (no git operations) rather than stashing. Future tasks in this campaign should avoid `git stash` inside a task worktree entirely.

- 2026-09-24: C00-T6 two real bugs in bestow's own resolution path, found proving Detective's Phoenix's graveyard permission and fixed as part of this task (both cited in `docs/engine-coverage.md`'s new section, not new templates): `CardState.move_to`'s CR 702.103b carve-out excused only a STACK->BATTLEFIELD move, so `Game._put_on_stack`'s ordinary re-statement of a spell already on the stack cleared `bestowed` before the cost was even paid - no bestow spell (hardcast or from a permission) could ever resolve as an Aura. Detective's Phoenix's own bestow path (the template this task owns) is unaffected and fully proven; a future task tightening `Casting`'s zone-legality check would need to touch every `KeywordCastOption` consumer, not just bestow's.

- 2026-09-24: C01-T2 two genuine, general engine bugs found and fixed while proving these templates, not new templates of their own: (1) `machines/targeting.py`'s bare-subtype-word elision ("target Soldier you control" standing in for CR 205.3m's elided "creature") defaulted `card_types` to `Type.CREATURE` for *every* bare subtype with no following noun, including non-creature subtypes like Equipment (CR 205.3g's artifact subtype) - "target Equipment you control" made every Equipment an illegal target for its own attach clause. Fixed generically (`data/subtypes.noncreature_subtype_card_type`, keyed off `subtypes.json`'s own category, elides the *correct* card type - artifact/enchantment/land/battle/planeswalker - never creature, for any subtype in that vocabulary), not special-cased to "Equipment". (2) A reflexive triggered ability (CR 603.12) whose own printed text opens with "When you do, ..." had its target thrown away by `Game._ask_trigger_targets`, because that path read `TriggeredAbility.text` (the whole, unsplit sentence) rather than `.effect`, and `machines/targeting.profile_for_text`'s own `_REFLEXIVE_CLAUSE_TAIL` cut - correctly built to keep an *outer* ability's announcement from claiming a target that belongs to a reflexive ability not yet created - had nothing left in front of it to leave behind when the text *is* that reflexive ability's own. Fixed by reading the reflexive's own consequence text (`reflexive_consequence_text`) only when the ability's own text opens with the marker, never for an outer ability that merely contains one embedded further in. Dain Ironfoot's "When you do, attach it to target creature you control." and Thorin's "When one or more Equipment become attached to that creature this way, ..." both needed this; `machines/delayed.DelayedTrigger.subject_id` (already a field, previously unused by any reflexive `DelayedEffectSpec`) carries which object ("it"/"that creature") the reflexive names, set by `Game.create_reflexive_trigger`'s new optional `subject_id` kwarg.
- 2026-09-24: C01-T2 shared code changed additively: `machines/effects.amass` now returns the `CardState` Army it acted on (`None` if CR 701.47b's process found none) - the caller Goblin Plate Mail's own "amass Goblins 1, then attach this Equipment to the amassed Army" needs it; every existing caller ignores the return value, unchanged behaviour. `machines/cost.additional_cost_phrases`/`alternative_cost_phrases` gained an optional `face` kwarg (default `None`, old behaviour) reading a split/Adventure/modal-DFC printing's own chosen `CardFace.oracle_text` instead of `Card.oracle_text`, which Scryfall leaves null for all three layouts - without it, an Adventure spell's own "As an additional cost to cast this spell, ..." sentence (Allure of Power's sacrifice) was never read at all; threaded through `machines/casting.mandatory_additional_costs`'s own new `face` kwarg and `Casting`'s two internal callers. `machines/layers._split_and_validate_keywords` gained a `can't be blocked[ by `<quality>`]` item (CR 509.1a) granted as the full self-referential sentence `machines/combat.py`'s own evasion-restriction reader already requires, not the bare phrase every other list item grants - the one item shape that reading side needs a subject in front of, unlike every CR 702 keyword. `machines/layers.compute_board` gained `_hone_counter_effects` (CR 122.1j, sublayer 7c) beside the existing `_counter_effects`, reading `CardState.attached_to` (not itself a computed characteristic) since a hone counter's own bonus lands on a *different* permanent than the one carrying the counter.

If you discard a land card this way, put it onto the battlefield tapped" (Silvan Reveler); "look at the top N, put any number of land cards onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle" as one comma-joined sentence with a literal count (Through the Forest Gate) - Famished Worldsire prints the closest relative but counts its own top N off a self-defined X ("where X is this creature's power") rather than a literal number, a widening out of this task's scope; "Put a card exiled with this Saga into its owner's hand" (Roads Go Ever, Ever On chapters II/III) - The Mystical Archive and Wall of Mourning print the identical sentence with a different object type but neither is legal in any target format.
- 2026-09-24: C01-T8 two reader-internal bugs found and fixed while proving generality, neither a new template: (1) `game.search_library`'s own returned cards leave the source zone's list but keep a stale `.zone` field (its own docstring: "the found cards aren't included in the shuffle" - the caller places them); `_parse_search_split_destination_clause`'s own placement helper originally gated on `state.zone is Zone.LIBRARY` before moving a found card, which is never true after `search_library` already removed it, so a split destination's "the other" card silently stayed nowhere. Fixed by dropping the gate and using `controller.add`/`put_into_graveyard`/`put_onto_battlefield` directly, the same way `Game.search_and_put`'s own identical loop already does. (2) Exile is a shared zone (CR 400.1); `_put_card_exiled_with_it_into_owners_hand_effect` first tried `player.move(chosen, Zone.HAND)`, which `Player.move` refuses for any shared-zone source - fixed with `game.exile.remove(chosen)` then `player.add(chosen, Zone.HAND)`, the two-step move `_parse_cast_from_hand_then_exile_clause`'s own Possibility Storm bottoming already uses to leave exile for a player zone.

Fixed by stripping the trailing period before the tail is read - Kozilek's Command itself unaffected (its own tail sentence never ends the whole card's text).
- 2026-09-24: C02-T2 second real, pre-existing bug: `effects._TARGET_PHRASE_ANYWHERE_RE` (the "instead"-replacement anaphor resolver, `_anaphoric_target_substitution`) stopped its target-phrase scan only at a fixed verb list, which did not include "unless" - so a counterspell's own "target spell unless its controller pays..." could never resolve its own short noun for a trailing "it deals more instead" / anaphoric rider to read. Widened the lookahead to also stop at "unless". We Say Thee Nay! is the reported card; no second printing needed since the fix is to the shared boundary regex itself, exercised on the identical shape by the same card's own two variants (with and without teamwork).

- 2026-09-24: C02-T7 real, pre-existing engine bug found and fixed (not a new template): The Vision's own "choose one that hasn't been chosen **this turn** -" was reading `CardState.chosen_modal_options`, the *permanent*, never-reset memory built for Gollum, Riddle Master's unqualified "hasn't been chosen -". A used mode would therefore never come back on a later turn, over-restricting Vision forever after its first three castings that game. Fixed with a second, sibling field, `CardState.chosen_modal_options_this_turn`, populated only when the instruction itself says "this turn" and cleared every `Game.cleanup_step` (CR 514.2's own "'this turn' effects end"); `Game._announce_trigger_modes` and `effects._resolve_modal_triggered_ability_clause`'s own no-mode-recorded fallback both branch on the same "this turn" substring check. Gollum's own field and behaviour are untouched. Schema 73 -> 74.
- 2026-09-24: C02-T7 second reader-internal bug found while proving The Vision's own "Solar Beam" bullet ("`<Name>` gains double strike until end of turn."): `_self_normalized_effect_text` already rewrites a legendary card's own printed name to "this permanent" (CR 201.5c) before a modal bullet's fallback re-parse, but `effects._SELF_KEYWORD_GRANT_UNTIL_EOT_RE` only ever accepted the bare pronoun "it" as the grant's subject, and the reader built on it (`_parse_self_keyword_grant_until_eot_clause`) was wired only into `_ability_effect_or_notice` (a whole-ability-text dispatcher), never into `_parse_mixed_clause` (the per-clause dispatcher `parse_mixed_effect`, and so every modal bullet, actually calls). Widened the regex to accept "this permanent" too and wired the reader into `_parse_mixed_clause` - generalizes to every other card sharing either gap, not just Vision.
- 2026-09-24: C02-T7 third reader-internal bug found while proving Cloak and Dagger, Entwined's own delayed return: `_watch_for_departure_and_return_exiled_card`/`delayed.StaticPrisonReturn` (the shared CR 610.2/610.3 "return when the exiler leaves" machinery, previously built only against Static Prison/Lagrella-style effects that always exile a *permanent*) unconditionally returned the exiled card to the battlefield - correct by CR 610.3c's own default for those, but CR 610.3's actual rule is "returns the object to its **previous zone**", and Cloak and Dagger can exile a card from a player's **hand** instead ("exile a nonland card from their hand or the chosen creature"). Widened `StaticPrisonReturn` with a `return_zone: Zone` field (default `Zone.BATTLEFIELD`, so every existing caller is unchanged) and the watcher function with a keyword-only `return_zone=` parameter, set from the exiled object's own zone just before the move at Cloak and Dagger's own call site. Schema 73 -> 74 (same bump as the modal fix above).
- 2026-09-24: C02-T7 fourth reader-internal bug found while proving Worlds Within Worlds: `_parse_exile_all_creatures_then_hand_refill_clause` (written earlier this same session, never before run through a real scenario) had two defects. First, "each player may put any number of creature cards **from their hand** onto the battlefield" was implemented as re-offering the *just-exiled battlefield creatures* back (reading `owner`-filtered slices of the mass-exile's own result) instead of reading `player.hand` - a wholly different, printed-text-contradicting pool of cards. Second, the unconditional final step ("put all cards exiled this way into their owners' hands") called `Player.move(state, Zone.HAND)` on a card sitting in the shared exile zone, which `Player.move` refuses outright (CR 400.1: exile has no per-player "own" copy) - fixed with `Game.return_to_hand`, the same zone-agnostic primitive Cloak and Dagger's own fix above already uses.
- 2026-09-24: C02-T7 genuine, documented cross-file blockers (locked files this task does not edit): (1) `casting.py`'s `Casting._slice_targets_by_mode` builds each modal spell mode's own `TargetingProfile` from that mode's bare bullet text with no zone context, so `targeting.profile_for_text("target creature card")` (no zone) returns `understood=False` even though the identical phrase either with an explicit zone or without the trailing "card" noun succeeds - Call Damage Control's own `casting.choose_targets(...)` therefore always leaves `CastRecord.mode_targets` empty; proven instead by supplying the `CastRecord` state directly, see `test_hand_reveal_and_graveyard_returns.py`'s own docstring. (2) `layers.py`'s `Count` discriminated union has no "cards in a player's hand" variant, so Ms. Marvel, Kamala Khan's granted "base power is equal to the number of cards in your hand" CDA cannot be added as a further layer-7b effect once the ability line itself is hand-granted; only the draw half of her trigger is proven. (3) `combat.py` has no reader for "can't be blocked this turn except by creatures with `<keyword>`" (only a bare restriction, a power comparison, and a token exclusion), so Speed, Young Avenger's own exception clause is matched and explicitly refused (stays a `notice`, never silently wrong). (4) `combat.py`'s own `_attack_requirement_match` (CR 508.1d) checks a requirement line's subject against `""`/`"this creature"`/`"this permanent"`/the creature's full printed name, never CR 201.5c's *shortened* legendary self-reference - Ares, God of War's own printed "Ares attacks each combat if able." (subject "Ares" alone, not "Ares, God of War") is therefore never recognized as a requirement at all; `xfail`ed in `test_attacking_creature_dies_return.py` rather than reshaped. Each of the four needs a change inside a file locked to a different C02 task this round.

(1) `combat.py` gained `_CANT_BE_BLOCKED_EXCEPT_BY_KEYWORD_RE`/`block_exception_keyword`, read by `evasion_restriction_refusal` (CR 509.1a with CR 509.1b's "unless" folded on): a blocker without the named keyword is refused, one with it is let through; `effects.py`'s `_TARGET_CANT_BE_BLOCKED_RE` now grants the exception text verbatim instead of refusing the whole clause, so Speed, Young Avenger sweeps `handled`, and `test_speed_grants_the_haste_exception_and_combat_enforces_it` proves it end to end (real cast, real payment, real combat declaration, `Game.can_block`/`why_cannot_block`/`block`). (2) `combat.py`'s `_attack_requirement_match` now accepts every name `_self_reference_names` reads off the creature (the same CR 201.5c short-name vocabulary every other self-reference in that module already uses), not just the full printed name/"this creature"/"this permanent" - Ares, God of War's own bare "Ares attacks each combat if able." is recognized, and `test_ares_is_forced_to_attack_by_the_engine` (the former `xfail`, now passing and unmarked) proves the requirement is actually enforced.
- 2026-09-24: C02-T7-r1, Ms. Marvel, Kamala Khan's granted power CDA (blocker 2 of the four): no new `Count` variant was needed after all - every existing off-battlefield CDA (Tarmogoyf, Boneyard Wurm, Omnath) already reads its count from `LayerContext` at *build* time and wraps it in the existing `FixedCount`, since `Count.__call__` itself only ever receives `(chars, board)`, never `context`; a live "hand size" `Count` member could not be evaluated at modify time regardless of how many union members exist. Added `LayerContext.hand_size_by_player`/`hand_size_of` (populated in `Game._compute_board`, alongside `life_by_player`) and `_HAND_SIZE_POWER_CDA_RE`/`_hand_size_power_cda_builder`, the general "`<this>`'s [base] power is equal to the number of cards in your hand" reader (sublayer 7a, CR 604.3). The real blocker was elsewhere: `effects_for`'s static-only battlefield sweep never consulted `_granted_ability_effects` at all - only the resolution path did, deliberately, because a granted line might be an *activated* ability (Umbral Mantle's own case) that must never apply passively. Widened `_granted_ability_effects` to take `static_only` and thread it into `parsed_layer_effects`, so `_effect_sentences`'s own `StaticAbility`-only gate does the actual filtering; the sweep now reads a granted line exactly when it is genuinely static, activated ones still refused. That still needed `CardState.granted_ability_texts` populated *before* the sweep asks - a one-shot resolved self-grant (Iron Fist's/Ms. Marvel's own "`<this>` gains '`<ability>`' until end of turn.", built via a hand-placed layer-6 `ContinuousEffect`) had never written to that field directly, unlike a static ability's own printed quoted grant (`quoted_ability_grants`/`sync_granted_ability_texts`, which never reads *this* shape - it only recognises the printed "Equipped/Enchanted creature has '...'" and "Other creatures ... have '...'" templates). Added `_stored_self_grant_ability_texts`/`_sync_stored_grant_ability_texts`, the stored-effect twin of `sync_granted_ability_texts`, called every `ContinuousEffects.all_effects` (every board computation): it matches a stored `GrantAbility`/`GrantAbilityInstance` effect's `Only`/`OnlyThisIncarnation` selector against each permanent by id (and, for the incarnation form, `zone_change_stamp`) directly rather than through `Selector.__call__`, since this reconcile step runs *before* the board it would need exists. Reconciled, not appended, so the grant comes off the instant `end_the_turn` drops the stored effect - nothing extra to clean up. `test_ms_marvel_grants_her_controller_a_power_equal_to_hand_size_and_it_tracks_live` proves both the grant and its live (not frozen-at-grant-time) recompute for the rest of the turn.
- 2026-09-24: C02-T7-r1, Speedball, New Warrior's own condition (blocker 3, and the `tests/test_behavioural_sweep.py` gap noted above): `triggers._cast_spell_targets_condition_holds` now checks a "that targets `<restriction>`" tail against `abilities.self_reference_names(source.name, ...)` *before* handing it to `targeting._read_phrase` - a literal CR 201.5/201.5c self-reference ("Speedball") is an identity check against `source.id` among the spell's own targets, never a noun-phrase description `_read_phrase`'s grammar could read (a proper name is not a type, a controller, or a keyword). Fixing this surfaced a second, independent bug the module docstring's own "hand-built `AbilityOnStack`" workaround had been quietly hiding: even with the condition now matching, the trigger never reached the stack at all - `Game._ask_trigger_targets` was handing `profile_for_text` the ability's *whole* printed text (condition included) rather than `TriggeredAbility.effect`, so the bare word "target[s]" inside Speedball's own condition read as an attempted, mandatory-and-unsatisfiable targeting requirement of the ability's own ("target Speedball" misparsed as "target [Speedball-subtype] creature"), and CR 603.3d's "no legal choices, ability removed from the stack" silently ate the whole trigger before its real, targetless effect ever ran. Fixed generally, not per-card: `_ask_trigger_targets` now reads `TriggeredAbility.effect` (already split off the condition by `machines/abilities.py`'s own parser) instead of `.text` for every ordinary (non-reflexive) triggered ability - the reflexive-clause branch right above it is untouched, since a reflexively-created ability's `.text` *is* its condition-plus-consequence and needs the existing special handling. This incidentally closed the exact gap `test_unparsed_trigger_targets.py`'s own docstring predicted and asked to be rewritten for ("Bonecrusher Giant", "Loki, God of Mischief", "Stirring Hopesinger" - all three misread a "becomes the target of"/"that targets" *condition* as an unreadable target of their own); that test is rewritten here to assert the corrected, no-notice outcome. `test_speedballs_own_condition_fires_and_the_pump_and_redirect_resolve` replaces the old hand-built-`AbilityOnStack` test and drives the whole thing - condition, stack placement, pump, redirect - through a real cast.

- 2026-09-26: sos-t2 - `machines/targeting._parse` collects every "any target" occurrence *before* its left-to-right scan of "target `<phrase>`" occurrences, so `spell.targets`'/`TargetingProfile.requirements`' order is not printed order for a card mixing the two (Together as One's own "Target player draws X, ~ deals X damage to any target, and you gain X life" stores `[any target, target player]` even though "target player" prints first). Discovered while writing `test_converge_effect_shapes.py`; documented in the reader's own comment rather than left as a silent trap for the next multi-target compound clause.

Read as its own whole-text trigger effect instead, computing the magnitude itself off `ability.event.subject_id`'s `CastRecord` and calling `_run_mass_pump` with it already substituted in - `sweep_pool.py` had silently classified this clause `handled` before the fix even though it was a complete no-op (the generic mass-pump reader accepted the literal "+1/+0 until end of turn" prefix and silently dropped the trailing "for each color ..." qualifier); confirmed and fixed with a scenario, not just a reclassification.
