The defeat trigger fires off a generic `LAST_COUNTER_REMOVED` raised inside `CardState.remove_counters` (every removal site, not just damage); `Game.put_on_stack` re-moves the spell to the stack, which used to reset `transformed` - it now keeps a back-face-up spell back face up. Agent of Treachery / Keiga's untimed "gain control of target X." is `control_change._parse_gain_control_indefinitely_clause`. Awakened Skyclave's self type grant has no other in-format card with the same template; proven on it alone.
- 2026-09-27: R0a-T1 - Mutavault/Den of the Bugbear were silent although test_manland_activation.py existed: the pool never had them, and `_TRAILING_RETENTION_RE` missed a retention clause after a quoted ability's `."` (the period sits inside the quote), so Den/Hive's duration-first sentence never normalised. "all creature types" in the animation keyword slot is split off (`_ALL_CREATURE_TYPES_ITEM_RE`) into a layer-4 `AddEveryCreatureType`, not fed to the keyword validator.
- 2026-09-27: R0a-T1 - "you've lost life this turn" is a turn-fact phrase (`_TURN_FACT_CONDITION_PHRASES` -> `LOST_LIFE_TURN_FACT`), filled by `Game`'s LayerContext build from `Player.life_lost_this_turn`, not a `record_turn_fact` entry. First attempt widened the gained-life gate regex; dead end - `_TURN_FACT_GATED_SELF_RE` claims every "As long as <condition>, this creature has <kw>" sentence first and returns None for an unknown condition. No other legal printing gates a static on "you've lost life this turn" (Scryfall, this session); Tenured Concocter proves the neighbouring gained-life gate unchanged.
- 2026-09-27: R0a-T1 - proof substitutions: Day of Black Sun's loses-all-abilities-then-destroy pair has no second legal printing (Scryfall), so its group reuses `effects._parse_mass_destroy_predicate` (the "Destroy each ..." qualifier grammar); Lumbering Worldwagon's "This Vehicle's power ..." has no second Vehicle printing, proven via Zendikar Incarnate's same count; Rope added as a third "can't be blocked by more than one creature" attachment (comma-list form) beside Wolfrider's Saddle.
- 2026-09-27: R0a-T1 - shared-file edits kept additive: `cost._PHRASE_TAP` gained an optional "other" after the count (Bladewheel Chariot's "Tap two other untapped artifacts" silently let the Vehicle tap itself); `effects._MASS_SUBJECT_RE`/`_mass_creature_group_targets`/`_mass_pump_parseable` gained "(all) other creatures" (Harvester of Misery, Shefet Archfiend); `layers._ATTACHED_CREATURE_STATIC_RE` gained a bare "and can't be blocked ..." and Rope's ", has X, and can't be blocked ..." branch. noted, not doing now: a noncreature Vehicle still reports its printed P/T in computed characteristics (CR 208.3 says none).
- 2026-09-27: fix-enters-with-counters review - a permanent entering as a copy gets loyalty/lore/defense from the copied object's copiable values (`Characteristics.loyalty` added beside `defense`), put on the event by `_enters_as_copy`; `enter_the_battlefield` skips the copier's own intrinsic counters when `entering_as_copy` is set. Ended "for as long as this remains" casting rules (Suncleanser) are dropped at each SBA check. The tapped_for_mana nonland DriverGap is gone: the sweep stages Llanowar Elves / Mind Stone for creature/artifact/nonland mana-source triggers.
- 2026-09-27: fix-r0a-leftovers - an intervening "if" printed as a serial list ("if A, B, and C, effect", Happily Ever After) runs to the last item opening with "and"/"or" short of the effect (`abilities._intervening_if_end`); `conditions._binary_splits` reads the list's bare commas with its closing joiner. Path of Bravery (off-pool proof of the life-vs-starting-life gate) needed `LayerContext.starting_life_by_player` and an unowned "the number of attacking creatures" count. `control_change` now has one untimed gain-control reader (`parse_gain_control_untimed_clause`, R0a-T5's slot); the old indefinite reader is gone - every pool text resolves to the same effect body, now one dispatch step earlier.
- 2026-09-27: fix-r0a-leftovers - the gain-control merge is behaviour-identical on every pool text, but off-pool Admiral Beckett Brass ("... controlled by a player who was dealt combat damage by three or more Pirates this turn") now fails closed: main's untimed reader accepted it and stole any nonland permanent (no reader enforces that damage-history target restriction). Pinned in test_gain_control_indefinitely.py; expect 184-xln to show it not handled after the next coverage refresh.
- 2026-09-27: CR 201.2b - `conditions/board._grouped_measure` drops nameless objects from "with different names" (a face-down permanent given all creature types by Amoeboid Changeling/Mirror Entity was counting as a name, and two of them as one). `_door_names_among` not changed: it counts `unlocked_doors`, and a manifested Room enters with none.

- 2026-10-03 s7c: proof-card searches (Scryfall, Modern/Pioneer/Standard): `o:"counter target" o:"loyalty ability"` returns only Verdant Command, so `TargetRequirement.loyalty_abilities_only` has no second in-format proof (the generality is the target grammar's category word); `o:"also deals" o:"damage to that creature's controller"` returns Blooming Blast, Heated Argument, Misthios's Fury, Ravaging Blaze (not in pool), Repulsor Blast - Blooming Blast is the only gift one, so Misthios's Fury proves the name-subject rider; Ajani's Response proves the general "costs N less if it targets a <description>" (39 in-format printings, none changed classification). A fail-closed guard for a missing damage amount was tried and removed - it broke X spells (Traumatic Critique, End-Blaze Epiphany).
- 2026-09-28 ec7-rv-oracle: the support oracle's ablation probe now removes every trailing sentence of a clause's *resolution text* (`support.probe_sentences`), not only if/otherwise/instead/unless riders. Which sentences count is decided by what the dispatcher hands its readers - `effects.spell_resolution_text` (extracted from `effect_for`) and `effects.activation_resolution_text` (from `activated_effect_for`), then `_strip_non_effect_preamble` - never by a keyword list; a first cut that ablated raw text flagged 88 keyword/reminder/separate-ability lines. `same_closure` compares `re.Match`, pydantic models and plain objects by value (20 rider clauses were silently unprobeable); an uncomparable closure fails closed (`UnprobeableClosure`). Real gaps found and fixed: Do or Die's "They can't be regenerated", the copy trigger's CR 707.10c new-targets permission, the Approach deck-construction line in resolution text.
- 2026-10-03 r0b-t3-proofs: Grove of the Burnwillows was NOT "works (untested)" - its "Each opponent gains 1 life." rider fail-closed the whole mana ability (`unparsed_rider`). `ManaAbilityRider` gained `opponents_gain_life`/`opponents_lose_life` (`_RIDER_OPPONENTS_LIFE`, applied to every non-controller in `players_in_game`). Grove is the only legal card with the template, so no second printing exists; the lose verb is proven on the bare sentence.
- 2026-10-03 r0b-t3-proofs: Flooded Grove's "Add {G}{G}, {G}{U}, or {U}{U}" could not be chosen: `ManaAbility.resolve` only took one colour for a choice between printed options. A caller that spells the whole pair out now selects the matching multi-mana option (order-free). Second printing: Sunken Ruins.
- 2026-10-03 r0b-t3-proofs: Firdoch Core's "{4}: becomes a 4/4 artifact creature" wiped every creature type (changeling's included) because `_animation_type_modifications` always appended `SetCreatureTypes(subtypes=())`; CR 205.1a replaces creature types only when the effect sets one, so it is now appended only when a subtype is named. Second printing: Alloy Animist on Obsidian Battle-Axe (keeps Warrior).
- 2026-10-03 r0b-t3-proofs: Fountainport's "Sacrifice a token" accepted ANY permanent: `_requirement("token")` returned None and `Game._choice_cost` then falls back to an unrestricted sacrifice. `CardRequirement.token` (True/False) now reads "token"/"nontoken". Dead end noted: the fall-back-to-unrestricted when a quality word is unread is itself fail-open; left alone as changing it risks other cards (name it if it bites). Second printing: Hardened Tactician.
- 2026-10-03 r0b-t3-proofs: Emeria's "if you control seven or more Plains" never triggered: `triggers._CONTROLLED_SUBTYPE_COUNT` strips the plural `s$`, turning "plains" into "plain". The subtype compare now also accepts `<subtype>s`. Second printing: Duergar Hedge-Mage's Plains half.
- 2026-10-03 r0b-t3-proofs: harness quirk, not an engine change: `PlayedScenario.activate` pays costs (a life cost included) before `activate_ability` validates the chosen colour, so a refused colour on Horizon Canopy still costs the life in a scenario; tests assert only the empty pool.
- 2026-10-03 r0b-t3-proofs: CR 119.4 verified: Canopy's pay-1-life is payable at exactly 1 life (life 0, then CR 704.5a loses the game); the unpayable-at-0 case is not asserted.

- 2026-10-03: R0b-T5 - `_CREATE_TOKEN_COPY_OF_TARGET_RE` widened to the temporary-copy tail (CR 707.9b except-clause incl. an added card type, "It/That token gains haste[ until end of turn].", "(Sacrifice|Exile) it at the beginning of the next end step." via `anaphoric_delayed_trigger`), read whole in `_parse_mixed_clauses`; the target's "you control" is optional. Closes The Fire Crystal, Molten Duplication and Cogwork Assembler - the planner called Cogwork "already handled", but it was `notice` on main, so it is a proof of the widening, not a regression guard.
- 2026-10-03: R0b-T5 - `_CREATE_TOKEN_COPIES_EXCEPT_RE`/`_TARGET_PLAYER_CREATES_TOKEN_COPY_RE` `phrase` was `target .+?` under DOTALL and swallowed later sentences into the target description (The Fire Crystal's sacrifice sentence was dropped while the copy still happened); now `[^.]+?`. `sweep_pool.py --effects` before/after: only the three cards above moved (notice -> handled).
- 2026-10-03: R0b-T5 bug - an enters trigger's "with mana value N or greater/less" qualifier was never read: Simulacrum Synthesizer fired for every artifact, its own 0/0 Construct token included, and looped forever (hung the scenario); Doc Ock's Tentacles fired for any creature (its old test only asserted nothing got attached). `triggers._entering_mana_value_qualifier_holds`, proven on both.
- 2026-10-03: R0b-T5 - Giott's "you may discard a card" with an empty hand is an illegal option the engine refuses to accept (CR 608.2d), so the empty-hand scenario scripts no answer rather than a "yes".
- 2026-10-03 s7c (Ochre Jelly, Yorion): read as templated shapes, not names - any delayed timing, any counter kind, half/that many/fixed N, rounded down/up; any permanent description, own-and-control or control. No second Modern/Pioneer/Standard printing exists (Scryfall 2026-10-03: o:"copy of it at the beginning of the next end step" -> Ochre Jelly only; o:"enters with half that many" -> Ochre Jelly only; o:"exile any number of other" o:"return those cards to the battlefield at the beginning of the next end step" -> Yorion only). A copy that dies keeps its copiable values as `CardState.last_known_copiable` (CR 707.2/603.10a), kept across later moves like `last_known_counters`; read only by the look-back copy, never by "copy target card in a graveyard". Sweep drives "exile any number of" via `_SomeOfAnyNumberExiled` (prompt shape, first card; declined is the second golden). Schema 106 -> 107.

- 2026-10-03: s7c-activated - a compound counter put is expanded to simple puts in `split_effect_clauses` (`counter_effects.expand_compound_counter_puts`); the trap was the clause loop pairing only the first "that creature" rider with the target and sending the second to the trigger subject - a run of anaphor riders now goes through `_parse_object_anaphor_clause_series`. The leave-battlefield exile rider is one stored `LeaveBattlefieldExile` (schema 107) keyed on `zone_change_stamp`, shared by Geth's quoted grant, Whip's bare rider and unearth. Kheru Lich Lord stays silent (its upkeep "you may pay ... return a creature card at random" is a different reader).
- 2026-10-03 293-fra-T3: no proof printing outside this round's own listed cards exists for empower Jace or for the "aren't put into their owners' graveyards for having 0 loyalty" exemption (Sanctum Lurker is the only printing), so both are proven on the listed FRA cards themselves (`test_planeswalker_ability_grants.py`, `test_zero_loyalty_exemption.py`); the grant reader needed no change - all eight grants already activated - and the cards' silent verdict is purely the "Empower Jace" keyword name `executes()` does not know yet (T1). Lurker's static stays static_unaudited in the sweep, proven by scenario only.
- 2026-10-03 293-fra T1: Scryfall's keyword name is "Empower Jace" (CR 701.71a), not "Empower"; `machines/keywords.executes` now lists "empower jace" so the sweep stops calling every empower card silent. No Modern/Pioneer/Standard printing outside the 293-fra round carries "Empower Jace" (35 checked against oracle bulk, all in T1-T4), so the proofs are on the round's own printings (clause (c) substitution). Fixtures refreshed/added with `add_card.py` (keywords now tagged); the dispatch-fingerprint baseline gained the new entries. Gap found and fixed: a modal spell's mass-pump mode ("Creatures you control get +1/+2 until end of turn.", Fatehold Charm mode 3) built no effect because the mode-scoped layer composer resolves no group; `parse_mode_effect` now tries `_mass_pump_spell_effect` first.
- 2026-10-04 293-fra T2 (empower Jace batch B): clause (c) - no Modern/Pioneer/Standard printing with "empower Jace" exists outside the 293-fra round, so proof runs on the ten listed 293-fra printings (refreshed via add_card.py), in tests/scenarios/test_empower_jace_batch_b.py; no proof printing outside the round.
- 2026-10-03 293-fra T4: "if you removed two or more loyalty counters to activate it" (Way of the Mind Sculptor) is read by `conditions/events._removed_loyalty_counters` off new `GameEvent.loyalty_removed`, set at the `LOYALTY_ABILITY_ACTIVATED` emit from the paid cost's LOYALTY component (cost as modified, CR 606.4), so CR 603.4's recheck reads the activation, not the board. It is the only legal printing of that condition (Scryfall oracle bulk checked); Ajani Unrelenting is the second printing for the widened event. No schema bump: the field defaults None and only a never-before-readable condition reads it. Two engine bugs fixed alongside: "put a loyalty counter on each planeswalker you control" (Way of the Mentor/Necromancer) treated "planeswalker" as a subtype and placed nothing (`_COUNTER_EACH_GROUP_RE` reader now reads it as a card type), and "loyalty counters among Jaces you control" summed only permanents literally named "Jace", missing Jace, Reality Sculptor (now by subtype too). `test_loyalty_among_named_condition.py`'s two boundary tests changed for that (guard approval needed).
- 2026-10-03 293-fra-t5 Clash of Elements: Scryfall's current oracle reads "on top of their library" (fixture refreshed via add_card.py); `_CHOOSE_TARGET_OWNER_MAY_TOP_ELSE_BOTTOM_RE` now takes "on (the )?top", old wording still readable. Substitution check (clause c): no other Modern/Pioneer/Standard printing carries the combined "owner may put on top / if they do damage / else bottom" shape (Scryfall oracle bulk), so Clash of Elements itself is the proof printing; scenarios cover owner-not-caster, caster's own permanent, token (CR 111.7), decline-to-bottom. dispatch fingerprint baseline regenerated for the fixture text change.
- 2026-10-04 293-fra integration (T5 escalation resolved): c58ec653's lose/gain/draw consequences and bottom-first side of the owner-may-place reader were removed - Scryfall oracle bulk holds no Modern/Pioneer/Standard printing with "its owner may put it on (the) top|bottom of their library" other than Clash of Elements, so those branches were parse-tested only and fitted to no real card. The reader keeps the proven shape (any target, source, N; both top wordings); widen it when a real printing needs another consequence.
- 2026-10-04: 293-fra T6: Null Summoner's sibling-static cast permission now sets `while_controlling=once_per_turn_key(source)` (CR 113.6/607.2a): without it the permission outlived the Summoner and a second Summoner's exiled card was not linked to its own. Clause (c) proofs: Gonti, Lord of Luxury and Thief of Sanity sweep `silent` (their look-at-top-N triggers are unread), so Taster of Wares proves the shared `while_controlling` reader instead; Hewed Stone Retainers proved a real gap ("Cast this spell only if" was never enforced; and "you've cast another spell this turn" with no type word was unread) - both fixed, fingerprint baseline regenerated (two previously-unread conditions now read). Talara's Battalion not separately staged (same reader).
- 2026-10-03 293-fra T7: 190 `set:fra` fixtures were fetched pre-release with all legalities `not_legal`, so the sweep never saw them; refreshing them (Scryfall, `add_card.py`) moved 189 cards in format and exposed 8 sweep failures. Seven were missing sweep drivers (loyalty-ability triggers, own-counter removal, opposing blocker quality, prepared-spell cast, noncombat-damage history); one was an engine crash: an ETB "deals X damage" on an uncast permanent (Ajani's Anguish) now resolves with X = 0 (CR 107.3b/107.3m). `Proft, Sinister Mastermind` is left to its Threshold owner (still `static_unaudited`).

- 2026-10-03 E5 integration: a criss-cross merge (two merge bases, 72f52550 and 03365614) made taking main's side of a conflicted file drop E5's own edits to it; redo such a file through a scratch `git merge` (recursive virtual base), never `git checkout --theirs` or a hand `git merge-file` against one base.

- 2026-10-04 game 891056b6 deadlock: a cast/activation by a player without priority was only refused by `Priority.retain` after `put_on_stack` had stacked its cast triggers, so a CR 603.3b order_triggers pause stood in front of a refusal and no seat could answer it. The gate (`Game.why_not_holding_priority`) is asked in `Game.cast` and `engine_api.cast_card` just before CR 601.2f, and in `begin_activating` for non-mana abilities - NOT in `Casting.model_post_init`/`why_cannot_cast_now`: there it broke 69 tests that probe an opponent's Casting for other refusals, and changed `legal_actions`/`blocked_casts` (which enumerate casts regardless of who holds priority; api seats and playscripts rely on that).

- 2026-10-06 NW01-T2: a printed additional cost's paid cards now carry which verb paid them (`CastRecord.additional_cost_kinds`) and their power as paid (`additional_cost_powers`, CR 608.2h - a sacrificed token has ceased to exist by resolution, so an id lookup is not enough); schema 113 -> 114. Shared casting edits kept additive: `_printed_additional_costs` fills through `cost.take_from_pool` ("any number of"/"one or more" take every nominated object - the old `pool[:required]` charged "any number" nothing), and the targeting-profile X gate also fires for an X announced by an additional cost ("discard X cards" bounding "up to X targets").

- 2026-10-06 NW01-T2: a spell's bare "deals X damage to <target>" reached through the mixed-clause path built `deal_damage(parse_count("X"))`, i.e. `deal_damage(None)`, which crashes at resolution (`ReplaceableEvent.happens` compares None > 0); `amounts._parse_expression_amount_damage_clause` now reads it (spells only) off the cast record's X. Surfaced by Crater's Claws, whose instead-base is read unsubstituted.

- 2026-10-06 NW01-T2 proof substitutions: Light Up the Night and Flame Discharge stay notice (their "that target is a creature or planeswalker" / "modified creature as you cast" conditions are unread) -> Stonesplitter Bolt's bargained "twice X damage ... instead"; Lithomantic Barrage / Sky Hussar print a colour condition or cost, not a count -> Kithkin Zealot's "for each black and/or red permanent target opponent controls"; Invasion of New Phyrexia's "tapped this way" is an effect with its own reader -> Vicious Betrayal; Channeled Force, Feed the Clan and The Rollercrusher Ride were already handled. Vicious Betrayal also needed the targeted "gets +N/+N until end of turn for each <count>" pump, a pool-wide layers notice (Might of the Masses, Defile, Primal Bellow - NW-41's family), now `amounts._parse_target_pump_for_each_clause`.

## Regression-guard approvals

Each line: a change to pre-existing test content, flagged by `scripts/regression_guard.py`, judged by an independent reviewer.

- 2026-10-06 NW01-T5 riders (engine, base ff07d8ba) guard: 3 flagged golden changes, fresh sonnet reviewer APPROVE all - the behavioural sweep now drives loyalty "-X" at X=1: chandra-chill-of-compliance/activated-2 (x 0->1, exactly 1 stun counter), sorin-vengeful-bloodlord/activated-1 (target Llanowar Elves, MV 1, still returned to the battlefield), tamiyo-compleated-sage/activated-1 (target Bonesplitter, a nonland permanent card with MV 1, exiled and copied). No assertion weakened.
- 2026-10-04 stranded-pregame-forced-pass (api repo magic-cards-model-api, base af28b20, commit 561ae01) guard: 2 flagged assert changes in tests/test_mulligans.py::test_the_backstop_records_that_the_seat_did_not_choose (alice's opening_hand_done live True->False, automatic False->True), fresh sonnet reviewer APPROVE - alice is a human seat with an empty CR 103.6 window in a PvAI game, now passed for her by `opening._pass_forced_human_windows` (prod game 5c0e9a57), so recording it as live would be the false claim the test exists to forbid.

- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt-T1 (Mutagen tokens) - proof-card substitutions, none of the four candidates in the task brief carried an exact-legal match: "Featherbrained Filcher-style leaves-the-battlefield token" needed no new reader at all (the LEAVES_THE_BATTLEFIELD+create-token combo already existed), so no substitute was needed there; the "if its mana value was N or less" destroy rider is proven on Extinguish the Light (Modern/Pioneer legal, "you gain 3 life" consequence) rather than Seedship Impact/Sound the Trumpets (both name a second predefined token, Lander/none, out of this task's scope); the "activated abilities of <group> cost {1} less" static is proven on Forensic Gadgeteer (Modern/Pioneer/Standard legal, "artifacts" group plus its own floor clause) rather than Training Grounds (names "creatures", explicitly steered away from in the brief); the "nontoken creatures you control" count is proven on Lena, Selfless Champion (Modern/Pioneer legal, reached through the "for each" tail of the identical `count_expression` reader Sally Pride's own "where X is the number of" uses) since no legal card repeats Sally Pride's exact phrase.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt-T1 - `Game._activated_ability_group_discount` does not exclude the discounting permanent's own activated ability from its own group discount (unlike Hulk, Gamma Goliath's "other creatures" `_power_up_group_discount`): CR 601.2f draws no such line and Mutagen Man's own printed line names no "other", so the Mutagen token it creates is discounted by its own controller's copy of the static exactly like any other artifact token would be.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt-T1 - the "can't reduce the mana in that cost to less than one mana" floor (Forensic Gadgeteer, Training Grounds, Heartstone) is clamped once, against the mana already reduced by every earlier step in `Game.activation_cost` (self cost_reduction, power-up, target-power discount), not against the printed cost - matches CR 601.2f's own "reductions are cumulative" ordering rather than treating the floor as an independent minimum.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T2 - sneak-paid riders (Karai/Turncoat Kunoichi/The Last Ronin's Technique) are tmt-only; checked every other tmt Sneak card's own oracle text and none carries an "if <cost> was paid, instead ..." rider, so no non-listed substitute exists - generality for those three readers rests on the three listed cards' own varying shapes (destination change, permanent-exile, tapped-and-attacking tokens) plus Rix Maadi Reveler (pre-existing pool card) picking up its own long-notice spectacle-paid rider as a side effect of the `_keyword_cost_paid` possessive widening.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T2 - Shredder's Technique's "if an enchantment was destroyed this way" and Party Dude's "whenever one or more of your opponents are attacked" both searched Scryfall for a second Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal printing of the exact template; none found. Generality for the destroy-type rider is structural (any two-type destroy, any destroyed type, any consequence); for the attacked trigger it is the shared batched-declaration event "you attack" already uses, plus Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal Ant-Man, Colony Commander proving the sibling "once each turn" counter-placed mechanism on the same card family.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T2 - fixed a real bug caught only by scenario testing, not by sweep_pool.py: `attacking_statics._ATTACKING_SUBTYPE_KEYWORD_GRANT_RE`'s subtype word used a greedy `[A-Za-z]*`, which swallowed a plural's own trailing "s" into the captured group ("Ninjas" instead of "Ninja"), so `_parse_subtype_list` handed the selector a word `chars.subtypes` never matches - the static parsed clean (`sweep_pool.py --card` read "handled") but never actually granted anything on a live board. Made the word non-greedy so the outer `s?` catches the plural, as `triggers._OTHER_SUBTYPE` already does for the identical shape. Lesson: `sweep_pool.py`'s "handled" means a parser produced a closure, not that the closure does the right thing on a real board - a static/combat-state grant still needs a scenario test that actually checks `has_keyword`/`characteristics_of`, not just the sweep.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T3 (counter payoffs) - Madame Null, Power Broker's "you may pay life equal to its power. If you do, put that many +1/+1 counters on it." has no second Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal printing anywhere: exhaustive `card_search` for "pay life equal to its power", "you may pay life equal to", and "pay life equal to" + "put that many" each returned only Madame Null herself. `counter_payoffs._may_pay_life_equal_to_its_power_then_counters` is still built generally (any positive power, any counter kind) and tested thoroughly on Madame Null; it ships without an off-pool proof scenario, a genuine gap rather than a narrowed scope.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T3 - Savanti Romero, Time's Exile's own "put a counter, then you draw X cards and lose X life, where X is the number of counters on it" also has no second legal printing of that *exact* combined clause (only Bebop, Skull & Crossbones shares it, and it is legal nowhere) - `card_search` on "where x is the number of counters on" plus "draw x cards and lose x life". Generalised the reader over an arbitrary `<clause>` tail (substituting X via `machines.modes.substitute_x`, dispatching through the ordinary whole-clause resolver) instead of fixing it to draw+lose, which let Spider-Man Noir's own "surveil X, where X is the number of counters on it" prove the reader without re-confirming Savanti Romero's own sentence.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T3 - Venus, Torn Between Worlds' second ability, "Whenever a creature you control with a counter on it deals combat damage to a player, you may pay {U}. If you do, draw a card.", sweeps `handled` but the "with a counter on it" qualifier is not actually enforced: `machines/triggers.condition_matches`'s own `DEALS_DAMAGE` branch reads only the type/controller words off the condition text and has no reader for a trailing counter-presence clause, so this would currently fire for any creature the controller attacks with, counter or not. Not fixed in this task: the fix belongs in `machines/triggers.py`, which is outside this task's file scope and plausibly touched by a sibling task this round. Documented rather than silently left, since sweep_pool's own classification does not catch it.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T3-r1 - fixed the gap the line above documented: `machines/triggers._SUBJECT_HAS_COUNTER_RE`/`_subject_has_required_counter` reads a `DEALS_DAMAGE` condition's trailing "with a[n] `<kind>` counter on it" qualifier (CR 122.1) and checks it against the actual subject's counters, called from `condition_matches`'s `DEALS_DAMAGE` branch. Proven on Venus, Torn Between Worlds (no counter -> no trigger; a counter -> fires and draws) and on the off-pool `card_search` find Yathan Tombguard - "Whenever a creature you control with a counter on it deals combat damage to a player, you draw a card and you lose 1 life." (legal Modern/Pioneer/Standard, added via `scripts/add_card.py`, not in any deck list) - whose board puts the ability on a different creature than the one dealing the damage, proving the general subject clause rather than a self-reference. `tests/scenarios/test_dealt_damage_counters.py` gained the three new scenarios.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T4 proof substitutions. Kasmina's Transmutation (candidate) prints "loses all abilities and has base power and toughness 1/1" - not Retro-Mutation's "is a `<Subtype>` with base P/T ... It ..." template - so Spider-Man No More and Lignify prove `layers._ATTACHED_CREATURE_IS_SUBTYPE_RE` instead. Switcheroo was already proven for the plain exchange; the widened parts ("you may", "other", the enters-or-combat-damage split) are proven on The Trickster-God's Heist and Tomebound Lich. Spark Double is an enters-as-copy, not a token copy, so Ember Island Production proves the copy exception list. Card text and M/P/S legality read off the Scryfall json `add_card.py` fetched (no resolve_cards in this session).
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T4 dead end: Machinist's Arsenal ("+2/+2 for each artifact you control and is an Artificer") was dropped as the Equipment-count proof - its Job select (CR 702.182a) never triggers and "Machina - Equip {4}" is not read as equip, so the card cannot be attached at all. Cranial Plating (same sentence) is the proof. Noted, not done here: Job select and an ability-word-prefixed Equip.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T4: Retro-Mutation's "It can't attack" is granted as the self sentence "This creature can't attack." (what `combat._self_attack_restriction_sentence` reads) *after* the Aura's own "loses all abilities" in the same builder (`ContinuousEffect.order`), so the Aura never strips its own restriction; a later, separate "loses all abilities" would still remove it - the same approximation every granted restriction here makes.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T4 found and fixed a shared defect: a *stored* until-end-of-turn grant of a quoted ability (Supernatural Stamina, Pain 101) left its line on `CardState.granted_ability_texts` after cleanup, so the granted dies trigger kept firing on later turns though the computed board no longer had it (`_sync_stored_grant_ability_texts` only reconciles texts a running grant still names). `ContinuousEffects.end_the_turn` now calls `layers._ungrant_ended_stored_texts` for every ended grant (additive; a printed static grant of the same line is re-added by the next `sync_granted_ability_texts`).
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T4: Mondo Gecko's "gains hexproof from that color" rider has no second in-format printing of the same sentence; Sungold Sentinel and Skrelv print "Choose a color. ... hexproof from that color ... can't be blocked by creatures of that color" (a colour-evasion restriction not built), so Distorting Lens proves the colour-choice reader and the rider rests on Mondo Gecko alone.
- 2026-09-27: 271-tmt T4: a trigger's default (unscripted) target answer took the first N candidates, so Kitsune's "two other target creatures controlled by different players" defaulted to two of one player's creatures and `TargetingProfile.check` raised mid-game (the behavioural sweep caught it). The `Game` target chooser now leads with one candidate per controller (computed control, `controller_of`) and removes the ability (CR 603.3d, NO_LEGAL_TARGET notice) when fewer controllers than targets exist - the same reordering `one_per_opponent` already used.
- 2026-09-27: tmt-t5-combat-restrictions - no MCP `resolve_cards`/`card_search`/`knowledge_search` tools were reachable in this worktree's session; every card's legality and oracle text were verified directly against the Scryfall REST API (`api.scryfall.com/cards/named` and `/cards/search`) instead, and every CR number was still verified with `mise run rules:find <n>` against a freshly pulled `.rules/` copy.
- 2026-09-27: tmt-t5-combat-restrictions - "This creature can't attack or block unless its power is N or greater" (Technodrome): a full-text Scryfall search (`o:"unless its power is"`) returned exactly one card in the whole database, Technodrome itself - no second Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal printing of this self power-threshold shape exists to prove generality on. The reader (`combat_statics._SELF_CANT_UNLESS_OWN_POWER_RE`) is still written general (either "or less"/"or greater" direction, "attack"/"block"/"attack or block" alone) rather than pinned to Technodrome's own combination, and is proven in both directions on Technodrome itself (`test_block_restrictions_tmt.py`).
- 2026-09-27: tmt-t5-combat-restrictions - "sacrifice `<X>` unless you discard a card" proof printing substituted: Argentum Masticore (the first candidate found) prints the identical first sentence but continues into a second sentence ("When you discard a card this way, destroy target nonland permanent...") this task does not own; Molten-Tail Masticore (Modern-legal) prints the same trigger with no second sentence and was used instead as the clean single-clause proof.
- 2026-09-27: tmt-t5-combat-restrictions - review finding: `src/magic_cards_model/data/printings/argentum_masticore.json` was left behind after the rejection above; `tests.cards.pool()` globs every file in that directory, so it silently rejoined the swept card pool as a fresh `notice`/`silent` card outside this task's scope. Removed the file, regenerated `tests/dispatch_fingerprint_baseline.json` (its own oracle text was in the baseline as a dispatch sample and dropped with it), and added `test_a_rejected_proof_candidate_is_not_left_in_the_pool` (`tests/test_sweep_pool.py`) to pin a rejected proof candidate out of the pool going forward.
- 2026-09-27: tmt-t5-combat-restrictions - fixed a pre-existing classifier bug in `combat.is_blocked_by_at_most_one_line`: it compared a static line's bare self-reference subject against the card's full printed name only, never CR 201.5c's short form (`_self_reference_names`) every other self-reference reader in the module already uses - Rocksteady, Crash Courser's own "Rocksteady can't be blocked by more than one creature." was already enforced correctly at runtime and only mis-reported `static_unaudited` by the sweep. Fixed additively in the same function this task already owns for the round's group-subject widening.

Not card-specific: every pre-existing "can't attack or block" printing in the pool benefits.

- 2026-09-27 ecl-T1 - `effects._SEARCH_AND_PUT_RE`'s `where_x` capture was greedy (`[^.]+`), which is harmless for every pre-existing "up to X `<quality>` cards, where X is `<expr>`" printing (none of them also print a "reveal" clause in the same sentence) but silently swallows a following "reveal those cards, put them ..." continuation once one exists - Prismatic Undercurrents is the first printing to combine both. Made non-greedy (`[^.]+?`); every existing caller still matches identically since nothing else in the pattern changes. `effects._rejoin_search_clauses` also gained a new elif arm (`_REVEAL_PUT_SEARCH_CONTINUATION_RE`) to reattach that continuation when `_CLAUSE_SEPARATOR` cuts it onto its own sentence, the same shape `_BARE_SHUFFLE_RE`'s own arm already handles for a split-off "then shuffle".
- 2026-09-27 ecl-T1 - `_PER_COUNT_COST_REDUCTION_LINE_RE` (the preamble-stripper that stops a spell's own self-reduction sentence from poisoning `_parse_mixed_clauses`'s per-clause fallback) did not tolerate a leading ability word the way its sibling `_SELF_COST_REDUCTION_LINE_RE` already does, and only matched a literal digit amount - both Rime Chill ("Vivid — This spell costs {1} less...") and Sunderflock ("...costs {X} less...") fell through it and came back `silent` for their whole card until this was fixed. Widened additively (optional ability-word prefix, `{X}` alongside digits, an "or" between the "for each"/"where X is" tails) - every pre-existing caller of this regex prints neither an ability word nor an X, so nothing else changes.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-T1 - `game._COSTS_LESS_X_WHERE_X_IS_RE` (the new generic "This spell costs {X} less to cast, where X is `<expr>`" fallback) first anchored its capture with a trailing `\.?$`, which only matches when the sentence is the *last* line of the oracle text - Sunderflock prints "Flying" and an ETB trigger after its own cost line, so `$` (no `re.MULTILINE`) never matched and the reduction silently computed 0. Fixed by capturing up to the next period or newline (`[^.\n]+`) instead of anchoring to the string's end.

- 2026-09-27: ecl-T3 - fixed a real, previously-undetectable bug in `machines/layers._indefinite_keyword_grant_builder` (used by every "creatures you control have `<keyword>`" static reader, not only this task's new singular one): it stored the *stripped* sentence (`layer_readable_sentences` had already removed the reminder text before the regex ever saw it) as `ContinuousEffect.source_ability`, but `_still_exists`'s own CR 613.6 check compares that field against `Characteristics.abilities`, which keeps the reminder text verbatim whenever the granting sentence's own printed line carries one - Isilu, Carrier of Twilight's persist grant does, since persist's own reminder is folded into the granting sentence rather than sitting on a separate keyword line. The grant parsed and even showed up once in `effects_for`'s own output, but `_still_exists` silently dropped it the very next layer-6 pass because the two strings never matched. - 2026-09-27: ecl-T3 - fixed a second, related gap in `machines/triggers.added_ability_lines`: it returned `()` unconditionally once `permanent.zone is not Zone.BATTLEFIELD`, which is correct for a permanent asked about *while it is still there* but wrong for CR 603.10a's own leaves-the-battlefield look-back - a keyword a static ability *granted* to another permanent (as opposed to one that permanent prints itself) could never produce a dies trigger at all, because `triggered_abilities_on` builds its granted-ability candidate list from this function's return and the departing object's `computed_characteristics` is already `None` (CR 400.7, a new object) by the time that list is asked for. Off the battlefield it now reads `CardState.abilities_as_it_left` (`remember_last_known_power_toughness`'s own pre-existing snapshot, taken the instant before the move) instead, filtered against both `printed`/`granted_ability_texts` and `granted_as_it_left` - the last exclusion is what keeps a *stored*, one-shot grant (Rhys, the Evermore's own "gains persist until end of turn", already reconciled onto `granted_ability_texts` by `machines/layers._sync_stored_grant_ability_texts` before the permanent left) from being counted twice, once through each snapshot. Regression-checked against `test_persist.py`'s own printed-persist suite (unaffected: printed keywords never go through this function at all) plus the six new scenario files this task added.

Added `machines/objects_as_costs.has_convoke_now` (a new function, no change to `spell_grants.py`'s own `SPELL_GRANT_KEYWORDS` dict or any function in it - only a read-only reuse of `_SPELLS_YOU_CAST_HAVE_RE`/`_TRAILING_REMINDER_RE`/`_normalized_quality`/`_object_has_quality`), wired into `objects_as_costs.convoke`'s own existing `has_convoke` check in place of the printed-only read. Proven end to end in `tests/scenarios/test_persist_grants.py`: a creature tapped for Eirdu's grant pays a real symbol of a cast Grizzly Bears' total cost, and a spell with no such grant still refuses to convoke at all.
- 2026-09-27: ecl-T3 review fix - Suncleanser, Decimator Beetle and Tam, Mindful First-Year (plus Cauldron Haze, already recorded above as the proof-substitution search that picked Antler Skulkin instead) were fetched with `scripts/add_card.py` while searching for a second printing, lost to the card actually used, and left behind as live `data/printings/*.json` - `tests.cards.pool()` is a bare glob of that directory, so `sweep_pool.py --effects` counted all four's own unhandled clauses as new silent/notice regressions on cards this task never claims (COMMON ACCEPTANCE (i)). Removed all four printing files and added `tests/test_sweep_pool.py::test_no_abandoned_proof_candidate_printings_pollute_the_pool` as a standing regression guard; regenerated `tests/dispatch_fingerprint_baseline.json` (`mise run dispatch:fingerprint`), which only lost the four cards' own clause rows.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - Champion of the Clachan's/Champions of the Path/Perfect/Shoal's own "behold a `<quality>` and exile it" has no second real printing at all: Scryfall `o:"and exile it" o:behold` returns only these four plus Champion of the Weird (T2's own card this round, not a substitute I can use since T2 adds its own scenario "on top of" this audit in the same file). Generality instead rests on reusing the CR 607.2q "exiled with" link `objects_as_costs.delve` already established (already proven by its own existing delve tests) - the new code here is only `CostComponent.exile_after` reusing that same linking step, plus each Champion's own printed use of it.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - Kindle the Inner Flame's own "Behold three Elementals" (a counted, article-less behold) and Celestial Reunion's own "behold two creatures of that type" are the *only* two counted-behold printings in the whole card database (Scryfall `o:/behold (two|three|four)/`) - both listed cards this round, no substitute available.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - Dawn-Blessed Pennant's/Eclipsed Realms' own restricted "choose Elemental, Elf, Faerie, Giant, Goblin, Kithkin, Merfolk, or Treefolk" enumeration and Chronicle of Victory's/Oko's own "get `<P>`/`<T>` and have `<keywords>`" chosen-type anthem tail each have no second real printing (Scryfall full-text searches this session for both exact shapes return only the listed cards). The restricted list is proven by exercising the restriction itself (a legal choice succeeds, an out-of-list choice - "Human", a genuine CR 205.3m type - is refused); the anthem-plus-keyword tail is proven on two structurally different carriers within the round's own cards (a permanent's own static line, and a freshly minted emblem), since no other printing combines the two shapes to prove against instead.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - fixed a real, pre-existing engine bug found while testing Dawn-Blessed Pennant: a bare lowercase "of the chosen type" inside an ENTERS_THE_BATTLEFIELD condition was never read by `triggers._required_entering_subtype` (which only recognises a printed, capitalised subtype word), so "a permanent you control of the chosen type enters" silently matched *any* permanent entering, itself included - Pennant gained life off its own arrival. Fixed generally in `condition_matches`'s own ENTERS_THE_BATTLEFIELD branch, not name-gated to Pennant.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - also fixed, in the same investigation, `effects._parse_self_damage_equal_to_count_clause`'s bare-pronoun ("it"/"he"/"she") subject: it always read the ability's own source for magnitude and damage source, which is right for a self-referential trigger (Smaug the Magnificent, Eivor, Battle-Ready) but wrong for Champion of the Path's "whenever another Elemental you control enters, it deals damage equal to its power" - "it" is CR 400.7's ordinary anaphora back to the *entering* Elemental, not Champion itself. Now prefers `AbilityOnStack.event`'s own subject for a bare pronoun, which is provably identical to the source for every existing self-referential printing (the event's own subject of a self-caused ENTERS/DIES/ATTACKS is the source).
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - `machines/cost.total_cost_for_phrase` tries the whole, un-split cost phrase first when it has no comma, before falling back to `_split_cost_phrase`'s "," / " and " splitter: a single-component phrase whose own words happen to include a bare "and" ("behold a Kithkin and exile it", "Behold three Elementals" is comma-joined with its own mana and unaffected) was being cut in two, with the second half ("exile it") unreadable on its own. No comma-joined or genuinely compound cost in the pool regressed (checked every existing "additional cost" printing containing "and" by hand).
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - Winnowing's own "don't share a creature type with the chosen creature" has no second printing at all (Scryfall `o:"share a creature type"`); proven instead on Changeling Wayfinder (Modern-legal, a genuine CR 604.3/702.73a changeling) as the comparator's own off-pool proof, since `shares_creature_type` reads `Game.characteristics_of`'s computed subtype set and a changeling's computed set is all 324 creature types, not merely its printed "Shapeshifter".
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - Bloodline Bidding's/Harmonized Crescendo's/Wanderwine Farewell's own convoke-spell bodies needed no proof beyond the listed cards: each reads a fresh, resolution-time "choose a creature type" through `chosen_type.ask_creature_type` (a plain `DecisionKind.CHOOSE_A_QUALITY` ask, not CR 614.12a's), and the actions after it (return from graveyard, draw per permanent, bounce plus conditional token creation) each reuse already-proven primitives (`game.put_onto_battlefield`, `game.draw_cards`, `_parse_token_clause`).
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type - `Selfless Safewright`'s "other permanents you control of that type" needed a genuinely new selector (`layers.PermanentsControlledByWithSubtype`): every existing chosen-type/subtype selector in the pool (`CreaturesControlledByWithSubtype`) requires `chars.is_creature`, which would wrongly exclude a noncreature permanent that happens to carry the chosen creature subtype. No off-pool proof exists for this exact non-creature-gated grant; covered by scenario coverage across a matching creature, a non-matching creature, and the source itself ("other").
- 2026-09-27 ecl-t4-behold-chosen-type (review fix) - Dawn-Blessed Pennant's real gameplay condition already fires correctly (the `triggers.py` fix logged above), but `sweep_pool.py --card` still reported its ETB trigger `unmatchable_condition`: `support._probe_cards`/`_condition_can_fire`'s reachability prober had no case for "of the chosen type" at all - `_required_entering_subtype` only recognises a literal capitalised subtype word, so the prober found no real permanent to test against and no synthetic source ever had `CardState.choice` set to satisfy the same-card "of the chosen type" filter. Fixed generally in `support.py` (`_chosen_type_domain`/`_chosen_type_probe`, gated on the literal condition phrase "of the chosen type", never on a card name): the prober now reads the printing's own CR 614.12a "as [this] enters, choose ..." restriction (the enumerated eight, or every CR 205.3m type otherwise), finds a real pool permanent of a type in that domain, and records that same choice on the synthetic source before asking `condition_matches` - so any future card with this shape gets a correct sweep verdict, not only Pennant.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-T2 (blight-transform-triggers): CR 701.68's Blight as an ordinary resolving *effect* (not the cost `machines/cost.py` already read) lives in a new `machines/blight_effects.py`, wired into `_ability_effect_or_notice`/`_spell_effect_or_notice` (whole-text) and `_parse_mixed_clause`/`_parse_mixed_clauses` (per-clause, for a "blight N" that is itself another reader's recursive `parse_mixed_effect` consequence - Dose of Dawnglow's own "Then if it isn't your main phase, blight 2." tail). The general CR 118.12 "you may `<action>`. If you [do|don't], `<consequence>`." reader (`parse_may_action_then_branch_clause`) is registered as the *true last resort* in both dispatchers - after every narrower "may ... if you do/don't" reader, `_parse_targeted_clause`, and even `_parse_optional_player_clause`/`_parse_optional_mixed_clause` - specifically so it never shadows a more specific existing reader for the identical shape; an earlier placement near the top of `_parse_mixed_clause` did shadow several (`_merge_may_discard_if_you_do_clauses`'s own shape among them), caught only by the dispatch-fingerprint diff, not by any test.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-T2: two real condition/effect-splitter bugs in `machines/abilities._parse_triggered`, both from a legend's own comma-bearing face name ("Ashling, Rekindled") colliding with the plain top-level comma split: "this creature **enters or** transforms into `<face>`," had no boundary reader at all (only the bare "transforms into `<face>`," shape did - `_transforms_into_face_boundary`), and "transforms into `<face>` **and at the beginning of your first main phase**," failed that same reader's own comma-immediately-after-the-face check. Fixed with two new boundary functions (`_enters_or_transforms_into_face_boundary`, `_transforms_into_face_and_first_main_boundary`); the second one's own combined trigger is then split into its two `TriggerEvent`s by a new `triggers._expand_transforms_and_first_main`, wired the same additive way every other `_expand_*` compound-condition splitter in that module already is.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-T2: CR 702.16h's "protection from each color" can be granted only as one line joined with CR 702.16g's own literal "`<A>` and from `<B>`" words (`Game._effective_protection_qualities`/`_qualities_from_text`/`_PROTECTION` stop scanning a granted line at the first period, so a *comma*-joined "Protection from white, Protection from blue, ..." reads back as one garbled, unmatched quality instead of five real ones) - Akroma's Memorial's own two-quality grant already relies on this same "and from" join; blight_effects.py's protection-from-each-color reader now builds the five-color grant the identical way.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-T2: reflexive "you may `<action>`. When you do, `<consequence>`." (CR 603.12, Warren Torchmaster) and a genuinely optional "up to one target" chosen when a triggered ability is put on the stack (Grub, Storied Matriarch's own "return up to one target Goblin card...") both need the target *pre-scripted* (`Scenario.will_choose_targets`) before the ability is ever put on the stack - `Scenario.choose_trigger_targets` (called after the fact) is a no-op once the ability has already gone on the stack with a default (frequently wrong, e.g. the source itself) or empty target list; it only works for a *mandatory* single-legal-target case with nothing else to ask (Mardu Woe-Reaper's own "target creature card from a graveyard" with exactly one candidate).
- 2026-09-27 ecl-T2: proof-printing substitutions - Mardu Woe-Reaper (Modern/Pioneer, `o:"you may exile target creature card from a graveyard"`) for the general "you may `<action>`. If you do, `<consequence>`." reader; Brutal Cathar // Moonrage Brute (Modern/Pioneer) for the enters-or-transforms-into fix on a face with no internal comma; Troyan, Gutsy Explorer (Modern/Pioneer/Standard) for a second "spend this mana only to cast spells with mana value N or greater"-style restriction, on an activated mana ability rather than a triggered one.
- 2026-09-27 ecl-T2 review fix: Champion of the Weird's own casting cost, "As an additional cost to cast this spell, behold a Goblin and exile it.", was left `xfail` deferring to 270-ecl T4's behold-and-exile audit; the task text requires this task's own scenario to prove it end-to-end, so it is closed here instead. `_PHRASE_BEHOLD` reads an optional trailing "and exile it" (CR 118.8/601.2f's one object, two verbs), `CostComponent.exile_after` makes `pay`/`unpay` move the beheld object like an ordinary `EXILE` component, and `total_cost_for_phrase` tries a comma-free phrase whole before its "and"-splitting fallback, so the phrase is read as one component rather than split into an unreadable "behold a Goblin" plus "exile it". General fix, not card-specific: also reads Champion of the Clachan's identically-shaped "behold a Kithkin and exile it" (off-pool, T4's own proof card).

They are now layer 6 grants at CR 613.7c timestamps (`ContinuousEffects.keyword_counter_grants`/`keyword_counter_stamps`, schema 92); `Game._supplement_characteristics` no longer adds the keyword-counter lines itself and `keyword_counters.supplemental_keyword_counter_lines` is gone.
- 2026-09-27: ecl T5 - a legend's first-word short name behind "a"/"an" or a colour word is a creature type, never CR 201.5's self-reference (`abilities._TYPE_NOUN_LOOKBEHIND`): Lizard, Connors's Curse's "becomes a green Lizard creature" was being read as "a green this permanent creature". `layer_readable_sentences` also now passes the card name as `face` so a self-named trigger condition with its own comma is cut after the name.
- 2026-09-27: ecl T5 - `support._static_ability_category` now passes `legendary=` like the battlefield sweep does; without it nonlegendary Noggle the Mind's own "is a colorless Noggle" read as a self-reference and scored static_unaudited.

- 2026-09-27: ecl T6 - `machines/layers.py`'s shared `_SUBJECT`/`_SUBJECT_NOUN` grammar (used by `_PUMP_RE` and a dozen sibling regexes) was left untouched; the new bare-subtype target noun (Boggart Prankster's "target attacking Goblin", Vinebred Brawler's "another target Elf", Tributary Vaulter's "another target Merfolk", Safewright Cavalry's "Target Elf") is read by a new, additive sibling regex (`_SUBTYPE_TARGET_PUMP_RE`) that validates the captured word with `canonical_subtype` before accepting it, rather than widening the shared `_SUBJECT_NOUN` alternation that a dozen other readers also depend on.
- 2026-09-27: ecl T6 - proof-printing dead ends, each verified with several Scryfall full-text oracle searches this session: no second Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal printing was found for the bare-subtype-target-pump shape itself (`o:"target attacking Goblin you control gets"`, `o:"target Merfolk you control gets"`, `o:"target Elf you control gets"` each return only the listed ecl cards - proven instead across all four listed cards' own differently-shaped triggers/activation); "attacked with N or more `<type>` this turn" as a static group-pump gate; "untap each other `<subtype>` you control"; the dynamic-mana-value-cap "onto the battlefield tapped and attacking, where X is the number of attacking creatures you control" (Paladin Elizabeth Taggerdy carries the identical shape but is not legal in Modern, Pioneer, or Standard); the reflexive (CR 603.12, "when you do") "tap another untapped `<X>` you control" (Civil Servant and Gravelgill Scoundrel carry the identical action but as CR 118.12's "if you do", not the reflexive shape); "as long as another creature entered the battlefield under your control this turn, `<static>`" (Wary Farmer and Waterspout Warden carry the identical clause but as an intervening-if on a triggered ability, never as a static "as long as" gate); "creatures you control get +X/+X, where X is the number of creatures that entered the battlefield under your control this turn"; "gets +1/+1 for each creature you control and each creature card in your graveyard" (the double-sided count); and the Equipment-scoped "As long as equipped creature's toughness is greater than its power, ..." (Ancient Lumberknot proves only the pre-existing whole-team shape). Each is instead proven either across the listed card's own real corner cases or, where found, a genuinely differently-worded off-pool printing (Smoke Bomb, Saryth the Viper's Fang, Akroan Hoplite, Warbriar Blessing - see `docs/engine-coverage.md`'s own 270-ecl T6 section for which reader each covers).
- 2026-09-27: ecl T6 - Giant's Amulet grants the identical sentence Illusion Spinners prints ("This creature has hexproof as long as it's untapped.") only as a *quoted* ability inside another static ("Equipped creature ... has '...'"). This engine's layer computation does not re-derive a fresh continuous effect from a granted ability's own quoted text (a granted *activated* ability gets special handling via `sync_granted_ability_texts` so it can be activated, but a granted *static* line is not itself re-parsed as a nested layer effect) - a separate, larger gap than this task's scope, so Giant's Amulet could not stand in as the off-pool proof for `_SELF_KEYWORD_WHILE_TAP_STATUS_RE` even though its own static clause already reads `handled` on the sweep.
- 2026-09-27: ecl T6 - `effects.count_expression`'s shared `_COUNT_YOU_CONTROL_RE` gained "attacking" alongside its existing "tapped"/"untapped" status word (CR 508.1k combat state, read off `Game.combat.attacker_ids` rather than `CardState.tapped` - a vigilance attacker can be both attacking and untapped at once, so the two are never folded together). Proven on Akroan Hoplite (off-pool, "the number of attacking creatures you control") as well as the listed Kinscaer Sentry.
- 2026-09-27: ecl T6 - `effects._IT_GETS_DYNAMIC_X_PUMP_RE`'s power and toughness are read independently (each either a literal number or "x"), not both forced to "x" together, so Akroan Hoplite's own "it gets +X/+0 ..." (power dynamic, toughness a literal "+0") reads through the identical reader Thoughtweft Imbuer's "+X/+X" does.

- 2026-09-27 ecl-T8: a reminder-text parenthetical is stripped off `TriggeredAbility.effect` before any dispatcher ever sees it (every other trigger's own reminder only restates a rule read elsewhere) - Mutable Explorer's own token definition lives entirely in that reminder, so `effects.effect_text_with_named_token_reminder` puts it back for this one shape, called from both `Game._etb_x_effect_text` (a real resolution) and `support.py`'s own dispatch-text assembly (the static sweep), which otherwise disagree about what the ability's own text even is. Added `TargetRequirement.distinct_from_prior_requirement` (set only for the paired "any target"/"any other target" idiom `_parse` reads off the whole text, never for a solitary "any other target" with nothing before it - Iron Fist, Living Weapon/Red Hulk/Screaming Nemesis keep meaning "other than this ability's own source", `excludes_source`) and `TargetingProfile._check_distinct_from_prior`, enforced in `check()` alongside `_check_one_per_opponent`/`_check_prior_target_player`. `_fill_slots` now returns its `taken` bookkeeping so the cross-check can compare which `TargetRef` filled each of the two requirements.

- 2026-09-27 R0a-T3 (scope-audit follow-up): Conduit of Ruin's own cast trigger - "you may search your library for a colorless creature card with mana value 7 or greater, reveal it, then shuffle and put that card on top" - was reported `silent` by a live `sweep_pool.py --card` run: its mana-value bound is a floor ("or greater"), and `effects._SEARCH_AND_PUT_RE`/`_SEARCH_REVEAL_SHUFFLE_TOP_RE` (and the shared `_search_descriptions`/`_bounded_quality_matcher`/`_quality_description` pipeline they both feed) only ever read a ceiling ("or less"), so the whole clause failed `fullmatch` and fell through to no effect and no notice. Widened all three with an `mv_cmp` ("less"/"greater") direction, defaulting to "less" for every other search regex that still only names `max_mv` (`groups.get("mv_cmp")`, never `match.group`, since that name does not exist on those patterns). Off-pool proof: Fierce Empath ("... a creature card with mana value 6 or greater, reveal it, put it into your hand, then shuffle" - the *other* search-and-put destination), Standard/Pioneer/Modern-legal, added with `add_card.py`; `code/magic-cards-model-api/decks/*.txt` was not reachable from this worktree (only this submodule is checked out here), so its absence from the deck pool is corroborated instead by `docs/card_coverage/044-scg/status.md`, which already lists it pool-wide as `silent` before this fix, not one of this task's own rows. `resolve_cards`/`card_search` MCP tools were unavailable in this session; legality and Oracle text were verified directly from Scryfall via `add_card.py`, the same source those tools resolve against.

If you control a Bird, counter that spell unless its controller pays {4} instead.", Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal, not in any deck list) is unchanged, now honestly attributed to `_parse_condition_instead_clause`.
- 2026-09-27: R0a-T7. Agadeem's Awakening's "Return from your graveyard to the battlefield any number of target creature cards that each have a different mana value X or less." exposed a real gap in the *announcement*-time targeting path, not just this reader: `Casting.targeting`/`spell_profile_for_text` never understood the phrase (the zone clause sits before the object noun phrase, the reverse of every other "return target ... from your graveyard" printing), so `TargetingProfile.check` returned early on `understood=False` and accepted *any* targets, silently - not merely "notice", a genuine legality hole. Fixed generally with a new `_return_from_zone_to_destination_profile` pre-scan in both `profile_for_text` and `spell_profile_for_text` (the `{X}`-costed spell path bypasses the former and calls the latter directly, so both needed the fix). Added `TargetRequirement.distinct_mana_values`, the mana-value twin of the pre-existing `distinct_controllers`, enforced in `TargetingProfile._why_not`/`_fill_slots`. No schema change - both new fields live on the runtime-computed `TargetingProfile`, never persisted.

- 2026-09-27 R0a-T6: Room permanents (Unholy Annex // Ritual Chamber) built from scratch - `CardState.unlocked_doors` (CR 709.5c designations), `Game.unlock_door` (the shared CR 709.5d/e/f entry point, called from `Game.enter_the_battlefield` for the cast half; a CR 709.5e special action and CR 709.5f "unlock" resolution effect are not built - no card in this task's list needs either), `ExtraTriggerEvent.DOOR_UNLOCKED` + condition_matches dispatch matched to `BaseAbility.face`. Found and fixed a real pre-existing bug on the way: `CardState.up_face` defaulted ANY split card off the stack to its front face alone (CR 712.8-style single-active-face), which is CR 709.4's combining default, not CR 709.3's - `machines/triggers.triggered_abilities_on`'s `up_face_name` filter (built for a transform/MDFC) then silently dropped every ability printed on a split permanent's non-front half. - 2026-09-28 ec7-cast-rest: three silent/unaudited clauses closed generically. Emrakul, the World Anew's leaves-the-battlefield "sacrifice all creatures you control" reads through a new `_parse_sacrifice_all_you_control_clause` (effects.py), `_UNTAP_ALL_YOU_CONTROL_RE`'s own shape with `game.sacrifice` in place of `.untap()`; proved generic on Death Pit Offering's identical enters-the-battlefield sentence (added to the pool). Kozilek, the Broken Reality's "Other colorless creatures you control get +3/+2" needed a `colorless` qualifier on `layers.QualifiedCreatures`/`CreatureQualifiers`/`_SUBJECT_QUALIFIERS` (CR 105.2c: no colour at all, never `without_color`'s negation of one named colour) - proved on Tide Drifter (Devoid, added to the pool). Herigast, Erupting Nullkite's "Each creature spell you cast has emerge. The emerge cost is equal to its mana cost." needed a new `casting._grant_spell_you_cast_has_keyword` (CR 611.3d), the "spell you cast has `<keyword>`" sibling of `_grant_on_a_card_in_a_zone`'s "card in a zone has `<keyword>`" - `_COST_IS_THE_CARDS_MANA_COST` widened to accept "its mana cost" alongside Iroh's "that card's mana cost"; no second real pool card shares this exact grant shape, so generality rests on the reader taking any keyword/quality, not on a second printing. Also fixed in-delivery (discovered while checking the two cards' keyword lines per instruction, not separately requested): Emrakul's own "Madness—Pay six {C}." was unreadable (`total_cost_for_phrase` had no "Pay `<count>` `{symbol}`" grammar) - fixed generically in `cost.py` (`pay_repeated_symbol_mana_cost`), refusing CR 107.14's `{E}` (not a mana symbol, an unimplemented energy-counter cost) rather than mistreating it as mana. "Protection from spells" (CR 112.1: a spell is a card on the stack, never a CR 205.2a card type) was also unread - `source_has_quality`/`source_has_quality_of_card` gained an `is_spell` question threaded from every `protection_refusal` call site (`_legal_retarget`, `legal_target`, `why_cannot_target`, `TargetingProfile._check_protection`, the last always `is_spell=True` since `source_card` is only ever set on a spell's own profile). Correction, same day: Emrakul's second quality, "permanents that were cast this turn", is implemented too now - the owner rule is no rule left for later. No new `CardState`/`Game` field was needed after all (no schema bump): `Game.was_cast_this_turn` combines two facts that already existed - `cast_record_of` (re-keyed onto the permanent as it resolves, CR 400.7d, and left behind by any *later* zone change, which is what correctly excludes a flickered or reanimated permanent) and membership in `spells_cast_this_turn` by card id (reset every turn, which is what excludes an old, undisturbed permanent cast turns ago). `source_has_quality`/`protection_quality_against` (damage, blocking, enchanting, equipping) already had the live `CardState` they needed; the targeting half (`protection_refusal`/`source_has_quality_of_card`) did not, so it gained a `source_state: CardState | None` threaded from the same call sites `is_spell` already uses - `None` for a spell (never a permanent, CR 110.1), the ability's own live source (`AbilityOnStack.source`) otherwise.

- 2026-10-03 R0b-T2: a Pathway's front face is itself a land, so `Card.land_face` (first land face) could never name the back; `Game.play_land(face=)` + `Card.land_faces` + one PLAY_LAND offer per land face fixes it. The api now passes it (`PlayLandRequest.land_face`, or a card name that is one of the Pathway's land faces; branch r0b-t2-api in code/magic-cards-model-api). Fixed: a mana ability activated with an invalid colour used to raise AFTER the cost had tapped the source; `Game.activate_ability` now rewinds the paid cost on that refusal (test_a_colour_not_on_offer_refuses_without_tapping_the_source). Auto-tap used to pay a generic cost with a painland's coloured (damaging) ability; `_prefer_painless_mana` swaps it for the rider-free ability when the plan still pays. Proof cards used for the reader changes are the listed cards themselves (R0b relaxation); no substitution.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T2 fix round: `sweep_pool.py --effects` before (549c9182) vs after (this branch) is byte-identical (116 of 2978 silent, 23 notice, 70 static_unaudited, 4 dead triggers): the `_prefer_painless_mana` auto-pay change moved no card outside this task.
- 2026-10-03 R0b T3 fix: Game._choice_cost no longer falls back to an unrestricted sacrifice/discard for an unread quality word (CR 602.5: refused with ValueError). Bullseye 'Sacrifice an artifact or discard a nonland card' is read as one either-or cost (CR 118.1); Kozilek 'Discard a card with mana value X' pays as 'a card' (X-to-target-spell link is not enforced here). sweep_pool --effects before/after vs 549c9182: identical per-card, silent 116, notice 23.
- 2026-10-03 R0b T3 fix: Kozilek 'Discard a card with mana value X' now links X to the target spell's mana value (Game._choice_cost takes the activation targets; the discard requirement gets mana_value = target's, CR 107.3). With no target chosen yet the cost still reads as 'a card'.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T4: the sweep calls Bitter Triumph, Deadly Precision, Dusk Mangler, Torch the Tower and March of Wretched Sorrow `handled` but four of them were not: Bitter Triumph/Deadly Precision/Dusk Mangler were uncastable (the "or" additional-cost reader knew only "[cost] or pay [mana]"; now `casting.or_additional_cost_halves` reads mana-first, life-fallback and comma-list spellings), March's "{2} less for each card exiled this way" had no cost machinery (`Casting.exile_for_cost_reduction`; its siblings were silent until the Swirling Mist "up to X target creatures phase out" shape was read too), and Torch the Tower silently dropped "and you scry 1" and its "dealt damage by [name]" exile-instead sentence. Lesson: a `handled` sweep row for a multi-sentence spell only proves the sentences were stripped or read, not that each runs - write the scenario.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T4: `Game.cast(bargain=, collect_evidence=, reveal_for_cost=)` announced those costs after the targets, so a targeted spell with one raised "cannot go back to modes from targets"; they now go before CR 601.2c's targets like casualty and conspire.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T4 generality note (c): the Torch the Tower readers ("you scry N", "that permanent" in an instead-clause, "dealt damage by [name]") have no second Modern/Pioneer/Standard-legal printing (Scryfall search: only Torch carries them), so Torch is their sole proof; the mana-first "pay {4} or sacrifice" reader is proved on Annihilating Glare, the life fallback and three-way reader on Dusk Mangler, and the exile-discount reader on March of Swirling Mist (all in no deck). No substitution of a named proof card was needed.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T4 dead end: tried reading Dusk Mangler's "sacrifice a creature, discard a card, or pay 4 life" as one compound cost; it is a choice of one, so the or-reader's `primary_alternatives` became an index -> alternative map instead of a set of indexes.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T6: Professor Dellian Fel's emblem "Whenever you gain life, target opponent loses that much life." was swept `handled` (the -6 clause) but the emblem's own trigger parsed to None and resolved as a silent no-op (bob stayed at 20); found by the scenario, not the sweep. Fixed with `effects._THAT_MUCH_LIFE_LOSS_RE` (loss only - no legal card in the pool needs the gain twin, so it is not built speculatively). Second-printing proof is Vito, Thorn of the Dusk Rose (Modern/Pioneer legal, verified from its Scryfall printing).
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T6: Hill Giant is mana value 4 ({3}{R}), not 3 - an Infusion (Moseo) test that gained 3 life and expected it back found no legal target; use gains of 1+3 for the "total life gained" case.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T7: Sapling Nursery's "Treefolk and Forests you control gain ..." only reached the Treefolk - the mass subtype-subject reader (effects.py) gated on creatures first; dropped the creature gate so land/artifact types work (CR 205.3i). No second legal printing exists with a land-type subject list (Scryfall search for "<land type>s you control gain"): the widening is proven on Nursery alone, creature subjects regress via Along the Crooked Way's golden.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T7: "create a Lander token" had no PREDEFINED row (CR 111.10u) so the token had no Lander subtype; added LANDER. Proof printing: Sami's Curiosity.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T7: Surrak, Elusive Hunter's compound becomes-target subject ("a creature you control or a creature spell you control") never triggered; _OBJECT_TARGET_SUBJECT now takes "or" alternatives and BECOMES_TARGET is emitted for spells on the stack. No other Modern/Pioneer/Standard card prints the spell alternative; Battle Mammoth is the negative/regression proof. Loki-style "a player or permanent" now ignores stack objects.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T7: Scryfall rate-limits (429, retry-after 42-60s) hit add_card.py hard when sibling tasks run in parallel; a retry loop with a 70s sleep per failure got all 14 printings.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T7: sweep_pool.py --effects before (549c9182) vs after (HEAD) diffed: silent/notice/static/never-fire sets identical (116 silent, 23 notice, 70 static, 4 never-fire); only change is +13 handled cards (2765 -> 2778 of 2978 -> 2991 with rules text).
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T8: Seam Rip/Sheltered by Ghosts exposed that `_parse_exile_until_leaves_battlefield_clause` ignored CR 610.3b (source gone before the ETB resolves still exiled the target); fixed with a battlefield-zone check, proven on Prayer of Binding and Dimensional Exile.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T8: She-Hulk's "Whenever a creature you control is dealt damage" never fired (passive branch of DEALS_DAMAGE only knew "this creature"/"opponent"), and Rite of Passage's "put a counter on it" hit the damage dealer; both fixed (triggers.py recipient clause, effects.py `_damage_recipient_is_its_subject`). Second printing: Rite of Passage (Modern).
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T8: `TurnMachine.insert_extra_phase` inserted an "after this phase" combat in the middle of the current combat (Fear of Missing Out, Desert Were-Worm, Hellkite Charger); now the rest of the phase runs first (CR 500.8). `test_total_power_extra_combat.py` asserted the old interleaving ("still attacking from the paused original combat") and was edited to assert the corrected order; it needs an independent sonnet regression-guard approval line at merge.
- 2026-10-03 R0b-T8: dead-end note: Snapcaster's granted flashback is a `PlayPermission` (use `Casting.use_permission()`), not a printed-keyword `use_keyword("Flashback")`; "until end of turn" grants only expire in a manual-turn-actions scenario after `turn_based_actions()` at cleanup.

- 2026-10-03 R0b integration: bulk classify (oracle bulk, Modern/Pioneer/Standard) r0b-integ vs main 5af4c8df showed Thousand-Faced Shadow notice -> silent: R0b-T5 tightened `_CREATE_TOKEN_COPIES_EXCEPT_RE`'s phrase so the trailing "The token enters tapped and attacking." no longer rode along unread. Fixed by reading that rider (CR 508.4) plus two runtime gaps it exposed: "enters from <zone>" was never read (fired from anywhere, and "from your graveyard" made the ability function only from the graveyard so Phyrexian Dragon Engine never triggered), and ninjutsu raised the enters event before the ninja was attacking, so "if it's attacking" (CR 603.4) was always false. Only Thousand-Faced Shadow prints the copy-enters-attacking rider in format, so no second printing exists; the enters-from reader is proven on Phyrexian Dragon Engine. Treacherous Pit-Dweller was tried as a proof and dropped: its Undying keyword is unimplemented (card stays silent, not added to the pool).

Adding Esika exposed that a nonland modal back face was never castable (The Prismatic Bridge's upkeep trigger failed the sweep as "never reached the stack"); now offered by `modal_dfc_spell_back_faces`, kept up on the battlefield through `land_face_played` set in `move_to` (CR 712.13) - chosen over `transformed` because CR 712.8f gives a modal permanent its up face's own mana value, whereas `transformed` reads the front's (CR 712.8e). `move_to` now also clears `land_face_played` off the battlefield (a bounced Witch-Blessed Meadow stayed the land face in hand), and `transform` turns such a permanent onto its front. Goldens regenerated and reviewed: inga-and-esika/triggered-0 (the plain Serra Angel cast now spends three granted creature mana, so cast_probe fires before creature_mana_probe), march-of-the-world-ooze/triggered-0 (the Elephant is 6/6 base plus Intangible Virtue = 7/7), new esika-god-of-the-tree-the-prismatic-bridge/triggered-0 and tocasia-dig-site-mentor/activated-0.
- 2026-10-03: s7c Adrestia/Guardian Sunmare: both were one-word readability gaps, not new subsystems. `_card_description_is_readable` lacked the "nonland " prefix and the bare "permanent" word that `_quality_matcher`/`_named_type_matcher` already read, so "a nonland permanent card with mana value 3 or less" noticed instead of running; and `self_normalized_effect_text` turns a sentence-initial self-name into lowercase "this permanent", which `_SENTENCE_BOUNDARY_RE` (capital-only) did not see as a sentence start, so "draw a card. Adrestia becomes an Assassin ..." stayed one unreadable sentence (the layer reader and crew-history condition already existed). No second printing names a subtype in "crewed it this turn"; the other Modern/Pioneer/Standard crew cards say "a creature that crewed it". The sweep driver stages the named-subtype crewer for it.

- 2026-10-05 "Add N mana in any combination of <colours>" is one family on both sides: abilities._ANY_COLOR_COMBINATION reads a printed palette ("{U} and/or {R}": options = the palette, any_color False, resolve refuses an outside colour) and an X the cost announces (ANNOUNCED_X <- x_value: storage lands, Crucible, Wizard's Rockets were stack abilities); effects._ADD_ANY_COMBINATION_MANA_RE reads palette + printed/where-X/"that much" for spells, loyalty (Xenagos), triggers (Radha, with "don't lose this mana") and Plasm Capture's delayed trigger. Auto-tap carries a mixed source as one cost.MixedProduction alternative (flow capacity N, one edge per palette colour, split read back off the flow) and branches over it like an exotic source - left non-exotic, the flow tapped a Forest beside an Axebane Guardian for mana the Guardian would have floated. Outside the family, not fixed: Omnath, Locus of All (reveal-if-coloured-symbols trigger + the unspent-mana-becomes-a-colour replacement it shares with Kruphix/Ozai), Xenagos's -6.

CR 616.1 order asked as CHOOSE_A_MODE when two distinct ones apply. Any-cost mana abilities mid-payment: CostPayment.activate_mana_ability swaps the working pool into the player's pool around a real begin_activating/activate_ability (inner payment pays from it), restores, re-registers open_payment_pools, holds triggers (Game._holding_mana_triggers) so they stack after the spell; rewind reverses inner payments + Game.activations/exhausted. Split manifest placed a new module-level class in cost "components" (eager cycle with payment): keep payment helpers as payment-rule-named defs, no new classes in cost.py. PAY_MANA answers name them via Answer.mana_abilities (NamedManaAbility) - schema 113. Tezzeret's +2 needed "where X is" carried to the next sentence (effects._carry_where_x_forward, fallback only). Leyline Tyrant was tried as a proof card and dropped (its "pay any amount of {R}. When you do" death trigger is an unrelated not-working mechanic); Omnath, Locus of Mana + Upwelling prove the "don't lose" family instead.
