## NW-01 T3 (damage part 1: group-sourced, multi-target, filtered, greatest-power edict)

- 2026-10-06 NW-01 T3: which target played which role in a multi-requirement spell cannot be read off the stored target order (CR 601.2c fills slots first-fit in announcement order, "any number of" slots last) - `TargetRef.slot` now records the requirement index at announcement (spells; schema 114), an ability's targets (no slot recorded) fall back to `TargetingProfile.assign_slots`' replay, which can misassign a source whose controller changed in response. Not fixed for triggered/activated abilities: their announcement sites (`_activation`, `_triggers_decisions`) do not stamp slots.
- 2026-10-06 NW-01 T3: first-fit dead-ended on Graceful Takedown (the enchanted creature took the "up to one other" slot ahead of its own); `TargetingProfile._fill_slots` now falls back to a depth-first `_search_slots`. Existing first-fit assignments are unchanged (the fallback runs only when first-fit raised).
- 2026-10-06 NW-01 T3: proof-printing substitutions - no second printing of Fear, Fire, Foes!'s "each other creature with the same controller" wording is legal in Modern/Pioneer/Standard (card_search over the oracle bulk: only Fear, Fire, Foes!), so its group half is proved on Radiating Lightning / Chandra's Fury ("that player controls"); no "N damage to any target, M damage to another target" printing besides Cone of Flame exists, so the distinct-target reader is proved on Serpentine Spike ("target creature ... another target creature ... a third target creature"); the greatest-power edict is proved on Professor Onyx -3 and Consumed by Greed (Gix's Command's modal wrapper still notices on its other modes, Extract a Confession reads only its first branch there).
- 2026-10-06 NW-01 T3 (review fix): Hail Storm's "N damage to each attacking creature and M damage to you and each creature you control" and Tandem Takedown/Terrific Team-Up/Coordinated Clobbering's pump-or-tap-then-"They each deal damage equal to their power" shapes are now read in damage.py (`_parse_damage_group_and_you_and_group_text`, `_parse_damage_sources_act_then_deal_power_text`, sharing `_group_sources_deal_power_effect`). Announced ability targets (activation, asked and scripted trigger) now carry `TargetRef.slot` via `Game.stamp_target_slots`, only when the profile has 2+ requirements.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T4: a damage spell and its back-referring clauses are one reader, `damage_backrefs.damage_then_followup_spell_effect` (SPELL_TEXT s18 rung in merges.py), not a `_merge_*` pair per card: the head's target quality and controller must be captured BEFORE the damage (a creature the damage kills still has its controller and its infect/counter state, CR 608.2h) and the total dealt carried to "you gain life equal to the damage dealt this way" (`life.gain_life_equal_to_damage_dealt`; reuse it read-only for Chant of Vitu-Ghazi). It needs at least one follow-up or a tail, so a plain "deals N damage" spell stays with its own reader.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T4 proof-printing substitutions: the radiance damage SPELL template has only Cleansing Beam and Brightflame in Modern/Pioneer/Standard (Wojek Embermage is an ability; Wojek Siren and Incite Hysteria are radiance over a pump, not damage, owned by the pump readers), so the group semantics are proven on both listed printings plus the unchanged ability reader. "Instead" is proven on Electrostatic Bolt and Hexgold Slash (Lithomantic Barrage is dropped: its "This spell can't be countered." line is a separate notice). The controller rider is proven on Rending Flame; The Searing Barrage, Incite Hysteria, Wojek Siren and Lust for War printings were removed from the pool (unused by any scenario; their adamant, pump-grant and aura-trigger clauses are other mechanics), so no legal card is left silent or on a notice.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T4 dead end: Venerated Rotpriest is a bad toxic proof creature - its own trigger goes on the stack above a spell that targets it, so `cast_and_resolve` resolves the trigger instead (Myr Convert is used).
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T8: spell-created turn-long damage effects (prevent by creatures + life, double, can't be prevented, redirect) ride `Game.created_replacements` through `created_replacement_spec` -> `machines/damage_modification.py` specs, reaching spells via tokens.py's existing created-replacement rungs (no new rung in damage.py, owned by T3 this wave). Shared-helper change (additive): `tokens._created_replacement_record` now calls a spec's `bind(game, stack_object)` so a redirect binds its targets and "a source of your choice" (CR 608.2b, 609.7a) as it resolves.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T8: tried a dispatch.py guard refusing "deals X damage" with an unread amount (so effect_for substitutes X); dead end - it turned Consuming Corruption, Tendrils of Corruption, Harsh Sustenance, Flameblast Dragon and Electro (all "X ... where X is"/paid-X shapes still dispatched to `deal_damage(None)`) from handled to silent/notice. Instead `Game.deal_damage` reads a None amount as the spell's announced X only for a spell with {X} in its mana cost (CR 107.3a); those five are unchanged (their None amount is a separate open gap).
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T8: additive regex widenings in other tasks' files: text.py `_CONDITIONAL_CANT_BE_COUNTERED_LINE_RE` and casting_restrictions `_CONDITIONAL_CANT_BE_COUNTERED_RE` accept "this spell can't be countered and the damage can't be prevented" (Banefire, Demonfire). Proof printings: Martial Coup / Red Sun's Twilight / Black Sun's Twilight rejected (other families unread; Black Sun's Twilight is "handled" but its X>=5 return never happens), Finale of Eternity used for the X condition, Demonfire for the line, Gratuitous Violence for the widened static doubling, Carom + Razia for redirection, Claim // Fame + Start // Finish for Aftermath.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T8: `Game.cast(x_value=, targets=)` on a non-modal spell now sets `casting.x_value` before `choose_targets` (CR 601.2b before 601.2c); it used to check X-reading target restrictions ("toughness X or less", Finale of Eternity) against X of 0 and refuse. One additive line in models/game/_casting.py.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T8: rejected proof candidates Martial Coup, Red Sun's Twilight and Reverse Damage (all Modern-legal, each carrying a clause from another family this task does not own) were dropped from data/printings rather than left in the pool silent or failing closed; the life-gain rider is proven on Chant of Vitu-Ghazi's own scenarios, the X>=5 condition on Finale of Eternity.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T5: `deals N damage to each creature target player controls` was read by the generic `_parse_targeted_clause` as damage TO the player (Aggravate, Ashling's Command, Savage Alliance modes); `damage._parse_damage_each_group_named_player_controls_clause` now runs ahead of it (MIXED_CLAUSE s1 edge). Its "that player" form is spell-only: on Siege Dragon's attack trigger the event player is the attacker, so reading it there damaged the dragon's own side.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T5: multi-clause spells hand every clause the spell's whole target list; `continuous._own_phrase_targets` scopes "Tap target land. That land doesn't untap ..." to the target announced for its own phrase (`TargetRef.slot` matched by requirement phrase), so Chandra's Revolution no longer taps and holds the creature it damaged. Other per-clause readers still see every target.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T5: shared-helper changes (additive): `text._parse_referent_object_clause` gains the "loses <keyword> until end of turn" rider (reader in continuous.py); `damage._parse_damage_group_phrase` reads "X and each Y" unions; `layers.model` gains `LoseKeyword` (new union kind, no new field, schema stays 115).
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T5 proof substitutions: Huatli, Warrior Poet (-X "Creatures dealt damage this way can't block") dropped from the pool - the behavioural sweep drives every "−X" loyalty cost at X=0 (`_x_for` reads only "{x}"/" x "), so its divided damage is refused; Ballista Wielder proves the same rider reader. No second printing doubles then fights; Chelonian Tackle proves the whole-text fight reader through its pump branch.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-T5 review fix: supersedes the "Other per-clause readers still see every target" and Huatli lines above. `text._parse_per_target_sentence_groups` (spell, SPELL_TEXT s23) and `_ability` (ABILITY_TEXT s40) run ahead of the generic mixed readers and scope each sentence group to its own `TargetRef.slot`s by temporarily narrowing `targets`/`division` (restored in `finally`). Huatli, Warrior Poet is back as the -X "dealt damage this way can't block" proof. Fixed by a whole-text MIXED_CLAUSES rung in tokens.py (`_parse_token_with_keywords_and_quoted_ability_sentences`) instead of editing the shared splitter in text.py.

Bails when the first sentence has an "up to"/variable requirement.

One additive line.

- 2026-10-06 NW01-F3: one Add-instruction reader, `mana.read_add_mana_instruction` (literal, listed choice, N of any one color, any combination), feeds `merges._merge_add_any_combination_mana_with_restriction_clauses`, so a CR 106.6 "Spend this mana only to ..." restriction now rides any of them on a spell or resolving ability (CR 608.2d choice as it applies). Closes Narset of the Ancient Way's +1, which T6 had found silent and dropped as a proof card; Tournament Grounds and Ancient Ziggurat prove the mana-ability side still reads.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-F4: counts of counters on players are one reader (`amounts.player_counter_count`, kind = any counter word or {E}, whose = you / your opponents / target player / they / among players), shared by effect amounts and the layer engine's static count; "they have"/"that player has" only resolve with `anaphoric=True` (the caller names the player). Closes Phyrexian Swarmlord (dropped by T6 as a proof card), Mycosynth Fiend, Whispering Specter and Vraska, Betrayal's Sting -9.
- 2026-10-06 NW01-F5: Support N (CR 701.41a), built in T1, was still reported as an unexecuted keyword by `support.executed_keywords`; it is now credited when the card's clause matches `counters._SUPPORT_RE`. Shoulder to Shoulder and Expedition Raptor prove the spell and enters-trigger forms. `spell_grants._affinity_is_counted` (granted affinity) went back to the counted tables only, dropping T3's `canonical_subtype` fallback there; `Game._affinity_count` keeps that fallback for printed "Affinity for <subtype>" (Allies at Last).
- 2026-10-06 NW-01 integration: F2 added proof printings without goldens and the sweep's material finder (`_find_material`/`_quick_fit`) could not stage a card for F2's new qualifiers, so Murasa Sproutling, Edgewall Inn and Groffskithur never reached the stack; the finder now reads `required_name`, `must_have_adventure` (front face of an adventurer) and the cycling/kicker keyword families. Groffskithur then exposed a real bug: `self_normalized_effect_text` rewrote "card named Groffskithur" to "card named this permanent", so the CR 608.2b recheck fizzled the ability; a name after "named " is now never a CR 201.5 self-reference (`abilities._NAMED_LOOKBEHIND`, Seven Dwarves as off-pool proof).
- 2026-10-06 NW-01 integration: test_the_full_pool_classifies_within_budget measured quiet (load < 4) inside pytest: main 0e0c74db 10.68-11.30 units, integ 11.19-11.47 (budget 13.0). The integ tree was ~0.47s slower cold on main's own pool; exact literal gates ahead of the costliest new readers (per-target sentence groups, expression damage, damage back-references, this-way riders, quoted-ability/Role tokens, fights, pump-for-each) brought it to about +0.2s; the rest is ~40 new readers' honest cost plus 158 new printings (~0.3s). Under a loaded machine (load 10-13) the test reached 13.0 and failed - it is load-sensitive, not a NW-01 regression.
- 2026-10-06 NW-02 integration: test_the_full_pool_classifies_within_budget failed in the integ full suite (13.2 units under load) and measured quiet (load < 2, inside pytest) main c76a5da2 11.4-11.5 units, integ 11.5-12.4 (budget 13.0; +103 printings, per-card cost flat). Cheap-check-first in the costliest pair readers (controller-split targets parsed both whole clauses before reading the profiles; landfall add-mana and damage-then-reveal parsed before their own regex) plus literal gates on the self-exile strippers and the self attack-alone / can't lines brought cold classify 5.7s -> 5.0s CPU, 10.3-10.7 units; full-pool card_support reprs identical (4098 entries) and the dispatch fingerprint unchanged. Remaining hot spots if it creeps back: Game private-attr default factories (pydantic inspects each factory's signature per Game, ~1826 Games per pass), _synthetic_permanent CardState builds (30k), function-level imports in hot readers.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T2: Refraction Trap's / Divine Deflection's "deals that much damage to any target" target is chosen as the spell is cast (CR 601.2c: every target the spell requires), not when damage is prevented as the plan suggested (CR 603.12 reflexive) - the sentence is part of the spell's own text, not a trigger. Stored on the shield as `ReflectedDamage` (id + stamp or player).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T2: Flames of the Blood Hand's / Combust's "The damage can't be prevented." is this one damage event (`Game.deal_damage(unpreventable=True)` -> `ReplaceableEvent.unpreventable`), not `_record_damage_cant_be_prevented`'s turn-wide Skullcrack rule, which would also unshield every later damage that turn.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T2: additive edits outside the T2 list, each one line: `casting/casting.py` (`_condition_refusal` falls back to `options.printed_condition_refusal`; `_with_bestowed_target` appends `with_keyword_spell_ability_target`) and `models/game/_stack_priority.py` (awaken hold-apart around the spell's effects, then `awaken_as_the_spell_resolves`; `legal_target` adds the awaken requirement for an awakened spell). Awaken needed both: CR 601.2c's cost-dependent target and CR 608.2c's resolve-order.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T2: no second legal printing exists for three templates (oracle bulk searched): "one or two target creatures would deal" (Soul Parry), "<ability word> - If <cond>, instead prevent ..." (Winds of Qal Sisma) and "that player gains no life instead" (Flames of the Blood Hand). Their readers are proven on the listed cards; the neighbouring shapes are proven on Prahv (source of choice), Combust (unpreventable), Thwart the Enemy (opponents' creatures).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T2: Ricochet Trap's alternative cost now reads ("an opponent cast a blue spell this turn" via the condition reader); before, every non-spell-count trap condition refused the offer as unread.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T3: schema 117 = `CastRecord.controlled_modified_creature_as_cast` (Flame Discharge's "as you cast" fact, CR 601.2i/700.9); 116 is another NW-02 task's. The modified test is shared (`board.permanent_is_modified`), so the present-tense "if you control a modified creature" (Kami's Flare, T1 wave 2) is untouched.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T3: a spell's "that player" is bound by rewriting it back to the one "target player/opponent" (the "Choose <target>." opener's reading 1), only when that is the only "target" word in the text; "If target player has ..." is evaluated as the target's own "you have ..." per resolved target. Found while doing it: `_DAMAGE_TARGET_RE`'s unquoted prefix swallowed any leading "If <condition>, " and ran the damage unconditionally (Hidetsugu's Second Rite, "If this spell was kicked, it deals 3 damage to target player"); guarded in `dispatch._parse_targeted_clause` with `_LEADING_IF_CONDITION_RE` (a guard only, per the round rules).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T3: proof substitutions. "Choose up to N <group> you control. Those ..." has no second legal printing (Temporal Firestorm is the only one), so only the phase-out predicate is read (no "gain <keyword>" predicate was added, it would have had no real printing to test); "target <colour> and/or <colour> creature" likewise has only Ignite Disorder, so the phrase reader is also checked on the literal "Destroy target white and/or blue creature." profile; "If target player has exactly N life" has no second printing, so the shared life reader is proven by Near-Death Experience's pre-existing "you have exactly 1 life" tests and the that-player binding by Haunting Hymn. Mana-spent gates: Rally for the Throne, Slaying Fire, Rolling Spoil, Dryads Caress, Netherborn Phalanx. Per-kick targets: Strength of the Tajuru. Increasing Confusion ("mills twice that many cards instead") was tried as a that-player proof and dropped: its "twice that many" is a different, unread template.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T3: Ghostfire's "is colorless" strip shares `_strip_self_is_all_colors_sentence` with Awaken the Maelstrom's "is all colors" (that card's tests stay green, which is the proof for the shared reader since Ghostfire has no other printing); the card's colour comes from the printing's empty `colors`, so CR 202.2's cost-derived colour never applies.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T3: additive edit outside the T3 file list: `models/game/_stack_priority.py` `Game._resolution_profile` gained one branch for the per-kick recheck (CR 608.2b), next to the existing kicked-instead branch; no existing line changed.

## NW02-T4 (damage amount readers)

- 2026-10-06 NW02-T4: "as you cast this spell" counts are stored as the cast record's own `x_value` (Casting._lock_cast_amount, first CR 601.2 choice that wants it, i.e. choose_targets) rather than a new persisted field: the division check (CR 601.2d) and resolution read the same number, and a response that changes the board cannot move it. The reader (`count_expression`'s " as you cast this spell" suffix) falls back to a live count only when the spell has no cast record. Touched T3-owned `targeting._read_division` (X counters) and a bare-"targets" ", where X is" cut in `_read_phrase`, and `casting.py`, additively; no schema bump.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T4: the ", where X is" cut in `targeting._read_phrase` applies only when nothing alphabetic precedes it (Jaws of Stone's bare "targets"); the first version cut every tail and broke Spellstutter Sprite's "mana value X or less, where X is the number of Faeries you control" (comparison_x_definition reads that tail).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T4: `_parse_where_x_is_continuous_effect_for_spell` claimed Undercity Upheaval's "Distribute X +1/+1 counters ..., where X is ..." (parsed_layer_effects skips the sentence it cannot read, so only the vigilance half ran) while the sweep called it handled; now refuses a pump sentence naming counters, and a dedicated distribute-X reader pays the division.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T4: `count_expression` is the only reader the mass-pump path (`continuous._parse_mass_pump_clause`) uses for "where X is", so "N plus <count>" lives in `count_expression` itself (Kjeldoran War Cry), not only in `amount_expression`.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T4: proof-card substitutions: Bearded Axe / Born to Drive ("for each ... and/or Vehicle you control") are layer statics owned by `layers/parse.py`, outside this task's files and silent today; the and/or reader is proven through Focus Fire and Collision Course (modal damage mode, crewed Vehicle counts once) instead. Frantic Inventory and Soratami Seer needed no change beyond the discard-all rung. Undercity Upheaval is the non-listed "as you cast this spell" proof (Jaws/Onslaught are the listed ones).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T5: the riders after a main effect are read from the whole text by one `MIXED_CLAUSES` rung (`continuous._parse_main_effect_riders`), not as per-clause rungs: "those creatures" is the previous sentence's group, "other creatures" excludes the previous sentence's target, and the clause splitter cuts "can't attack or block and their activated abilities can't be activated" at its "and". Sentences that are not riders go back through `_parse_mixed_clauses` a contiguous run at a time, so the main effect keeps its readings.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T5: "those creatures" after a group tap is every creature of the group, tapped already or not (Sleep's and Clinging Mists' rulings; attackers are normally tapped already, so holding only the freshly tapped would make Clinging Mists do nothing). `_tap_all_group_members` returns the group; `_tap_all_group_runner` (Suppression Ray's "tapped this way") still returns only what it tapped.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T5: proof-card substitution. No second printing carries a colour-qualified pump-then-fight pair (Scryfall o: search, Hunt the Hunter alone), so the widened `_PUMP_TARGET_YOU_CONTROL_RE` is proven on Hunt the Hunter's corner cases and the unqualified template is pinned on Wild Instincts and Epic Confrontation. No second "Other creatures can't attack" printing exists either; Mark for Death proves the exclusion on the blocking side ("that player controls"), which needed the "blocks this turn if able" requirement (the "attacks" reader widened) and "Untap that creature." read with it. Exhaustion was dropped as a proof ("Creatures and lands target opponent controls don't untap ..." is a different shape); Sleep and Snow Day stand in.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T5: "other creatures can't attack this turn" is a live `qualified_creatures(excluded_id, ..., other=True)` selector, so a creature that enters later this turn is restricted too (CR 611.2c fixes the set only for characteristic or control changes). The mana-spent gate is read from `Game.cast_record(spell).mana_of_color_spent` inside the rider's own reader (modal.py/conditions/ untouched): each colour symbol needs at least one mana of that colour (CR 601.2h).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T5: adding printings to the pool makes the behavioural sweep demand goldens for every handled ability on them (Edifice of Authority's two activated abilities needed `golden:update`); proof cards with no ability beyond a spell add none, so only the cards a test needs were added (Oriss, Academic Probation, Lyev Decree, Rhino's Rampage, Exhaustion, Berg Strider, Savage Smash were tried and removed).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T6: Scryfall `o:"excess damage is dealt to that"` (Modern/Pioneer/Standard) returns only Flame Spill, Pigment Storm, Gandalf's Sanction and Ram Through, so Ram Through is the one second-printing proof for the excess-to-controller reader; `o:"amount of excess damage"` returns only the three listed token cards plus Unleash the Inferno (a "when it deals excess damage this way, destroy ..." trigger, a different template), so the token reader is proven across the three listed token kinds (1/1 Goblin, tapped Treasure, Blood) with no fourth printing. Blood (CR 111.10g) was not in `PREDEFINED`; added `BLOOD` to `models/token.py` (additive).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T6: the excess readers are whole-text SPELL_TEXT rungs in merges.py (the two sentences are one CR 120.4a/608.2c instruction; a per-clause pair parser cannot carry the damage event across the sentence cut). The excess threshold is read before `deal_damage` and the token count uses the damage actually dealt (CR 120.10), as the Violent Echoes and converge readers do.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T1: proof-card substitutions (Scryfall Modern/Pioneer/Standard `o:` searches): "each nontoken creature", "each creature dealt damage this turn", the conditional-kicked split second, Kami's Flare's trailing modified condition, Flames of the Raze-Boar's "each other creature that player controls", the shuffle-hand wheel and the per-opponent drawn-this-turn damage each have a single printing, so the readers are proven on the listed card plus the nearest shared path (Izzet Staticaster for the name group, Synchronized Spellcraft for where-X controller damage, Refuse for the spell-controller template, Psionic Blast for any-target-and-you, Squall Line/Rain of Embers/Hammerfist Giant/Rockcaster Platoon/Cloudthresher/Jaya Ballard for the mass-damage union).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T1: "can't be regenerated this turn" is a layer-6 granted line (`CANT_BE_REGENERATED_LINE`, until end of turn, bound to the incarnation) read by `replacement._regeneration_effects` off the computed abilities, not a persisted flag, so no schema bump: CR 701.19c says the shield is not applied, so an unspent shield is still there and the effect ends with the turn on its own.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T1: a conditional split second ("If this spell was kicked, it has split second.") is read from the spell's own text in `split_second_restriction_source` through `Game.condition_holds` (a spell on the stack has its cast record), and the sentence is stripped from the resolution text in `spell_resolution_text`; the printed-keyword path (`has_split_second`) is untouched.
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T1: two new whole-text SPELL_TEXT.s18 rungs (`_parse_damage_target_also_damages_controller_clause` widened to `that permanent's controller`, `_parse_damage_target_and_each_other_group_text`) run `after=` merges' `_parse_damage_then_followup_spell`, which already read Carnival // Carnage and Cleansing Beam, so no existing text changes reader (the dispatch fingerprint only gained entries).
- 2026-10-06 NW02-T1: Murasa Sproutling's golden (re-picked by Aggressive Sabotage, added by NW02-T3) and the missing goldens for Kor Firewalker and Netherborn Phalanx fail on the wave-1 integration branch before this task's changes; `golden:update` for them is left to the integrator (this task only added the goldens of the five cards it newly handled).
